5      MODULE user_command_0001 INPUT.
6        CLEAR : pnl,                                                          "Variable for user selected program/report on main screen
7                mode.                                                         "Variable for user selected mode i.e. Create,Change,etc
8
9        CASE sy-ucomm.
10         WHEN 'PROJLIST'.                                                  "Project list Mapping Table Maintainance
11           pnl = 'PROJLIST'.
12           PERFORM projlist_maintain.
13         WHEN 'GLMAPPING'.                                                  "GL Mapping Table Maintainance
14           pnl = 'GLMAP'.
15           PERFORM gl_maintain.
16         WHEN 'QUANTINFO'.                                                  "Qunatitative Information
17           pnl = 'QUANTINFO'.
18           CLEAR g_custom_container.                                         "Clear screen/custom-container
19           CALL SCREEN '0101'.
20         WHEN 'PRD_ASST'.                                                   "Producing project/Project Input
21           pnl = 'PRD_ASST'.
22           CLEAR g_custom_container.
23           CALL SCREEN '0201'.
24         WHEN 'OTH_PROJ'.                                                  "Other Project Input
25           pnl = 'OTH_PROJ'.
26           CLEAR g_custom_container.
27           CALL SCREEN '0301'.
28         WHEN 'PRD_PROJ'.                                                  "Profitability Statement - Producing Projects
29           pnl = 'PRD_PROJ'.
30           CLEAR: g_custom_container.
31           CALL SCREEN '0401'.
32         WHEN 'PER_BAR1'.                                                   "Producing Projects & Per Barrel Analysis Report
33           pnl = 'PER_BAR'.
34           CLEAR: g_custom_container.
35           CALL SCREEN '0401'.
36         WHEN 'NON_PRD'.                                                   "Profitability Statement - Non Producing Projects
37           pnl = 'NON_PRD'.
38           CLEAR: g_custom_container.
39           CALL SCREEN '0401'.
40         WHEN 'VAR_PRD'.                                                   "Variance Analysis for Producing Projects
41           pnl = 'VAR_PRD'.
42           CLEAR: g_custom_container.
43           CALL SCREEN '0401'.
44         WHEN 'VAR_NON'.                                                   "Variance Analysis for Non-Produing Projects
45           pnl = 'VAR_NON'.
46           CLEAR: g_custom_container.
47           CALL SCREEN '0401'.
48         WHEN 'PRO_AN'.
49           pnl = 'PRO_ANA'.                                               "Profitability Analysis Report
50           CLEAR: g_custom_container.
51           CALL SCREEN '0501'.
52         WHEN 'PEND_INP'.                                                  "Pending Inputs Reports
53           pnl = 'PEND_INP'.
54           CLEAR: g_custom_container.
55           CALL SCREEN '0601'.
56         WHEN 'PRD_QTY'.
57           pnl = 'PRD_QTY'.                                               " Production and sales quantity analysis
58           CLEAR: g_custom_container.
59           CALL SCREEN '0401'.
60         WHEN 'REV'.
61           pnl = 'REV'.                                               " Revenu and cost
62           CLEAR: g_custom_container.
63           CALL SCREEN '0401'.
64         WHEN 'BAR_REP'.
65           pnl = 'BAR_REP'.                                               " Per barrel analysis
66           CLEAR: g_custom_container.
67           CALL SCREEN '0401'.
68         WHEN 'AGE_REP'.
69           pnl = 'AGE_REP'.                                               " agenda report
70           CLEAR: g_custom_container.
71           CALL SCREEN '0401'.
72         WHEN 'GUIDE'.
73           PERFORM process_guide.                                         "Process Guide
74         WHEN 'BACK'.
75           LEAVE PROGRAM.
76         WHEN 'EXIT'.
77           LEAVE PROGRAM.
78         WHEN 'CANC'.
79           LEAVE PROGRAM.
80         WHEN ' '.
81       ENDCASE.
82
83     ENDMODULE."USER_COMMAND_0001

86     MODULE user_command_0101 INPUT.
87       CLEAR mode .
88
89       PERFORM initail_chk.                                                "check mandatory input fields
90
91       PERFORM auth_check.                                                 "Authorization check for Create/Change/Display/Approve/Freeze
92
93       IF p_company IS NOT INITIAL
94            AND p_proj IS NOT INITIAL
95            AND p_year IS NOT INITIAL
96            AND s_period_low IS NOT INITIAL.
97
98         SELECT SINGLE * FROM zpnl_qi_oil
99           INTO @DATA(chk_cre_quant)
100          WHERE company = @p_company
101            AND bukrs   = @p_comp
102            AND pro_nam = @p_proj
103            AND fisyear = @p_year
104            AND zperiod = @s_period_low.
105      ENDIF.
106
107      CLEAR stat.
108      IF sy-ucomm NE 'CREATE' AND chk_cre_quant IS NOT INITIAL.             ""Get data to display Freezed/Approved status on screen
109        CLEAR stat.
110        IF chk_cre_quant-freezed_by IS NOT INITIAL.
111          stat = 'FREEZED'.
112        ELSEIF chk_cre_quant-approved_by IS NOT INITIAL AND chk_cre_quant-approved_by_rp IS  INITIAL.
113          stat = 'APPROVED'.
114        ELSEIF chk_cre_quant-approved_by_rp IS NOT INITIAL AND  chk_cre_quant-approved_by IS NOT INITIAL.
115          stat = 'APPROVEFI'.
116        ENDIF.
117      ENDIF.
118
119      IF s_period_low NE 'Q2' AND s_period_low NE 'Q3' AND s_period_low NE 'Q4'.
120        IF sy-ucomm = 'CREATE' AND chk_cre_quant IS NOT INITIAL.           ""Check the status of entered inputs
121          CLEAR : sy-ucomm,chk_cre_quant.
122          MESSAGE 'Data already created for given inputs' TYPE 'E'.
123
124        ELSEIF ( sy-ucomm = 'CHANGE' OR sy-ucomm = 'APPROVE' OR sy-ucomm = 'FREEZE' ) AND ( chk_cre_quant IS NOT INITIAL ).
125          IF chk_cre_quant-freezed_by IS NOT INITIAL.
126            CLEAR : sy-ucomm,chk_cre_quant.
127            MESSAGE 'Data is already freezed. You can only display now' TYPE 'E'.
128          ENDIF.
129
130        ELSEIF ( sy-ucomm = 'CHANGE' OR sy-ucomm = 'DISPLAY' OR sy-ucomm = 'APPROVE' OR sy-ucomm = 'FREEZE' )
131           AND ( chk_cre_quant IS INITIAL ).
132          CLEAR : sy-ucomm,chk_cre_quant.
133          MESSAGE 'Data not yet created for given inputs' TYPE 'E'.
134        ENDIF.
135
136        IF stat = 'APPROVED' AND sy-ucomm = 'APPROVE' AND sy-ucomm = 'APPROVEFI'.
137          CLEAR : sy-ucomm.
138          MESSAGE 'Data already approved' TYPE 'E'.
139        ELSEIF stat = 'FREEZED' AND sy-ucomm = 'FREEZE'.
140          CLEAR : sy-ucomm.
141          MESSAGE 'Data already freezed' TYPE 'E'.
142        ENDIF.
143
144      ELSEIF s_period_low EQ 'Q2' OR s_period_low EQ 'Q3' OR s_period_low EQ 'Q4'.
145        IF sy-ucomm = 'CREATE' OR sy-ucomm = 'CHANGE' OR sy-ucomm = 'APPROVE' OR sy-ucomm = 'FREEZE'.
146          CLEAR : sy-ucomm,chk_cre_quant.
147          MESSAGE 'Data for Q2, Q3 and Q4 is auto calculated. Data can be displayed directly' TYPE 'E' DISPLAY LIKE 'S'.
148        ELSEIF sy-ucomm = 'DISPLAY'.
149          IF s_period_low EQ 'Q2'.
150            SELECT * FROM zpnl_qi_oil INTO TABLE @DATA(lt_pre_quant) WHERE company = @p_company
151                 AND bukrs   = @p_comp
152                 AND pro_nam = @p_proj
153                 AND fisyear = @p_year
154                 AND zperiod IN ('Q1','H1').
155            DESCRIBE TABLE lt_pre_quant LINES DATA(lv_pre_quant).
156            IF lv_pre_quant NE 2.
157              MESSAGE 'Q1 or H1, or entries for both not available' TYPE 'E'.
158            ENDIF.
159            REFRESH lt_pre_quant[].
160            CLEAR lv_pre_quant.
161
162          ELSEIF s_period_low EQ 'Q3'.
163            REFRESH lt_pre_quant[].
164            CLEAR lv_pre_quant.
165            SELECT * FROM zpnl_qi_oil INTO TABLE lt_pre_quant WHERE company = p_company
166                 AND bukrs   = p_comp
167                 AND pro_nam = p_proj
168                 AND fisyear = p_year
169                 AND zperiod IN ('9M','H1').
170            DESCRIBE TABLE lt_pre_quant LINES lv_pre_quant.
171            IF lv_pre_quant NE 2.
172              MESSAGE '9M or H1, or entries for both not available' TYPE 'E'.
173            ENDIF.
174
175          ELSEIF s_period_low EQ 'Q4'.
176            REFRESH lt_pre_quant[].
177            SELECT * FROM zpnl_qi_oil INTO TABLE lt_pre_quant WHERE company = p_company
178                 AND bukrs   = p_comp
179                 AND pro_nam = p_proj
180                 AND fisyear = p_year
181                 AND zperiod IN ('9M','ANNUAL').
182            DESCRIBE TABLE lt_pre_quant LINES lv_pre_quant.
183            IF lv_pre_quant NE 2.
184              MESSAGE '9M or ANNUAL, or entries for both not available' TYPE 'E'.
185            ENDIF.
186          ENDIF.
187        ENDIF.
188      ENDIF.
189
190
191      CLEAR chk_cre_quant.
192
193      CASE sy-ucomm.
194        WHEN 'CREATE'.
195          mode = 'CRE'.
196          bbl_mmbbl    = '0.000001'.
197          bbl_mmt      = '0.000001'.
198          mmscm_mmboe  = '0.00629'.
199          mmscm_bcm    = '0.001'.
200          PERFORM create_quant.                         "Prepare screen and its input parameters for Quantitative Analysis
201          CALL SCREEN 0111.
202
203        WHEN 'CHANGE'.
204          mode = 'CHA'.
205          PERFORM create_quant.
206          PERFORM fetch_quant.                          "Fetch saved/created data from tables
207          PERFORM cal_quant.
208          CALL SCREEN 0111.
209
210        WHEN 'DISPLAY'.
211          mode = 'DIS'.
212          PERFORM create_quant.
213          PERFORM fetch_quant.
214          PERFORM cal_quant.                            "Calculate the Output/Read only fields
215          CALL SCREEN 0111.
216
217        WHEN 'APPROVE'.
218          mode = 'APP'.
219          PERFORM create_quant.
220          PERFORM fetch_quant.
221          PERFORM cal_quant.
222          CALL SCREEN 0111.
223    *ADDED BY APARNA 27.06.2024  " ADDITIONAL REQURIMENT BY CORP FOR APPROVLA
224        WHEN 'APPROVEFI'.
225          mode = 'APF'.
226          PERFORM create_quant.
227          PERFORM fetch_quant.
228          PERFORM cal_quant.
229          CALL SCREEN 0111.
230
231    * ENDED BY APARNA 27.06.2024
232        WHEN 'FREEZE'.
233          mode = 'FRE'.
234          PERFORM create_quant.
235          PERFORM fetch_quant.
236          PERFORM cal_quant.
237          CALL SCREEN 0111.
238
239        WHEN 'BACK'.
240          REFRESH it_quant[].
241          CLEAR it_quant.
242          PERFORM clear_on_exit.                       "Clear Variables in exit
243          LEAVE TO SCREEN 0001.
244
245        WHEN 'EXIT'.
246          REFRESH it_quant[].
247          CLEAR it_quant.
248          PERFORM clear_on_exit.
249          LEAVE PROGRAM.
250
251        WHEN 'CANC'.
252          REFRESH it_quant[].
253          CLEAR it_quant.
254          PERFORM clear_on_exit.
255          LEAVE TO SCREEN 0001.
256
257        WHEN ' '.
258
259      ENDCASE.
260
261    ENDMODULE."USER_COMMAND_0101

264    MODULE pov_company INPUT.               "get dropdown list for Company field
265      REFRESH it_company[].
266      REFRESH it_company2[].
267      REFRESH it_return[].
268
269      SELECT company FROM zfi_pnl_proj INTO CORRESPONDING FIELDS OF TABLE it_company2.
270      IF it_company2 IS NOT INITIAL.
271        SELECT name1 rcomp
272          FROM t880 INTO TABLE it_company
273          FOR ALL ENTRIES IN it_company2 WHERE rcomp = it_company2-company.
274      ENDIF.
275
276      SORT it_company BY company.
277      DELETE ADJACENT DUPLICATES FROM it_company COMPARING company.
278
279      CALL FUNCTION 'F4IF_INT_TABLE_VALUE_REQUEST'
280        EXPORTING
281          retfield        = 'RCOMP_D'
282          dynpprog        = sy-cprog
283          dynpnr          = sy-dynnr
284          dynprofield     = 'P_COMPANY'
285          value_org       = 'S'
286        TABLES
287          value_tab       = it_company
288          return_tab      = it_return
289        EXCEPTIONS
290          parameter_error = 1
291          no_values_found = 2
292          OTHERS          = 3.
293      IF sy-subrc <> 0.
294    * Implement suitable error handling here
295      ENDIF.
296
297    ENDMODULE.

300    MODULE pov_comp INPUT.       "Autofill company code based on Company and Get the dropdown list
301
302      REFRESH it_comp[].
303      REFRESH it_return[].
304      CLEAR p_comp.
305
306      REFRESH lt_dynpfields[].
307      ls_dynpfields-fieldname = 'P_COMPANY'.
308      APPEND ls_dynpfields TO lt_dynpfields.
309      CLEAR ls_dynpfields.
310
311      CALL FUNCTION 'DYNP_VALUES_READ'
312        EXPORTING
313          dyname               = sy-repid
314          dynumb               = sy-dynnr
315        TABLES
316          dynpfields           = lt_dynpfields
317        EXCEPTIONS
318          invalid_abapworkarea = 1
319          invalid_dynprofield  = 2
320          invalid_dynproname   = 3
321          invalid_dynpronummer = 4
322          invalid_request      = 5
323          no_fielddescription  = 6
324          invalid_parameter    = 7
325          undefind_error       = 8
326          double_conversion    = 9
327          stepl_not_found      = 10
328          OTHERS               = 11.
329
330      READ TABLE lt_dynpfields INTO ls_dynpfields WITH KEY fieldname = 'P_COMPANY'.
331      IF ls_dynpfields IS NOT INITIAL.
332        p_company = ls_dynpfields-fieldvalue.
333      ENDIF.
334
335      SELECT txtcc bukrs FROM zfi_pnl_proj INTO TABLE it_comp WHERE company = p_company.
336      SORT it_comp BY bukrs.
337      DELETE ADJACENT DUPLICATES FROM it_comp COMPARING bukrs.
338
339      READ TABLE it_comp INTO DATA(wa_comp2) INDEX 1.
340      IF wa_comp2 IS NOT INITIAL.
341        p_comp = wa_comp2-bukrs.
342      ENDIF.
343
344    ENDMODULE.

346    MODULE pov_proj INPUT.         "Get dropdown list for Projet field base on Company and Company code
347
348      REFRESH it_proj[].
349      CLEAR it_proj.
350      REFRESH it_return[].
351      CLEAR p_proj.
352
353      REFRESH lt_dynpfields[].
354      ls_dynpfields-fieldname = 'P_COMP'.
355      APPEND ls_dynpfields TO lt_dynpfields.
356      CLEAR ls_dynpfields.
357
358      CALL FUNCTION 'DYNP_VALUES_READ'
359        EXPORTING
360          dyname               = sy-repid
361          dynumb               = sy-dynnr
362        TABLES
363          dynpfields           = lt_dynpfields
364        EXCEPTIONS
365          invalid_abapworkarea = 1
366          invalid_dynprofield  = 2
367          invalid_dynproname   = 3
368          invalid_dynpronummer = 4
369          invalid_request      = 5
370          no_fielddescription  = 6
371          invalid_parameter    = 7
372          undefind_error       = 8
373          double_conversion    = 9
374          stepl_not_found      = 10
375          OTHERS               = 11.
376
377      READ TABLE lt_dynpfields INTO ls_dynpfields WITH KEY fieldname = 'P_COMP'.
378      IF ls_dynpfields IS NOT INITIAL.
379        p_comp = ls_dynpfields-fieldvalue.
380      ENDIF.
381
382      IF p_comp NE 'N/A' AND p_company IS NOT INITIAL.
383        IF pnl = 'QUANTINFO'.
384          SELECT txtjv jvnam FROM zfi_pnl_proj INTO TABLE it_proj
385                             WHERE bukrs  = p_comp
386                               AND prod   = 'YES'.
387
388        ELSEIF pnl = 'PRD_ASST'.
389          SELECT txtjv jvnam FROM zfi_pnl_proj INTO TABLE it_proj
390                             WHERE bukrs  = p_comp
391                               AND prod   = 'YES'
392                               AND manual = 'YES'.
393
394        ELSEIF pnl = 'OTH_PROJ'.
395          SELECT txtjv jvnam FROM zfi_pnl_proj INTO TABLE it_proj
396                             WHERE bukrs  = p_comp
397                               AND prod   = 'NO'
398                               AND manual = 'YES'.
399
400        ELSEIF pnl = 'PRD_PROJ'.
401          SELECT txtjv jvnam FROM zfi_pnl_proj INTO TABLE it_proj
402                             WHERE bukrs   = p_comp
403                                AND prod   = 'YES'
404                                AND manual = 'NO'.
405
406        ELSEIF pnl = 'NON_PRD'.
407          SELECT txtjv jvnam FROM zfi_pnl_proj INTO TABLE it_proj
408                             WHERE bukrs   = p_comp
409                                AND prod   = 'NO'
410                                AND manual = 'NO'.
411
412        ELSE.
413          SELECT txtjv jvnam FROM zfi_pnl_proj INTO TABLE it_proj
414                             WHERE bukrs  = p_comp.
415
416        ENDIF.
417
418      ELSEIF p_comp EQ 'N/A' AND p_company IS NOT INITIAL.
419
420        IF pnl = 'QUANTINFO'.
421          SELECT txtjv jvnam FROM zfi_pnl_proj INTO TABLE it_proj
422                             WHERE company = p_company
423                               AND prod    = 'YES'.
424
425        ELSEIF pnl = 'PRD_ASST'.
426          SELECT txtjv jvnam FROM zfi_pnl_proj INTO TABLE it_proj
427                             WHERE company = p_company
428                               AND prod    = 'YES'
429                               AND manual  = 'YES'.
430
431        ELSEIF pnl = 'OTH_PROJ'.
432          SELECT txtjv jvnam FROM zfi_pnl_proj INTO TABLE it_proj
433                             WHERE company = p_company
434                               AND prod    = 'NO'
435                               AND manual  = 'YES'.
436
437        ELSEIF pnl = 'PRD_PROJ'.
438          SELECT txtjv jvnam FROM zfi_pnl_proj INTO TABLE it_proj
439                              WHERE company = p_company
440                                AND prod    = 'YES'
441                                AND manual  = 'NO'.
442
443        ELSEIF pnl = 'NON_PRD'.
444          SELECT txtjv jvnam FROM zfi_pnl_proj INTO TABLE it_proj
445                              WHERE company = p_company
446                                AND prod    = 'NO'
447                                AND manual  = 'NO'.
448        ENDIF.
449
450      ENDIF.
451
452      SORT it_proj BY jvnam.
453      DELETE ADJACENT DUPLICATES FROM it_proj COMPARING jvnam.
454
455      IF it_proj IS INITIAL AND p_comp IS NOT INITIAL.
456        CLEAR sy-ucomm.
457        MESSAGE 'No Projects found' TYPE 'I' DISPLAY LIKE 'E'.
458      ENDIF.
459
460      CALL FUNCTION 'F4IF_INT_TABLE_VALUE_REQUEST'
461        EXPORTING
462          retfield        = 'VNAME'
463          dynpprog        = sy-cprog
464          dynpnr          = sy-dynnr
465          dynprofield     = 'P_PROJ'
466          value_org       = 'S'
467        TABLES
468          value_tab       = it_proj
469          return_tab      = it_return
470        EXCEPTIONS
471          parameter_error = 1
472          no_values_found = 2
473          OTHERS          = 3.
474      IF sy-subrc <> 0.
475    *   Implement suitable error handling here
476      ENDIF.
477
478    ENDMODULE.

480    MODULE pov_period INPUT.          "Get Dropdown list for Period field addec by aparna
481
482      REFRESH it_return[].
483      REFRESH it_period[].
484
485      CLEAR wa_period.
486      wa_period-period = 'Apr - Jun'.
487      wa_period-per = 'Q1'.
488      APPEND wa_period TO it_period.
489      CLEAR wa_period.
490
491      IF ( pnl = 'PRD_PROJ' ) OR ( pnl = 'NON_PRD' ) OR ( pnl = 'VAR_PRD' ) OR ( pnl = 'VAR_NON' ) OR ( pnl = 'QUANTINFO' )
492        OR ( pnl = 'PER_BAR' ) OR ( pnl = 'PRO_ANA' )  OR ( pnl = 'PRD_QTY' ) OR ( pnl = 'REV' ) OR ( pnl = 'BAR_REP' ) OR ( pnl = 'AGE_REP' ).
493        wa_period-period = 'Jul - Sept'.
494        wa_period-per = 'Q2'.
495        APPEND wa_period TO it_period.
496        CLEAR wa_period.
497      ENDIF.
498
499      wa_period-period = 'Apr - Sept'.
500      wa_period-per = 'H1'.
501      APPEND wa_period TO it_period.
502      CLEAR wa_period.
503
504      IF ( pnl = 'PRD_PROJ' ) OR ( pnl = 'NON_PRD' ) OR ( pnl = 'VAR_PRD' ) OR ( pnl = 'VAR_NON' ) OR ( pnl = 'QUANTINFO' )
505        OR ( pnl = 'PER_BAR' ) OR ( pnl = 'PRO_ANA' )  OR ( pnl = 'PRD_QTY' ) OR ( pnl = 'REV' ) OR ( pnl = 'BAR_REP' ) OR ( pnl = 'AGE_REP' ).
506        wa_period-period = 'Oct - Dec'.
507        wa_period-per = 'Q3'.
508        APPEND wa_period TO it_period.
509        CLEAR wa_period.
510      ENDIF.
511
512      wa_period-period = 'Apr - Dec'.
513      wa_period-per = '9M'.
514      APPEND wa_period TO it_period.
515      CLEAR wa_period.
516
517      IF ( pnl = 'PRD_PROJ' ) OR ( pnl = 'NON_PRD' ) OR ( pnl = 'VAR_PRD' ) OR ( pnl = 'VAR_NON' ) OR ( pnl = 'QUANTINFO' )
518         OR ( pnl = 'PER_BAR' ) OR ( pnl = 'PRO_ANA' )  OR ( pnl = 'PRD_QTY' ) OR ( pnl = 'REV' ) OR ( pnl = 'BAR_REP' ) OR ( pnl = 'AGE_REP' ).
519        wa_period-period = 'Jan - Mar'.
520        wa_period-per = 'Q4'.
521        APPEND wa_period TO it_period.
522        CLEAR wa_period.
523      ENDIF.
524
525      wa_period-period = 'Apr - Mar'.
526      wa_period-per = 'Annual'.
527      APPEND wa_period TO it_period.
528      CLEAR wa_period.
529
530    *   IF ( PNL = 'PRD_PROJ' ) OR ( PNL = 'NON_PRD' ) OR ( PNL = 'VAR_PRD' ) OR ( PNL = 'VAR_NON' ) OR ( PNL = 'QUANTINFO' ) OR ( PNL = 'PER_BAR' ).
531    *     WA_PERIOD-PERIOD = 'Jul - Sept'.
532    *     WA_PERIOD-PER = 'Q2'.
533    *     APPEND WA_PERIOD TO IT_PERIOD.
534    *     CLEAR WA_PERIOD.
535    *
536    *     WA_PERIOD-PERIOD = 'Oct - Dec'.
537    *     WA_PERIOD-PER = 'Q3'.
538    *     APPEND WA_PERIOD TO IT_PERIOD.
539    *     CLEAR WA_PERIOD.
540    *
541    *     WA_PERIOD-PERIOD = 'Jan - Mar'.
542    *     WA_PERIOD-PER = 'Q4'.
543    *     APPEND WA_PERIOD TO IT_PERIOD.
544    *     CLEAR WA_PERIOD.
545    *
546    *   ENDIF.
547
548      CALL FUNCTION 'F4IF_INT_TABLE_VALUE_REQUEST'
549        EXPORTING
550          retfield        = 'PER'
551          dynpprog        = sy-cprog
552          dynpnr          = sy-dynnr
553          dynprofield     = 'S_PERIOD_LOW'
554          value_org       = 'S'
555        TABLES
556          value_tab       = it_period
557          return_tab      = it_return
558        EXCEPTIONS
559          parameter_error = 1
560          no_values_found = 2
561          OTHERS          = 3.
562      IF sy-subrc <> 0.
563    *   Implement suitable error handling here
564      ENDIF.

566    ENDMODULE.

568    MODULE user_command_0111 INPUT.
569
570
571      CASE sy-ucomm.
572        WHEN 'BACK'.
573
574          REFRESH : it_quant[]. "Clear inputs
575          CLEAR: it_quant,bbl_mmbbl,bbl_mmt,mmscm_mmbtu,mmscm_mmboe,mmscm_bcm,ton_bbl,wa_final_qi_o,wa_final_qi_g,wa_final_conv,stat.
576
577          IF grid1 IS BOUND.         "Clear grid and conatainer
578            CALL METHOD grid1->free.
579            CLEAR grid1.
580            CALL METHOD g_custom_container->free.
581            CLEAR g_custom_container.
582            CLEAR g_container.
583          ENDIF.
584
585          LEAVE TO SCREEN 0101 .    "move to previous screen
586
587        WHEN 'EXIT'.
588
589          REFRESH : it_quant[].  "Clear inputs
590          CLEAR: it_quant,bbl_mmbbl,bbl_mmt,mmscm_mmbtu,mmscm_mmboe,mmscm_bcm,ton_bbl,wa_final_qi_o, wa_final_qi_g, wa_final_conv,stat.
591
592          IF grid1 IS BOUND.           "Clear grid and conatainer
593            CALL METHOD grid1->free.
594            CLEAR grid1.
595            CALL METHOD g_custom_container->free.
596            CLEAR g_custom_container.
597            CLEAR g_container.
598          ENDIF.
599
600          LEAVE TO SCREEN 0001.    "move to main screen
601
602        WHEN 'CANC'.
603
604          REFRESH : it_quant[].    "Clear inputs
605          CLEAR: it_quant,bbl_mmbbl,bbl_mmt,mmscm_mmbtu,mmscm_mmboe,mmscm_bcm,ton_bbl,wa_final_qi_o, wa_final_qi_g, wa_final_conv,stat.
606
607          IF grid1 IS BOUND.         "Clear grid and conatainer
608            CALL METHOD grid1->free.
609            CLEAR grid1.
610            CALL METHOD g_custom_container->free.
611            CLEAR g_custom_container.
612            CLEAR g_container.
613          ENDIF.
614
615          LEAVE TO SCREEN 0101 ."move to previous screen
616
617        WHEN 'CALC'.
618          PERFORM cal_quant.     "calculate Quant Info values
619
620        WHEN 'SAVE'.
621          PERFORM cal_quant.
622          PERFORM save_quant.   "Save Quant Info data into database tables
623
624          CALL METHOD grid1->set_ready_for_input
625            EXPORTING
626              i_ready_for_input = 0.     "After save freeze the screen input
627
628          LOOP AT SCREEN.
629            IF screen-group1 = 'DIS'.    "After save freeze the screen input
630              screen-input     = 0.
631              MODIFY SCREEN.
632            ENDIF.
633          ENDLOOP.
634
635          IF sy-ucomm EQ 'OPT1'.          "
636            CLEAR: it_quant,bbl_mmbbl,bbl_mmt,mmscm_mmbtu,mmscm_mmboe,mmscm_bcm,ton_bbl,wa_final_qi_o, wa_final_qi_g, wa_final_conv.
637            REFRESH : it_quant[].
638            CLEAR it_quant[].
639            LEAVE TO SCREEN 0101.
640          ENDIF.
641
642        WHEN 'APPROVE'.
643          PERFORM app_quant.             "Approve the Quant data
644
645        WHEN 'FREEZE'.
646          PERFORM app_quant.             "Freeze the Quant data
647
648        WHEN 'PRI'.
649          PERFORM download_xl.           "Download Quantitative Information Excel.
650
651        WHEN 'ATTACHMENT'.
652          PERFORM attachments.            "Create/Change/Display/Delete attachments
653
654        WHEN OTHERS.
655
656      ENDCASE.
657
658    ENDMODULE.
659
660    MODULE user_command_0201 INPUT.
661
662      PERFORM auth_check.         "Authorization check for Create/Change/Display/Approve/Freeze
663
664      PERFORM initail_chk.        "check mandatory input fields
665      IF p_company IS NOT INITIAL
666          AND p_proj IS NOT INITIAL
667          AND p_year IS NOT INITIAL
668          AND s_period_low IS NOT INITIAL.
669
670        SELECT SINGLE * FROM zpnl_prd_oil
671          INTO @DATA(chk_cre_oil)
672          WHERE company = @p_company
673            AND bukrs   = @p_comp
674            AND pro_nam = @p_proj
675            AND fisyear = @p_year
676            AND zperiod = @s_period_low.
677      ENDIF.
678
679      IF sy-ucomm NE 'CREATE' AND chk_cre_oil IS NOT INITIAL.
680        CLEAR stat.
681        IF chk_cre_oil-freezed_by IS NOT INITIAL.      "to set status freezed
682          stat = 'FREEZED'.
683        ELSEIF chk_cre_oil-approved_by IS NOT INITIAL AND chk_cre_oil-approved_by_rp IS  INITIAL.. "to set status approved
684          stat = 'APPROVED'.
685        ELSEIF chk_cre_oil-approved_by IS NOT INITIAL AND chk_cre_oil-approved_by_rp IS NOT INITIAL. "to set status approved
686          stat = 'APPROVEDFI'.
687        ENDIF.
688      ENDIF.
689
690      IF sy-ucomm = 'CREATE' AND chk_cre_oil IS NOT INITIAL.      "if data already created for given inputs
691        CLEAR : sy-ucomm,chk_cre_oil.
692        MESSAGE 'Data already created for given inputs' TYPE 'E'.
693      ELSEIF ( sy-ucomm = 'CHANGE' OR sy-ucomm = 'APPROVE' OR sy-ucomm = 'FREEZE' ) AND ( chk_cre_oil IS NOT INITIAL ).
694        IF chk_cre_oil-freezed_by IS NOT INITIAL AND chk_cre_oil-freezed_on IS NOT INITIAL.
695          CLEAR : sy-ucomm,chk_cre_oil.
696          MESSAGE 'Data is already freezed. You can only display now' TYPE 'E'.    "cannot change/approve if data freezed
697        ENDIF.
698      ELSEIF ( sy-ucomm = 'CHANGE' OR sy-ucomm = 'DISPLAY' OR sy-ucomm = 'APPROVE' OR sy-ucomm = 'FREEZE' )
699              AND ( chk_cre_oil IS INITIAL ).
700        CLEAR : sy-ucomm,chk_cre_oil.
701        MESSAGE 'Data not yet created for given inputs' TYPE 'E'.    "cannot perform any action if data not created
702      ENDIF.
703
704      CLEAR: mode,chk_cre_oil.
705
706      CASE sy-ucomm.
707        WHEN 'CREATE'.
708          mode = 'CRE'.
709
710          PERFORM create_prof_prd.     "Create screen layout for producing project input
711          PERFORM fcat_fill_prof_prd.  "fill field catalog for producing project input
712
713          CALL SCREEN 0211.
714
715        WHEN 'CHANGE'.
716          mode = 'CHA'.
717
718          PERFORM create_prof_prd.
719          PERFORM change_prof_prd.    "to update the changes in the existing data
720
721          CLEAR sy-ucomm.
722          CALL SCREEN 0211.
723
724        WHEN 'DISPLAY'.
725          mode = 'DIS'.
726
727          PERFORM create_prof_prd.
728          PERFORM change_prof_prd.
729
730          CLEAR sy-ucomm.
731          CALL SCREEN 0211.
732
733        WHEN 'APPROVE'.
734          mode = 'APP'.
735
736          PERFORM create_prof_prd.
737          PERFORM change_prof_prd.
738
739          CLEAR sy-ucomm.
740          CALL SCREEN 0211.
741    *******************ADDED BY APARNA ON 31 .07 .2024
742        WHEN 'APPROVEFI'.
743          mode = 'APF'.
744
745          PERFORM create_prof_prd.
746          PERFORM change_prof_prd.
747
748          CLEAR sy-ucomm.
749          CALL SCREEN 0211.
750    *************** ENDED BY APARNA ON 31.07 .2024
751        WHEN 'FREEZE'.
752          mode = 'FRE'.
753
754          PERFORM create_prof_prd.
755          PERFORM change_prof_prd.
756
757          CLEAR sy-ucomm.
758          CALL SCREEN 0211.
759
760        WHEN 'BACK'.
761
762          PERFORM clear_on_exit.
763          LEAVE TO SCREEN 0001.
764
765        WHEN 'EXIT'.
766          PERFORM clear_on_exit.
767          LEAVE PROGRAM.
768
769        WHEN 'CANC'.
770
771          PERFORM clear_on_exit.
772          LEAVE TO SCREEN 0001.
773
774        WHEN OTHERS.
775
776      ENDCASE.
777
778    ENDMODULE.

780    MODULE user_command_0211 INPUT.
781
782      CASE sy-ucomm.
783        WHEN 'BACK'.
784          REFRESH : it_prof_prd[],it_prof_prd2[].
785          CLEAR : it_prof_prd,wa_prd_oil.
786          CLEAR stat.
787          IF grid1 IS BOUND.
788            CALL METHOD grid1->free.
789            CLEAR grid1.
790            CALL METHOD g_custom_container->free.
791            CLEAR g_custom_container.
792            CLEAR g_container.
793          ENDIF.
794
795          LEAVE TO SCREEN 0201 .
796
797        WHEN 'EXIT'.
798          REFRESH : it_prof_prd[],it_prof_prd2[].
799          CLEAR : it_prof_prd,wa_prd_oil.
800          CLEAR stat.
801
802          IF grid1 IS BOUND.
803            CALL METHOD grid1->free.
804            CLEAR grid1.
805            CALL METHOD g_custom_container->free.
806            CLEAR g_custom_container.
807            CLEAR g_container.
808          ENDIF.
809
810          LEAVE TO SCREEN 0201.
811
812        WHEN 'CANC'.
813          REFRESH : it_prof_prd[],it_prof_prd2[].
814          CLEAR : it_prof_prd,wa_prd_oil.
815          CLEAR stat.
816
817          IF grid1 IS BOUND.
818            CALL METHOD grid1->free.
819            CLEAR grid1.
820            CALL METHOD g_custom_container->free.
821            CLEAR g_custom_container.
822            CLEAR g_container.
823          ENDIF.
824
825          LEAVE TO SCREEN 0201.
826
827        WHEN 'CALC'.
828          PERFORM calc_0211.
829          CALL METHOD grid1->refresh_table_display.
830
831        WHEN 'SAVE'.
832          PERFORM calc_0211.
833          PERFORM save_prof_prd.
834
835          CALL METHOD grid1->set_ready_for_input
836            EXPORTING
837              i_ready_for_input = 0.
838
839          LOOP AT SCREEN.
840            IF screen-group1 = 'DIS'.    "After save freeze the screen input
841              screen-input     = 0.
842              MODIFY SCREEN.
843            ENDIF.
844          ENDLOOP.
845
846          IF sy-ucomm EQ 'OPT1'.
847            REFRESH : it_prof_prd[].
848            CLEAR it_prof_prd[].
849            LEAVE TO SCREEN 0201.
850          ENDIF.
851
852        WHEN 'APPROVE' OR  'APPROVEFI'.
853          PERFORM app_prd_i.
854        WHEN 'FREEZE'.
855          PERFORM app_prd_i.
856        WHEN OTHERS.
857
858      ENDCASE.
859
860    ENDMODULE.

862    MODULE user_command_0301 INPUT.
863
864      PERFORM auth_check.   "Authorization check for Create/Change/Display/Approve/Freeze
865
866      PERFORM initail_chk.
867
868      IF p_company IS NOT INITIAL
869           AND p_proj IS NOT INITIAL
870           AND p_year IS NOT INITIAL
871           AND s_period_low IS NOT INITIAL.
872
873        SELECT SINGLE * FROM zpnl_oth_proj
874          INTO @DATA(chk_cre_oth)
875          WHERE company = @p_company
876            AND bukrs   = @p_comp
877            AND pro_nam = @p_proj
878            AND fisyear = @p_year
879            AND zperiod = @s_period_low.
880      ENDIF.
881
882      IF ( sy-ucomm NE 'CREATE' ) AND ( chk_cre_oth IS NOT INITIAL ). "Check for frezeed and approved
883        IF chk_cre_oth-freezed_by IS NOT INITIAL.
884          CLEAR stat.
885          stat = 'FREEZED'.
886        ELSEIF chk_cre_oth-approve_by IS NOT INITIAL  AND chk_cre_oth-approved_by_rp IS  INITIAL.
887          CLEAR stat.
888          stat = 'APPROVED'.
889
890        ELSEIF chk_cre_oth-approve_by IS NOT INITIAL AND chk_cre_oth-approved_by_rp IS NOT INITIAL.
891          CLEAR stat.
892          stat = 'APPROVEDFI'.
893        ENDIF.
894      ENDIF.
895
896      IF sy-ucomm = 'CREATE' AND chk_cre_oth IS NOT INITIAL.   "Check for already created
897        CLEAR : sy-ucomm,chk_cre_oth.
898        MESSAGE 'Data already created for given inputs' TYPE 'E'.
899      ELSEIF ( sy-ucomm = 'CHANGE' OR sy-ucomm = 'DISPLAY' ) AND ( chk_cre_oth IS INITIAL ).  "Check for data not created
900        CLEAR : sy-ucomm,chk_cre_oth.
901        MESSAGE 'Data not yet created for given inputs' TYPE 'E'.
902      ELSEIF ( sy-ucomm = 'CHANGE' OR sy-ucomm = 'APPROVE' OR sy-ucomm = 'FREEZE' ) AND ( chk_cre_oth IS NOT INITIAL ).
903        IF chk_cre_oth-freezed_by IS NOT INITIAL AND chk_cre_oth-freezed_on IS NOT INITIAL.
904          CLEAR : sy-ucomm,chk_cre_oth.
905          MESSAGE 'Data is already freezed. You can only display now' TYPE 'E'.    "cannot change/approve if data freezed
906        ENDIF.
907      ELSEIF ( sy-ucomm = 'CHANGE' OR sy-ucomm = 'DISPLAY' OR sy-ucomm = 'APPROVE' OR sy-ucomm = 'FREEZE' )
908         AND ( chk_cre_oth IS INITIAL ).
909        CLEAR : sy-ucomm,chk_cre_oth.
910        MESSAGE 'Data not yet created for given inputs' TYPE 'E'.    "cannot perform any action if data not created
911      ENDIF.
912
913      CLEAR : chk_cre_oth.
914
915      CASE sy-ucomm.
916        WHEN 'CREATE'.
917          mode = 'CRE'.
918          PERFORM create_oth_proj.   "Create layout for non producing projects
919          CLEAR sy-ucomm.
920          CALL SCREEN 0311.
921        WHEN 'CHANGE'.
922          mode = 'CHA'.
923          PERFORM create_oth_proj.
924          PERFORM change_oth_proj.    "get previously filled data
925          CLEAR sy-ucomm.
926          CALL SCREEN 0311.
927        WHEN 'DISPLAY'.
928          mode = 'DIS'.
929          PERFORM create_oth_proj.
930          PERFORM change_oth_proj.
931          CLEAR sy-ucomm.
932          CALL SCREEN 0311.
933        WHEN 'APPROVE'.
934          mode = 'APP'.
935          PERFORM create_oth_proj.
936          PERFORM change_oth_proj.
937          CLEAR sy-ucomm.
938          CALL SCREEN 0311.
939        WHEN 'FREEZE'.
940          mode = 'FRE'.
941          PERFORM create_oth_proj.
942          PERFORM change_oth_proj.
943          CLEAR sy-ucomm.
944          CALL SCREEN 0311.
945        WHEN 'BACK'.
946          PERFORM clear_on_exit.
947          LEAVE TO SCREEN 0001.
948        WHEN 'EXIT'.
949          LEAVE PROGRAM.
950        WHEN 'CANC'.
951          LEAVE TO SCREEN 0001.
952    *----------------------------------" Changes begins by Aparna on 31.07.2024 "------------------------------*
953        WHEN 'APPROVEFI'.
954          mode = 'APF'.
955          PERFORM create_oth_proj.
956          PERFORM change_oth_proj.
957          CLEAR sy-ucomm.
958          CALL SCREEN 0311.
959    *----------------------------------" Changes ended  by Aparna on 31.07.2024 "------------------------------*
960      ENDCASE.
961
962    ENDMODULE.

1047           EXPORTING

36     FORM fetch_layout_texts.
37
38       SELECT * FROM zfi_pnl_glf4 INTO TABLE it_prod_f4 WHERE prod_proj NE ' '.
39       SELECT * FROM zfi_pnl_glf4 INTO TABLE it_oth_f4 WHERE oth_proj NE ' '.
40
41       IF it_prod_f4 IS NOT INITIAL.
42         SORT it_prod_f4 BY prod_proj.
43         DELETE ADJACENT DUPLICATES FROM it_prod_f4 COMPARING prod_proj.
44         SORT it_prod_f4 BY text_no.
45       ENDIF.
46
47       IF it_oth_f4 IS NOT INITIAL.
48         SORT it_oth_f4 BY oth_proj.
49         DELETE ADJACENT DUPLICATES FROM it_oth_f4 COMPARING oth_proj.
50         SORT it_oth_f4 BY text_no.
51       ENDIF.
52
53     ENDFORM."FETCH_LAYOUT_TEXTS

130    FORM fetch_comp_code.
131
132      REFRESH it_comp[].
133      REFRESH it_return[].
134      CLEAR p_comp.
135
136      REFRESH lt_dynpfields[].
137      ls_dynpfields-fieldname = 'P_COMPANY'.
138      APPEND ls_dynpfields TO lt_dynpfields.
139      CLEAR ls_dynpfields.
140
141      CALL FUNCTION 'DYNP_VALUES_READ'
142        EXPORTING
143          dyname               = sy-repid
144          dynumb               = sy-dynnr
145        TABLES
146          dynpfields           = lt_dynpfields
147        EXCEPTIONS
148          invalid_abapworkarea = 1
149          invalid_dynprofield  = 2
150          invalid_dynproname   = 3
151          invalid_dynpronummer = 4
152          invalid_request      = 5
153          no_fielddescription  = 6
154          invalid_parameter    = 7
155          undefind_error       = 8
156          double_conversion    = 9
157          stepl_not_found      = 10
158          OTHERS               = 11.
159
160      READ TABLE lt_dynpfields INTO ls_dynpfields WITH KEY fieldname = 'P_COMPANY'.
161      IF ls_dynpfields IS NOT INITIAL.
162        p_company = ls_dynpfields-fieldvalue.
163      ENDIF.
164
165      SELECT txtcc bukrs FROM zfi_pnl_proj INTO TABLE it_comp WHERE company = p_company.
166      SORT it_comp BY bukrs.
167      DELETE ADJACENT DUPLICATES FROM it_comp COMPARING bukrs.
168
169      READ TABLE it_comp INTO DATA(wa_comp2) INDEX 1.
170      IF wa_comp2 IS NOT INITIAL.
171        p_comp = wa_comp2-bukrs.
172      ENDIF.
173
174    ENDFORM.

184        DATA(lv_year1) = lv_p_year - 1.
185        p_company = p_compy1.
186        p_comp    = p_comp1.
187        p_proj    = p_proj1-low.
188      ELSE.

190

192
193      SELECT SINGLE * FROM zpnl_qi_oil INTO wa_opstk_o                   "get Quant Oil data
194                WHERE company = p_company
195                  AND bukrs   = p_comp
196                  AND pro_nam = p_proj
197                  AND fisyear = lv_year1
198                  AND zperiod = 'ANNUAL' .
199

201            WHERE company = p_company
202              AND bukrs   = p_comp
203              AND pro_nam = p_proj
204              AND fisyear = lv_year1
205              AND zperiod = 'ANNUAL' .
206    *   ENDIF.
207

231        wa_quant-mmscm = wa_opstk_g-cst_pip.
232      ENDIF.
233      wa_quant-io_flag = ' '.
234      APPEND wa_quant TO it_quant.

454            PERFORM fill_celltab_quant USING 'RW' '1'
455                                 CHANGING lt_celltab.
456          ELSE.
457            PERFORM fill_celltab_quant USING 'RO' ' '        "Both as output field i.e Read Only
458                                 CHANGING lt_celltab.
459          ENDIF.
460
461          INSERT LINES OF lt_celltab INTO TABLE it_quant-celltab.
462          MODIFY it_quant INDEX l_index.
463
464        ENDLOOP.
465
466      ENDIF.

470    FORM fill_celltab_quant USING VALUE(p_mode) VALUE(io_flag2)              ""making field as input field or output only
471                      CHANGING pt_celltab TYPE lvc_t_styl.
472
473      DATA : ls_celltab TYPE lvc_t_styl WITH HEADER LINE.

481      ENDIF.

485      ls_celltab-style = cl_gui_alv_grid=>mc_style_disabled.
486      INSERT ls_celltab INTO TABLE pt_celltab.
487
488      ls_celltab-fieldname = 'BBL'.                                        "Cloumn 2 - Oil(MMBBL)
489      IF io_flag2 CA '1'.
490        ls_celltab-style = l_mode.

493      ENDIF.

496      ls_celltab-fieldname = 'MMSCM'.                                        "Columm 3 - Gas(MMBTU)
497      IF io_flag2 CA '2'.
498        ls_celltab-style = l_mode.
499      ELSE.
500        ls_celltab-style = cl_gui_alv_grid=>mc_style_disabled.
501      ENDIF.
502      INSERT ls_celltab INTO TABLE pt_celltab.
503
504    *COLUMN no.4 to 9 are Output only and visible only in mode Display,Approve and Freeze.
505      ls_celltab-fieldname = 'MMBBL'.
506      ls_celltab-style = cl_gui_alv_grid=>mc_style_disabled.
507      INSERT ls_celltab INTO TABLE pt_celltab.
508
509      ls_celltab-fieldname = 'MMT'.
510      ls_celltab-style = cl_gui_alv_grid=>mc_style_disabled.
511      INSERT ls_celltab INTO TABLE pt_celltab.

513      ls_celltab-fieldname = 'TON'.
514      ls_celltab-style = cl_gui_alv_grid=>mc_style_disabled.
515      INSERT ls_celltab INTO TABLE pt_celltab.

518      ls_celltab-style = cl_gui_alv_grid=>mc_style_disabled.
519      INSERT ls_celltab INTO TABLE pt_celltab.

523      INSERT ls_celltab INTO TABLE pt_celltab.

527      INSERT ls_celltab INTO TABLE pt_celltab.

532    FORM fcat_fill_quant . "Bulid field catalog for Quantitiatve Information
533
534      DATA col_hide TYPE c.
535      REFRESH lt_fcat[].
536
537      IF ( mode EQ 'CRE' ) OR ( mode EQ 'CHA' ).
538        col_hide = 'X'.                                         "Hiding cloumn 4-8 in create and change mode
539      ELSE.
540        col_hide = ' '.
541      ENDIF.
542
543      PERFORM alv_fcat_quant USING :  'SR_NO'   'No.'                      ' '        '3'     ,
544                                      'DATA'    'Quantative Inforamtion'   ' '        '60'    ,
545                                      'BBL'     'CRUDE OIL (BBL)'          ' '        '20'    ,
546                                      'MMSCM'   'GAS (MMSCM)'              ' '        '20'    ,
547                                      'MMBBL'   'CRUDE OIL (MMBBL)'        col_hide   '20'    ,
548                                      'MMT'     'CRUDE OIL (MMT)'          col_hide   '20'    ,
549                                      'TON'     'CRUDE OIL (Tonnes)'       col_hide   '20'    ,
550                                      'MMBTU'   'GAS (Mn.MMBTU)'           col_hide   '20'    ,
551                                      'MMBOE'   'GAS (MMBOE)'              col_hide   '20'    ,
552                                      'BCM'     'GAS (BCM)'                col_hide   '20'    .
553
554    ENDFORM. "fcat_fill_quant

556    FORM alv_fcat_quant USING  VALUE(p_011)   ""Append field catalog table
557                               VALUE(p_012)
558                               VALUE(p_013)
559                               VALUE(p_014).
560
561      ls_fcat-fieldname   = p_011.
562      ls_fcat-scrtext_l   = p_012.
563      ls_fcat-no_out      = p_013.
564      ls_fcat-outputlen   = p_014.
565
566      APPEND ls_fcat TO lt_fcat.
567      CLEAR ls_fcat.
568
569    ENDFORM.  " alv_fcat_quant
570
571    FORM cal_quant.   "calculate Quant Info values
572
573      IF pnl NE 'PRO_ANA'.
574        PERFORM conv_fact_chk.
575        IF NOT grid1 IS INITIAL.
576          CALL METHOD grid1->check_changed_data.
577        ENDIF.
578      ENDIF.
579
580      DATA lv_msg1 TYPE string .
581
582      IF mode EQ 'CRE'.
583        CLEAR : wa_final_qi_o,wa_final_qi_g,wa_final_conv.
584      ENDIF.
585
586    *moving input parameters to Oil, Gas & Conversion table
587      wa_final_conv-company = wa_final_qi_g-company = wa_final_qi_o-company = p_company.
588      wa_final_conv-bukrs   = wa_final_qi_g-bukrs   = wa_final_qi_o-bukrs   = p_comp.
589      wa_final_conv-pro_nam = wa_final_qi_g-pro_nam = wa_final_qi_o-pro_nam = p_proj.
590      wa_final_conv-fisyear = wa_final_qi_g-fisyear = wa_final_qi_o-fisyear = p_year.
591      wa_final_conv-zperiod = wa_final_qi_g-zperiod = wa_final_qi_o-zperiod = s_period_low.
592
593      wa_final_conv-bbl_mmbbl   = bbl_mmbbl.
594      wa_final_conv-bbl_mmt     = bbl_mmt.
595      wa_final_conv-ton_bbl     = ton_bbl.
596
597      wa_final_conv-mmscm_mmbtu = mmscm_mmbtu.
598      wa_final_conv-mmscm_mmboe = mmscm_mmboe.
599      wa_final_conv-mmscm_bcm   = mmscm_bcm  .
600
601      "variables for calc.
602      DATA: prf_oil_o(15)  TYPE c, sh_ent_o(15) TYPE c, trl_flr_o(15) TYPE c, atrl_flr_o(15) TYPE c, int_con_o(15) TYPE c,
603            prf_oil_g(15)  TYPE c, sh_ent_g(15) TYPE c, trl_flr_g(15) TYPE c, atrl_flr_g(15) TYPE c, int_con_g(15) TYPE c,
604
605            net_prd_o(15)  TYPE c, opn_sto_o(15) TYPE c, clo_sto_o(15) TYPE c, chg_inv_o(15) TYPE c,
606            net_prd_g(15)  TYPE c,                                             chg_inv_g(15) TYPE c.
607
608      DATA : spl_flg TYPE c.
609
610
611    *check for any special characters
612      IF pnl NE 'PRO_ANA'.
613        LOOP AT it_quant INTO DATA(wa_quant_c).
614
615          IF wa_quant_c-bbl CA lv_spl.
616            CLEAR sy-ucomm.
617            spl_flg = 'X'.
618            MESSAGE 'Special character in amount field is not allowed' TYPE 'W' DISPLAY LIKE 'E'.
619          ENDIF.
620          IF wa_quant_c-mmscm CA lv_spl.
621            CLEAR sy-ucomm.
622            spl_flg = 'X'.
623            MESSAGE 'Special character in amount field is not allowed' TYPE 'W' DISPLAY LIKE 'E'.
624          ENDIF.
625
626          CLEAR wa_quant_c.
627        ENDLOOP.
628      ENDIF.
629
630      IF ton_bbl EQ '0.000000'. "to avoid divide by 0 error
631        ton_bbl = '1'.
632        DATA(ton_flg) = 'X'.
633      ENDIF.
634
635    *     Calculate the values screen input/fetched (formula :- oil X conversion|gas X conversion||exception=>TON:- oil / Conversion)
636      IF spl_flg NE 'X'.
637        LOOP AT it_quant INTO wa_quant.
638          CASE sy-tabix.
639            WHEN 1.  " 1 Opening inventory OVL:
640              IF mode = 'CRE'.
641                PERFORM cal_quant_cre USING sy-tabix.       "fetch values from internal table to calculate total value i.e 2 = 3 + 4.
642
643                wa_quant-bbl = opn_sto_o = q_val1_o + q_val2_o.
644
645              ELSEIF mode NE 'CRE'.
646                CLEAR opn_sto_o.
647                wa_quant-bbl = opn_sto_o = wa_final_qi_o-ost_tnk + wa_final_qi_o-ost_pip.   "When 1 = 2 + 3.
648
649              ENDIF.
650
651              wa_quant-mmbbl = wa_quant-bbl * bbl_mmbbl.
652              wa_quant-ton   = wa_quant-bbl / ton_bbl.
653              wa_quant-mmt   = wa_quant-ton * bbl_mmt.
654
655              wa_quant-mmbtu = 0 * mmscm_mmbtu.
656              wa_quant-mmboe = 0 * mmscm_mmboe.
657              wa_quant-bcm   = 0 * mmscm_bcm.
658
659            WHEN 2.  " 1a Opening inventory OVL: Stock in Storage tanks/locations/FPSO
660              wa_final_qi_o-ost_tnk = wa_quant-bbl.
661    *           " N/A for Gas
662
663              wa_quant-mmbbl = wa_final_qi_o-ost_tnk * bbl_mmbbl.
664              wa_quant-ton   = wa_final_qi_o-ost_tnk / ton_bbl.
665              wa_quant-mmt   = wa_quant-ton * bbl_mmt.
666
667              wa_quant-mmbtu = 0 * mmscm_mmbtu.
668              wa_quant-mmboe = 0 * mmscm_mmboe.
669              wa_quant-bcm   = 0 * mmscm_bcm.
670
671            WHEN 3.  "1b Opening inventory OVL: Stock in pipelines/linefill
672              wa_final_qi_o-ost_pip = wa_quant-bbl.
673              " N/A for Gas
674
675              wa_quant-mmbbl = wa_final_qi_o-ost_pip * bbl_mmbbl.
676              wa_quant-ton   = wa_final_qi_o-ost_pip / ton_bbl.
677              wa_quant-mmt   = wa_quant-ton * bbl_mmt.
678
679              wa_quant-mmbtu = 0 * mmscm_mmbtu.
680              wa_quant-mmboe = 0 * mmscm_mmboe.
681              wa_quant-bcm   = 0 * mmscm_bcm.
682
683            WHEN 5. "2 OVL share of Production
684              wa_final_qi_o-sh_prod = wa_quant-bbl.
685              wa_final_qi_g-sh_prod = wa_quant-mmscm.
686
687              wa_quant-mmbbl = wa_final_qi_o-sh_prod * bbl_mmbbl.
688              wa_quant-ton   = wa_final_qi_o-sh_prod / ton_bbl.
689              wa_quant-mmt   = wa_quant-ton * bbl_mmt.
690
691              wa_quant-mmbtu = wa_final_qi_g-sh_prod * mmscm_mmbtu.
692              wa_quant-mmboe = wa_final_qi_g-sh_prod * mmscm_mmboe.
693              wa_quant-bcm   = wa_final_qi_g-sh_prod * mmscm_bcm.
694
695            WHEN 7. "3 Profit Oil/Gas:
696              CLEAR : prf_oil_o,prf_oil_g.
697
698              IF mode = 'CRE'.
699                PERFORM cal_quant_cre USING sy-tabix.
700
701                wa_quant-bbl   = prf_oil_o = q_val1_o + q_val2_o.
702                wa_quant-mmscm = prf_oil_g = q_val1_g + q_val2_g.
703
704              ELSEIF mode NE 'CRE'.
705                prf_oil_o = wa_quant-bbl   = wa_final_qi_o-sh_lift + wa_final_qi_o-sh_take.
706                prf_oil_g = wa_quant-mmscm = wa_final_qi_g-sh_lift + wa_final_qi_g-sh_take.
707
708              ENDIF.
709
710              wa_quant-mmbbl = wa_quant-bbl * bbl_mmbbl.
711              wa_quant-ton   = wa_quant-bbl / ton_bbl.
712              wa_quant-mmt   = wa_quant-ton * bbl_mmt.
713
714              wa_quant-mmbtu = wa_quant-mmscm * mmscm_mmbtu.
715              wa_quant-mmboe = wa_quant-mmscm * mmscm_mmboe.
716              wa_quant-bcm   = wa_quant-mmscm * mmscm_bcm.
717
718
719            WHEN 8. "3a State/Govt. share-lifted by OVL
720              wa_final_qi_o-sh_lift = wa_quant-bbl.
721              wa_final_qi_g-sh_lift = wa_quant-mmscm.
722
723              wa_quant-mmbbl = wa_final_qi_o-sh_lift * bbl_mmbbl.
724              wa_quant-ton   = wa_final_qi_o-sh_lift / ton_bbl.
725              wa_quant-mmt   = wa_quant-ton * bbl_mmt.
726
727              wa_quant-mmbtu = wa_final_qi_g-sh_lift * mmscm_mmbtu.
728              wa_quant-mmboe = wa_final_qi_g-sh_lift * mmscm_mmboe.
729              wa_quant-bcm   = wa_final_qi_g-sh_lift * mmscm_bcm.
730
731
732            WHEN 9. "3b State/Govt. share-taken by state in kind
733              wa_final_qi_o-sh_take = wa_quant-bbl.
734              wa_final_qi_g-sh_take = wa_quant-mmscm.
735
736              wa_quant-mmbbl = wa_final_qi_o-sh_take * bbl_mmbbl.
737              wa_quant-ton   = wa_final_qi_o-sh_take / ton_bbl.
738              wa_quant-mmt   = wa_quant-ton * bbl_mmt.
739
740              wa_quant-mmbtu = wa_final_qi_g-sh_take * mmscm_mmbtu.
741              wa_quant-mmboe = wa_final_qi_g-sh_take * mmscm_mmboe.
742              wa_quant-bcm   = wa_final_qi_g-sh_take * mmscm_bcm.
743
744
745            WHEN 11.  "4 OVL share of entitled production (entitlement) (2-3)
746              wa_quant-bbl   = sh_ent_o = wa_final_qi_o-sh_prod - prf_oil_o.  "     When 11 = 5 - 7
747              wa_quant-mmscm = sh_ent_g = wa_final_qi_g-sh_prod - prf_oil_g.
748
749              wa_quant-mmbbl = wa_quant-bbl * bbl_mmbbl.
750              wa_quant-ton   = wa_quant-bbl / ton_bbl.
751              wa_quant-mmt   = wa_quant-ton * bbl_mmt.
752
753              wa_quant-mmbtu = wa_quant-mmscm * mmscm_mmbtu.
754              wa_quant-mmboe = wa_quant-mmscm * mmscm_mmboe.
755              wa_quant-bcm   = wa_quant-mmscm * mmscm_bcm.
756
757              CLEAR: prf_oil_o,
758                     prf_oil_g.
759
760
761            WHEN 13. "5 Transit Loss & Flaring:
762              IF mode = 'CRE'.
763                PERFORM cal_quant_cre USING sy-tabix.  "fetch values from the internal tables for further calculations
764
765                wa_quant-bbl   = trl_flr_o = q_val1_o + q_val2_o.
766                wa_quant-mmscm = trl_flr_g = q_val1_g + q_val2_g.
767
768              ELSEIF mode NE 'CRE'.
769
770                wa_quant-bbl   = trl_flr_o = wa_final_qi_o-trn_los + wa_final_qi_o-fl_vent.  " When 13 = 14 + 15
771                wa_quant-mmscm = trl_flr_g = wa_final_qi_g-trn_los + wa_final_qi_g-fl_vent.
772
773              ENDIF.
774
775              wa_quant-mmbbl = wa_quant-bbl * bbl_mmbbl.
776              wa_quant-ton   = wa_quant-bbl / ton_bbl.
777              wa_quant-mmt   = wa_quant-ton * bbl_mmt.
778
779              wa_quant-mmbtu = wa_quant-mmscm * mmscm_mmbtu.
780              wa_quant-mmboe = wa_quant-mmscm * mmscm_mmboe.
781              wa_quant-bcm   = wa_quant-mmscm * mmscm_bcm.
782
783
784            WHEN 14.    "5a Transit loss
785              wa_final_qi_o-trn_los = wa_quant-bbl.
786              wa_final_qi_g-trn_los = wa_quant-mmscm.
787
788              wa_quant-mmbbl = wa_final_qi_o-trn_los * bbl_mmbbl.
789              wa_quant-ton   = wa_final_qi_o-trn_los / ton_bbl.
790              wa_quant-mmt   = wa_quant-ton * bbl_mmt.
791
792              wa_quant-mmbtu = wa_final_qi_g-trn_los * mmscm_mmbtu.
793              wa_quant-mmboe = wa_final_qi_g-trn_los * mmscm_mmboe.
794              wa_quant-bcm   = wa_final_qi_g-trn_los * mmscm_bcm.
795
796
797            WHEN 15. "5b Flaring/vented
798              wa_final_qi_o-fl_vent = wa_quant-bbl.
799              wa_final_qi_g-fl_vent = wa_quant-mmscm.
800
801              wa_quant-mmbbl = wa_final_qi_o-fl_vent * bbl_mmbbl.
802              wa_quant-ton   = wa_final_qi_o-fl_vent / ton_bbl.
803              wa_quant-mmt   = wa_quant-ton * bbl_mmt.
804
805              wa_quant-mmbtu = wa_final_qi_g-fl_vent * mmscm_mmbtu.
806              wa_quant-mmboe = wa_final_qi_g-fl_vent * mmscm_mmboe.
807              wa_quant-bcm   = wa_final_qi_g-fl_vent * mmscm_bcm.
808
809
810            WHEN 17.  "6 Production quantity after transit loss & flaring (4-5)
811              wa_quant-bbl   = atrl_flr_o = sh_ent_o - trl_flr_o .            " When 17 = 11 - 13
812              wa_quant-mmscm = atrl_flr_g = sh_ent_g - trl_flr_g .
813
814              wa_quant-mmbbl = wa_quant-bbl * bbl_mmbbl.
815              wa_quant-ton   = wa_quant-bbl / ton_bbl.
816              wa_quant-mmt   = wa_quant-ton * bbl_mmt.
817
818              wa_quant-mmbtu = wa_quant-mmscm * mmscm_mmbtu.
819              wa_quant-mmboe = wa_quant-mmscm * mmscm_mmboe.
820              wa_quant-bcm   = wa_quant-mmscm * mmscm_bcm.
821
822
823            WHEN 19. "7 Internal consumption:
824              IF mode = 'CRE'.
825                PERFORM cal_quant_cre USING sy-tabix.
826
827                wa_quant-bbl   = int_con_o = q_val1_o + q_val2_o.
828                wa_quant-mmscm = int_con_g = q_val1_g + q_val2_g.
829
830              ELSEIF mode NE 'CRE'.
831                wa_quant-bbl   = int_con_o = wa_final_qi_o-prd_act + wa_final_qi_o-others .    "When 19 = 20 + 21
832                wa_quant-mmscm = int_con_g = wa_final_qi_g-prd_act + wa_final_qi_g-others .
833
834              ENDIF.
835
836              wa_quant-mmbbl = wa_quant-bbl * bbl_mmbbl.
837              wa_quant-ton   = wa_quant-bbl / ton_bbl.
838              wa_quant-mmt   = wa_quant-ton * bbl_mmt.
839
840              wa_quant-mmbtu = wa_quant-mmscm * mmscm_mmbtu.
841              wa_quant-mmboe = wa_quant-mmscm * mmscm_mmboe.
842              wa_quant-bcm   = wa_quant-mmscm * mmscm_bcm.
843
844
845            WHEN 20. "7a Production activity
846              wa_final_qi_o-prd_act = wa_quant-bbl.
847              wa_final_qi_g-prd_act = wa_quant-mmscm.
848
849              wa_quant-mmbbl = wa_final_qi_o-prd_act * bbl_mmbbl.
850              wa_quant-ton   = wa_final_qi_o-prd_act / ton_bbl.
851              wa_quant-mmt   = wa_quant-ton * bbl_mmt.
852
853              wa_quant-mmbtu = wa_final_qi_g-prd_act * mmscm_mmbtu.
854              wa_quant-mmboe = wa_final_qi_g-prd_act * mmscm_mmboe.
855              wa_quant-bcm   = wa_final_qi_g-prd_act * mmscm_bcm.
856
857
858            WHEN 21. "7b Others
859              wa_final_qi_o-others = wa_quant-bbl.
860              wa_final_qi_g-others = wa_quant-mmscm.
861
862              wa_quant-mmbbl = wa_final_qi_o-others * bbl_mmbbl.
863              wa_quant-ton   = wa_final_qi_o-others / ton_bbl.
864              wa_quant-mmt   = wa_quant-ton * bbl_mmt.
865
866              wa_quant-mmbtu = wa_final_qi_g-others * mmscm_mmbtu.
867              wa_quant-mmboe = wa_final_qi_g-others * mmscm_mmboe.
868              wa_quant-bcm   = wa_final_qi_g-others * mmscm_bcm.
869
870
871            WHEN 23. "8 Net production quantity available (6-7)
872              wa_quant-bbl   = net_prd_o = atrl_flr_o - int_con_o .            " When 23 = 17 - 19
873              wa_quant-mmscm = net_prd_g = atrl_flr_g - int_con_g .
874
875              wa_quant-mmbbl = wa_quant-bbl * bbl_mmbbl.
876              wa_quant-ton   = wa_quant-bbl / ton_bbl.
877              wa_quant-mmt   = wa_quant-ton * bbl_mmt.
878
879              wa_quant-mmbtu = wa_quant-mmscm * mmscm_mmbtu.
880              wa_quant-mmboe = wa_quant-mmscm * mmscm_mmboe.
881              wa_quant-bcm   = wa_quant-mmscm * mmscm_bcm.
882
883
884            WHEN 25. "9 Closing Stock:
885              IF mode = 'CRE'.
886                PERFORM cal_quant_cre USING sy-tabix.
887
888                wa_quant-bbl = clo_sto_o = q_val1_o + q_val2_o.
889
890              ELSEIF mode NE 'CRE'.
891                wa_quant-bbl = clo_sto_o = wa_final_qi_o-cst_tnk + wa_final_qi_o-cst_pip .      "When 25 = 26 + 27
892
893              ENDIF.
894
895              wa_quant-mmbbl = wa_quant-bbl * bbl_mmbbl.
896              wa_quant-ton   = wa_quant-bbl / ton_bbl.
897              wa_quant-mmt   = wa_quant-ton * bbl_mmt.
898
899              wa_quant-mmbtu = wa_quant-mmscm * mmscm_mmbtu.
900              wa_quant-mmboe = wa_quant-mmscm * mmscm_mmboe.
901              wa_quant-bcm   = wa_quant-mmscm * mmscm_bcm.
902
903
904            WHEN 26. "9a Stock in Storage tanks/locations/FPSO
905              wa_final_qi_o-cst_tnk = wa_quant-bbl.
906
907              wa_quant-mmbbl = wa_final_qi_o-cst_tnk * bbl_mmbbl.
908              wa_quant-ton   = wa_final_qi_o-cst_tnk / ton_bbl.
909              wa_quant-mmt   = wa_quant-ton * bbl_mmt.
910
911              wa_quant-mmbtu = 0 * mmscm_mmbtu.
912              wa_quant-mmboe = 0 * mmscm_mmboe.
913              wa_quant-bcm   = 0 * mmscm_bcm.
914
915
916            WHEN 27. "9b Stock in pipelines/linefill
917              wa_final_qi_o-cst_pip = wa_quant-bbl.
918              "N/A for gas
919
920              wa_quant-mmbbl = wa_final_qi_o-cst_pip * bbl_mmbbl.
921              wa_quant-ton   = wa_final_qi_o-cst_pip / ton_bbl.
922              wa_quant-mmt   = wa_quant-ton * bbl_mmt.
923
924              wa_quant-mmbtu = 0 * mmscm_mmbtu.
925              wa_quant-mmboe = 0 * mmscm_mmboe.
926              wa_quant-bcm   = 0 * mmscm_bcm.
927
928
929            WHEN 29. "10 Adj. for Changes in Inventories/Other adj.
930              wa_final_qi_o-ch_invt = chg_inv_o = wa_quant-bbl.
931              wa_final_qi_g-ch_invt = chg_inv_g = wa_quant-mmscm.
932
933              wa_quant-mmbbl = wa_final_qi_o-ch_invt * bbl_mmbbl.
934              wa_quant-ton   = wa_final_qi_o-ch_invt / ton_bbl.
935              wa_quant-mmt   = wa_quant-ton * bbl_mmt.
936
937              wa_quant-mmbtu = wa_final_qi_g-ch_invt * mmscm_mmbtu.
938              wa_quant-mmboe = wa_final_qi_g-ch_invt * mmscm_mmboe.
939              wa_quant-bcm   = wa_final_qi_g-ch_invt * mmscm_bcm.
940
941
942            WHEN 31.  "11 Net quantity available for sale (8-9-10+1+3a)
943              wa_quant-bbl   = net_prd_o - clo_sto_o - chg_inv_o + opn_sto_o + wa_final_qi_o-sh_lift.
944              wa_quant-mmscm = net_prd_g - chg_inv_g + wa_final_qi_g-sh_lift.
945              "When 31 = 23 - 25 - 29 + 1 + 8.
946              wa_quant-mmbbl = wa_quant-bbl * bbl_mmbbl.
947              wa_quant-ton   = wa_quant-bbl / ton_bbl.
948              wa_quant-mmt   = wa_quant-ton * bbl_mmt.
949
950              wa_quant-mmbtu = wa_quant-mmscm * mmscm_mmbtu.
951              wa_quant-mmboe = wa_quant-mmscm * mmscm_mmboe.
952              wa_quant-bcm   = wa_quant-mmscm * mmscm_bcm.
953
954
955            WHEN 33. "12 Overlift/(underlift) quantity
956              IF mode = 'CRE'.
957                PERFORM cal_quant_cre USING sy-tabix.
958
959                wa_final_qi_o-ovr_und = wa_quant-bbl = ( q_val2_o ) - ( net_prd_o - clo_sto_o - chg_inv_o + opn_sto_o + wa_final_qi_o-sh_lift ).
960                "N/A for gas
961
962              ELSEIF mode NE 'CRE'.
963                wa_final_qi_o-ovr_und = wa_quant-bbl = ( wa_final_qi_o-tot_sal ) - ( net_prd_o - clo_sto_o - chg_inv_o + opn_sto_o + wa_final_qi_o-sh_lift ).        " 35 = 36 - 33
964                "N/A for gas
965              ENDIF.
966
967              wa_quant-mmbbl = wa_final_qi_o-ovr_und * bbl_mmbbl.
968              wa_quant-ton   = wa_final_qi_o-ovr_und / ton_bbl.
969              wa_quant-mmt   = wa_quant-ton * bbl_mmt.
970
971              wa_quant-mmbtu = 0 * mmscm_mmbtu.
972              wa_quant-mmboe = 0 * mmscm_mmboe.
973              wa_quant-bcm   = 0 * mmscm_bcm.
974
975
976            WHEN 35.  "13 Total Sales quantity
977              wa_final_qi_o-tot_sal = wa_quant-bbl.
978              wa_final_qi_g-tot_sal = wa_quant-mmscm.
979
980              wa_quant-mmbbl = wa_final_qi_o-tot_sal * bbl_mmbbl.
981              wa_quant-ton   = wa_final_qi_o-tot_sal / ton_bbl.
982              wa_quant-mmt   = wa_quant-ton * bbl_mmt.
983
984              wa_quant-mmbtu = wa_final_qi_g-tot_sal * mmscm_mmbtu.
985              wa_quant-mmboe = wa_final_qi_g-tot_sal * mmscm_mmboe.
986              wa_quant-bcm   = wa_final_qi_g-tot_sal * mmscm_bcm.
987
988            WHEN 37.  "14 Total Sales quantity-net of state share (13-3a)
989              READ TABLE it_quant INTO DATA(wa_quant_z1) INDEX 8.
990              READ TABLE it_quant INTO DATA(wa_quant_z2) INDEX 35.
991              wa_quant-bbl   = wa_quant_z2-bbl - wa_quant_z1-bbl.
992              wa_quant-mmscm = wa_quant_z2-mmscm - wa_quant_z1-mmscm.
993
994              wa_quant-mmbbl = wa_quant_z2-mmbbl - wa_quant_z1-mmbbl.
995              wa_quant-ton   = wa_quant_z2-ton - wa_quant_z1-ton.
996              wa_quant-mmt   = wa_quant_z2-mmt - wa_quant_z1-mmt.
997
998              wa_quant-mmbtu = wa_quant_z2-mmbtu - wa_quant_z1-mmbtu.
999              wa_quant-mmboe = wa_quant_z2-mmboe - wa_quant_z1-mmboe.
1000             wa_quant-bcm   = wa_quant_z2-bcm - wa_quant_z1-bcm.
1001
1002             CLEAR : wa_quant_z1,wa_quant_z2.
1003         ENDCASE.
1004
1005
1006         IF ( mode NE 'CRE' ) OR ( mode NE 'CHA' ).
1007           IF sy-ucomm = 'SAVE'.
1008
1009             IF wa_quant-io_flag CA '1'.        "Check Mandatory fields for OIL column
1010               IF wa_quant-bbl = ' '.
1011                 CLEAR : sy-ucomm,lv_msg1.
1012                 CONCATENATE 'Field' wa_quant-data 'of BBL column cannot be blank' INTO lv_msg1 SEPARATED BY space.
1013                 MESSAGE lv_msg1 TYPE 'E'.
1014               ENDIF.
1015             ENDIF.
1016
1017             IF wa_quant-io_flag CA '2'.         "Check Mandatory fields for GAS column
1018               IF wa_quant-mmscm = ' '.
1019                 CLEAR : sy-ucomm,lv_msg1.
1020                 CONCATENATE 'Field' wa_quant-data 'of MMSCM column cannot be blank' INTO lv_msg1 SEPARATED BY space.
1021                 MESSAGE lv_msg1 TYPE 'E'.
1022               ENDIF.
1023             ENDIF.
1024
1025           ENDIF.
1026
1027           IF wa_quant-bbl IS NOT INITIAL.  "round off all values to 3 decimals
1028             wa_quant-bbl   = round( val = wa_quant-bbl   dec = '3' ).
1029             wa_quant-mmbbl = round( val = wa_quant-mmbbl dec = '3' ).
1030             wa_quant-mmt   = round( val = wa_quant-mmt   dec = '3' ).
1031             wa_quant-ton   = round( val = wa_quant-ton   dec = '3' ).
1032           ENDIF.
1033
1034           IF wa_quant-mmscm IS NOT INITIAL. "round off all values to 3 decimals
1035             wa_quant-mmscm = round( val = wa_quant-mmscm dec = '3' ).
1036             wa_quant-mmbtu = round( val = wa_quant-mmbtu dec = '3' ).
1037             wa_quant-mmboe = round( val = wa_quant-mmboe dec = '3' ).
1038             wa_quant-bcm   = round( val = wa_quant-bcm   dec = '3' ).
1039           ENDIF.
1040
1041           MODIFY it_quant FROM wa_quant TRANSPORTING bbl mmscm mmbbl mmt ton mmbtu mmboe bcm.
1042           CLEAR wa_quant.
1043
1044         ENDIF.
1045
1046       ENDLOOP.
1047     ENDIF.
1048
1049     IF ton_flg = 'X'.
1050       CLEAR ton_bbl.
1051     ENDIF.
1052     CLEAR ton_flg.
1053
1054     CLEAR : prf_oil_o, sh_ent_o, trl_flr_o, atrl_flr_o, int_con_o,
1055             prf_oil_g, sh_ent_g, trl_flr_g, atrl_flr_g, int_con_g,
1056             net_prd_o, opn_sto_o, clo_sto_o, chg_inv_o, chg_inv_o,
1057             net_prd_g,                                  chg_inv_g.
1058
1059
1060
1061   ENDFORM."CAL_QUANT

1064   FORM cal_quant_cre USING VALUE(quant_indx). "fetch values from the internal tables for further calculations
1065
1066     CLEAR : q_val1_o,q_val2_o,q_val1_g,q_val2_g.
1067     quant_indx = quant_indx + 1.
1068
1069     READ TABLE it_quant INTO DATA(wa_quant2) INDEX quant_indx.
1070     q_val1_o = wa_quant2-bbl.
1071     q_val1_g = wa_quant2-mmscm.
1072
1073     CLEAR wa_quant2.
1074     quant_indx = quant_indx + 1.
1075     READ TABLE it_quant INTO wa_quant2 INDEX quant_indx.
1076     q_val2_o = wa_quant2-bbl.
1077     q_val2_g = wa_quant2-mmscm.
1078
1079   ENDFORM. "CAL_QUANT_CRE

1082   FORM save_quant.      "Save Quant Info data into database tables
1083
1084     IF ( mode NE 'DIS' ) .
1085
1086       PERFORM chk_before_save.   "checking (1a+8-13=9a) and conv factor not blank
1087
1088       PERFORM popup_save.        "confirmation before saving to database tables
1089
1090       IF ( mode EQ 'CRE' ) .     "update status as Created and user detials
1091         wa_final_conv-created_by = wa_final_qi_g-created_by = wa_final_qi_o-created_by  = sy-uname.
1092         wa_final_conv-created_on = wa_final_qi_g-created_on = wa_final_qi_o-created_on  = sy-datum.
1093
1094       ELSEIF ( mode EQ 'CHA' ) .  "update status as Changed and user detials
1095         wa_final_conv-changed_by = wa_final_qi_g-changed_by = wa_final_qi_o-changed_by  = sy-uname.
1096         wa_final_conv-changed_on = wa_final_qi_g-changed_on = wa_final_qi_o-changed_on  = sy-datum.
1097
1098         wa_final_conv-approved_by = wa_final_qi_g-approved_by = wa_final_qi_o-approved_by  = ' '.
1099         wa_final_conv-approved_on = wa_final_qi_g-approved_on = wa_final_qi_o-approved_on  = '00000000'.
1100
1101       ENDIF.
1102
1103       IF ans = '1'.   "save data into database tables on confirmation
1104         MODIFY zpnl_qi_oil FROM wa_final_qi_o.   "Save oil data into oil table
1105         CLEAR wa_final_qi_o.
1106
1107         MODIFY zpnl_qi_gas FROM wa_final_qi_g.   "Save gas data into gas table
1108         CLEAR wa_final_qi_g.
1109
1110         MODIFY zpnl_qi_convo FROM wa_final_conv. "Save conversion factor data into conversion factor
1111         CLEAR wa_final_conv.
1112
1113         MESSAGE 'Data saved successfully.' TYPE 'S'.
1114
1115       ENDIF.
1116       CLEAR ans.
1117
1118     ENDIF.
1119
1120   ENDFORM."SAVE_QUANT

1123
1124     IF mode EQ 'FRE'.

1140         wa_final_conv-freezed_on = wa_final_qi_g-freezed_on = wa_final_qi_o-freezed_on  = sy-datum.

1150       IF mode = 'APP'.  "update status as approve and user details in database tables

1153
1154

1158         MODIFY zpnl_qi_gas FROM wa_final_qi_g.
1159         CLEAR wa_final_qi_g.
1160
1161         MODIFY zpnl_qi_convo FROM wa_final_conv.
1162         CLEAR wa_final_conv.
1163
1164         MESSAGE 'Data has been approved.' TYPE 'S'.
1165
1166   *******************      ADDED BY APARNA ON 03.07.2024
1167
1168       ELSEIF mode = 'APF'.  "update status as approve and user details in database tables
1169         wa_final_conv-approved_by_rp = wa_final_qi_g-approved_by_rp = wa_final_qi_o-approved_by_rp  = sy-uname.
1170         wa_final_conv-approved_on_rp = wa_final_qi_g-approved_on_rp = wa_final_qi_o-approved_on_rp  = sy-datum.
1171
1172
1173         MODIFY zpnl_qi_oil FROM wa_final_qi_o.
1174         CLEAR wa_final_qi_o.
1175
1176         MODIFY zpnl_qi_gas FROM wa_final_qi_g.
1177         CLEAR wa_final_qi_g.
1178
1179         MODIFY zpnl_qi_convo FROM wa_final_conv.
1180         CLEAR wa_final_conv.
1181
1182         MESSAGE 'Data has been approved.' TYPE 'S'.
1183
1184   ********      ENDED BY APARNA ON 03.07.2024
1185
1186       ELSEIF mode = 'FRE'.    "update status as freeze and user details in database tables
1187         wa_final_conv-freezed_by = wa_final_qi_g-freezed_by = wa_final_qi_o-freezed_by  = sy-uname.
1188         wa_final_conv-freezed_on = wa_final_qi_g-freezed_on = wa_final_qi_o-freezed_on  = sy-datum.
1189
1190         MODIFY zpnl_qi_oil FROM wa_final_qi_o.
1191         CLEAR wa_final_qi_o.
1192
1193         MODIFY zpnl_qi_gas FROM wa_final_qi_g.
1194         CLEAR wa_final_qi_g.
1195
1196         MODIFY zpnl_qi_convo FROM wa_final_conv.
1197         CLEAR wa_final_conv.
1198
1199         MESSAGE 'Data has been freezed. You cannot change this data now.' TYPE 'S'.
1200
1201       ENDIF.
1202     ENDIF.
1203
1204   ENDFORM."APP_QUANT

1206   FORM fetch_quant.  "Fetch saved/created data from tables
1207
1208     DATA s_period_low_1(6) TYPE c.
1209     DATA s_period_low_2(6) TYPE c.
1210     DATA auto_cal TYPE c.
1211

1213            wa_final_qi_g_1 TYPE zpnl_qi_oil,
1214            wa_final_conv_1 TYPE zpnl_qi_convo.

1232       s_period_low_2 = '9M'.
1233       auto_cal = 'X'.
1234     ELSEIF s_period_low EQ 'Q4'.

1236       s_period_low_2 = 'ANNUAL'.

1301                                                              bukrs = p_comp

1508   FORM download_xl. "Download Quantitative Information Excel.
1509     DATA : BEGIN OF excel_heading ,
1510              head1 TYPE char30,
1511              head2 TYPE char30,
1512            END OF excel_heading .
1513
1514     DATA : it_heading LIKE STANDARD TABLE OF excel_heading INITIAL SIZE 0.
1515
1516     CONSTANTS g_c_ftype TYPE char10 VALUE 'ASC'.
1517     DATA : i_file_table TYPE  TABLE OF file_table,
1518            l_filetable  TYPE  file_table,
1519            l_rc         TYPE  i,
1520            l_p_def_file TYPE  string,
1521            l_p_file     TYPE  string,
1522            def_name     TYPE  string,
1523            l_path       TYPE string,
1524            l_fullpath   TYPE string,
1525            l_usr_act    TYPE  i.
1526
1527     DATA : it_quant_xl TYPE STANDARD TABLE OF ty_quant,
1528            wa_quant_xl TYPE ty_quant.
1529
1530     CONCATENATE p_comp'-'p_proj'-'p_year'-'s_period_low'.XLS' INTO def_name . "Default file name
1531
1532     CALL METHOD cl_gui_frontend_services=>file_save_dialog
1533       EXPORTING
1534         window_title         = 'Download Qunatitative Information Excel'
1535         default_file_name    = def_name
1536       CHANGING
1537         filename             = l_p_file
1538         path                 = l_path
1539         fullpath             = l_fullpath
1540         user_action          = l_usr_act
1541       EXCEPTIONS
1542         cntl_error           = 1
1543         error_no_gui         = 2
1544         not_supported_by_gui = 3
1545         OTHERS               = 4.
1546
1547     IF sy-subrc = 0 AND l_usr_act <> cl_gui_frontend_services=>action_cancel.
1548
1549       LOOP AT i_file_table  INTO l_filetable.
1550         l_p_file = l_filetable.
1551         EXIT.
1552       ENDLOOP.
1553
1554       CLEAR:it_quant_xl,wa_quant_xl-data.
1555       REFRESH it_quant_xl[].
1556
1557       "Create header
1558       CONCATENATE 'Input Parameters:- ' wa_quant_xl-data INTO wa_quant_xl-data.
1559       CONCATENATE 'Conversion Factor:- ' wa_quant_xl-bbl INTO wa_quant_xl-bbl .
1560       APPEND wa_quant_xl TO it_quant_xl.
1561       CLEAR wa_quant_xl.
1562
1563       CONCATENATE 'Company:- ' p_company INTO wa_quant_xl-data .
1564       wa_quant_xl-bbl   = 'Crude Oil'.
1565       wa_quant_xl-mmscm = 'BBL To MMBBL'.
1566       wa_quant_xl-mmbbl = bbl_mmbbl.
1567       APPEND wa_quant_xl TO it_quant_xl.
1568       CLEAR wa_quant_xl.
1569
1570       CONCATENATE 'Company Code:- ' p_comp INTO wa_quant_xl-data .
1571       wa_quant_xl-bbl   = 'Crude Oil'.
1572       wa_quant_xl-mmscm = 'BBL To MMT'.
1573       wa_quant_xl-mmbbl = bbl_mmt.
1574       APPEND wa_quant_xl TO it_quant_xl.
1575       CLEAR wa_quant_xl.
1576
1577       CONCATENATE 'Project:- ' p_proj INTO wa_quant_xl-data .
1578       wa_quant_xl-bbl   = 'Crude Oil'.
1579       wa_quant_xl-mmscm = 'TON To BBL'.
1580       wa_quant_xl-mmbbl = ton_bbl.
1581       APPEND wa_quant_xl TO it_quant_xl.
1582       CLEAR wa_quant_xl.
1583
1584       CONCATENATE 'Fiscal Year:- ' p_year INTO wa_quant_xl-data .
1585       wa_quant_xl-bbl   = 'Natural Gas'.
1586       wa_quant_xl-mmscm = 'MMSCM To MMBTU'.
1587       wa_quant_xl-mmbbl = mmscm_mmbtu.
1588       APPEND wa_quant_xl TO it_quant_xl.
1589       CLEAR wa_quant_xl.
1590
1591       CONCATENATE 'Period:- ' s_period_low INTO wa_quant_xl-data .
1592       wa_quant_xl-bbl   = 'Natural Gas'.
1593       wa_quant_xl-mmscm = 'MMSCM To MMBOE'.
1594       wa_quant_xl-mmbbl = mmscm_mmboe.
1595       APPEND wa_quant_xl TO it_quant_xl.
1596       CLEAR wa_quant_xl.
1597
1598       wa_quant_xl-bbl   = 'Natural Gas'.
1599       wa_quant_xl-mmscm = 'MMSCM To BCM'.
1600       wa_quant_xl-mmbbl = mmscm_bcm.
1601       APPEND wa_quant_xl TO it_quant_xl.
1602       CLEAR wa_quant_xl.
1603
1604       APPEND wa_quant_xl TO it_quant_xl.
1605       CLEAR wa_quant_xl.
1606
1607       APPEND wa_quant_xl TO it_quant_xl.
1608       CLEAR wa_quant_xl.
1609
1610       "Create Cloumns
1611       wa_quant_xl-sr_no = 'No.'.
1612       wa_quant_xl-data  = 'Quantitative Information'.
1613       wa_quant_xl-bbl   = 'CRUDE OIL(BBL)'.
1614       wa_quant_xl-mmscm = 'GAS(MMSCM)'.
1615       wa_quant_xl-mmbbl = 'CRUDE OIL(MMBBL)'.
1616       wa_quant_xl-mmt   = 'CRUDE OIL(MMT)'.

1618       wa_quant_xl-mmbtu = 'GAS(MMBTU)'.
1619       wa_quant_xl-mmboe = 'GAS(MMBOE)'.
1620       wa_quant_xl-bcm   = 'GAS(BCM)'.
1621
1622       APPEND wa_quant_xl TO it_quant_xl.
1623       CLEAR wa_quant_xl.
1624

1628         wa_quant_xl-io_flag = ''.

1631       ENDLOOP.
1632
1633       APPEND wa_quant_xl TO it_quant_xl.
1634       CLEAR wa_quant_xl.
1635
1636       APPEND wa_quant_xl TO it_quant_xl.
1637       CLEAR wa_quant_xl.

1640         EXPORTING
1641           filename                = def_name

1987
1988         ELSE.

1990             PERFORM fill_celltab_prof_prd USING 'RO' '0'       "make field as read only/available as output field
1991                                  CHANGING lt_celltab.
1992           ELSE.
1993             PERFORM fill_celltab_prof_prd USING 'RO' ' '       "make field as read only/available as output field
1994                                  CHANGING lt_celltab.
1995           ENDIF.
1996         ENDIF.
1997
1998         INSERT LINES OF lt_celltab INTO TABLE it_prof_prd-celltab.
1999         MODIFY it_prof_prd INDEX l_index.
2000
2001       ENDLOOP.
2002
2003     ENDIF.

2007   FORM fill_celltab_prof_prd USING VALUE(p_mode) VALUE(io_flag2)
2008                     CHANGING pt_celltab TYPE lvc_t_styl.
2009
2010     DATA : ls_celltab TYPE lvc_t_styl WITH HEADER LINE.
2011
2012     CLEAR l_mode.
2013     CLEAR l_mode2.
2014
2015     IF p_mode EQ 'RW'.
2016       l_mode = cl_gui_alv_grid=>mc_style_enabled.
2017     ELSE.
2018       l_mode = cl_gui_alv_grid=>mc_style_disabled.
2019     ENDIF.
2020
2021     ls_celltab-fieldname = 'PER'.
2022     ls_celltab-style = cl_gui_alv_grid=>mc_style_disabled.   "first column as read only
2023
2024     IF io_flag2 CA '0'.
2025     ENDIF.
2026     INSERT ls_celltab INTO TABLE pt_celltab.
2027
2028     ls_celltab-fieldname = 'OILNG'.
2029     ls_celltab-style = cl_gui_alv_grid=>mc_style_disabled.   "second column as read only
2030     INSERT ls_celltab INTO TABLE pt_celltab.
2031
2032     ls_celltab-fieldname = 'OIL'.                            "third column as read only/read and write field
2033     IF io_flag2 CA '2'.
2034       ls_celltab-style = l_mode.
2035     ELSE.
2036       ls_celltab-style = cl_gui_alv_grid=>mc_style_disabled.
2037     ENDIF.
2038     INSERT ls_celltab INTO TABLE pt_celltab.
2039
2040     ls_celltab-fieldname = 'GAS'.                           "fourth column as read only/read and write field
2041     IF io_flag2 CA '3'.
2042       ls_celltab-style = l_mode.
2043     ELSE.
2044       ls_celltab-style = cl_gui_alv_grid=>mc_style_disabled.
2045     ENDIF.
2046     INSERT ls_celltab INTO TABLE pt_celltab.
2047
2048   ENDFORM."fill_celltab_prof_prd

2061   FORM alv_fcat  USING  VALUE(p_011)
2062                         VALUE(p_012)
2063                         VALUE(p_013).
2064
2065     ls_fcat-fieldname  = p_011.
2066     ls_fcat-scrtext_l  = p_012.
2067     ls_fcat-outputlen  = p_013.
2068     ls_fcat-no_zero    = abap_true.
2069
2070     IF ls_fcat-fieldname NE 'PER'.
2071       ls_fcat-decimals   = 2.
2072       ls_fcat-decimals_o = 2.
2073       ls_fcat-round      = 2.
2074       ls_fcat-inttype   = 'P'.
2075       ls_fcat-decimals  = '3'.
2076     ENDIF.
2077     IF ls_fcat-fieldname EQ 'VALS'.
2078       ls_fcat-datatype   = 'DEC'.
2079       ls_fcat-decimals   = 2.
2080       ls_fcat-decimals_o = 2.
2081       ls_fcat-round      = 2.
2082       ls_fcat-inttype   = 'P'.
2083       ls_fcat-decimals  = '3'.
2084     ENDIF.
2085
2086
2087     APPEND ls_fcat TO lt_fcat.
2088     CLEAR ls_fcat.
2089
2090   ENDFORM." alv_fcat
