2139            in_tax_oil(10)   TYPE c,
2140            in_tax_gas(10)   TYPE c,
2141
2142            net_atax_ong(10) TYPE c,   "Net Profit/(Loss) after tax IX (VII-VIII)
2143            net_atax_oil(10) TYPE c,
2144            net_atax_gas(10) TYPE c.
2145
2146
2147     IF NOT grid1 IS INITIAL.
2148       CALL METHOD grid1->check_changed_data.
2149     ENDIF.
2150
2151     wa_prd_oil-company = p_company.
2152     wa_prd_gas-company = p_company.
2153
2154     wa_prd_oil-bukrs = p_comp.
2155     wa_prd_gas-bukrs = p_comp.
2156
2157     wa_prd_oil-pro_nam = p_proj.
2158     wa_prd_gas-pro_nam = p_proj.
2159
2160     wa_prd_oil-fisyear = p_year.
2161     wa_prd_gas-fisyear = p_year.
2162
2163     wa_prd_oil-zperiod = s_period_low.
2164     wa_prd_gas-zperiod = s_period_low.
2165
2166
2167     IF it_prof_prd2[] IS NOT INITIAL.
2168       REFRESH it_prof_prd[].
2169       CLEAR : it_prof_prd, wa_prof_prd.
2170
2171       DO 6 TIMES.
2172         APPEND wa_prof_prd TO it_prof_prd.
2173       ENDDO.
2174
2175       LOOP AT it_prof_prd2 INTO DATA(wa_prof_prd2).
2176         MOVE-CORRESPONDING wa_prof_prd2 TO wa_prof_prd.
2177         APPEND wa_prof_prd TO it_prof_prd.
2178         CLEAR : wa_prof_prd2,wa_prof_prd.
2179       ENDLOOP.
2180     ENDIF.
2181
2182     LOOP AT it_prof_prd INTO wa_prof_prd.
2183
2184       IF wa_prof_prd-oil CA lv_spl.
2185         CLEAR sy-ucomm.
2186         MESSAGE 'Special character in amount field is not allowed' TYPE 'W' DISPLAY LIKE 'E'.
2187         EXIT.
2188       ENDIF.
2189
2190       IF wa_prof_prd-gas CA lv_spl.
2191         CLEAR sy-ucomm.
2192         MESSAGE 'Special character in amount field is not allowed' TYPE 'W' DISPLAY LIKE 'E'.
2193         EXIT.
2194       ENDIF.
2195
2196       CASE sy-tabix.
2197         WHEN 1 ."GROSS PRODUCTION
2198           wa_prof_prd-oilng  = wa_prof_prd-oil + wa_prof_prd-gas.
2199           wa_prd_oil-sh_prod = wa_prof_prd-oil.
2200           wa_prd_gas-sh_prod = wa_prof_prd-gas.
2201
2202         WHEN 2 ."LESS: PROFIT OIL
2203           wa_prof_prd-oilng  = wa_prof_prd-oil + wa_prof_prd-gas.
2204           wa_prd_oil-prf_oil = wa_prof_prd-oil.
2205           wa_prd_gas-prf_oil = wa_prof_prd-gas.
2206
2207         WHEN 3 ."OVLs ENTITLEMENT (AFTER PROFIT OIL))
2208           wa_prof_prd-oilng  = wa_prof_prd-oil + wa_prof_prd-gas.
2209           wa_prd_oil-sh_entl = wa_prof_prd-oil.
2210           wa_prd_gas-sh_entl = wa_prof_prd-gas.
2211
2212         WHEN 4 ."ADD/(LESS):OVERLIFT QTY/(UNDER LIFT)
2213           wa_prof_prd-oilng  = wa_prof_prd-oil + wa_prof_prd-gas.
2214           wa_prd_oil-ovr_und = wa_prof_prd-oil.
2215           wa_prd_gas-ovr_und = wa_prof_prd-gas.
2216
2217         WHEN 5 ."ADD/(LESS): CHANGES IN INVENTORIES
2218           wa_prof_prd-oilng  = wa_prof_prd-oil + wa_prof_prd-gas.
2219           wa_prd_oil-chg_inv = wa_prof_prd-oil.
2220           wa_prd_gas-chg_inv = wa_prof_prd-gas.
2221
2222         WHEN 6 ."LESS: INTERNAL CONSUMPTION/RE-INJECTION IN RESERVOIR
2223           wa_prof_prd-oilng  = wa_prof_prd-oil + wa_prof_prd-gas.
2224           wa_prd_oil-int_con = wa_prof_prd-oil.
2225           wa_prd_gas-int_con = wa_prof_prd-gas.

2229           wa_prd_oil-rev_oil = wa_prof_prd-oil.
2230           wa_prd_gas-rev_oil = wa_prof_prd-gas.

2235           wa_prd_gas-rev_gas = wa_prof_prd-gas.

2238           wa_prof_prd-oilng  = wa_prof_prd-oil + wa_prof_prd-gas.
2239           wa_prd_oil-prfoilc = wa_prof_prd-oil.
2240           wa_prd_gas-prfoilc = wa_prof_prd-gas.
2241

2243           wa_prof_prd-oilng  = wa_prof_prd-oil + wa_prof_prd-gas.
2244           wa_prd_oil-sal_vap = wa_prof_prd-oil.
2245           wa_prd_gas-sal_vap = wa_prof_prd-gas.

2248           wa_prof_prd-oilng  = wa_prof_prd-oil + wa_prof_prd-gas.
2249           wa_prd_oil-oth_rev = wa_prof_prd-oil.
2250           wa_prd_gas-oth_rev = wa_prof_prd-gas.
2251
2252         WHEN 12."SALES REALISATION(I)
2253           net_slrl_oil = wa_prof_prd-oil   = ( wa_prd_oil-rev_oil + wa_prd_oil-rev_gas + wa_prd_oil-sal_vap + wa_prd_oil-oth_rev )
2254                                               - ( wa_prd_oil-prfoilc ).
2255
2256           net_slrl_gas = wa_prof_prd-gas   = ( wa_prd_gas-rev_oil + wa_prd_gas-rev_gas + wa_prd_gas-sal_vap + wa_prd_gas-oth_rev )
2257                                              - ( wa_prd_gas-prfoilc ).
2258
2259           net_slrl_ong = wa_prof_prd-oilng = net_slrl_oil + net_slrl_gas .
2260
2261         WHEN 13.
2262
2263         WHEN 14."OPERATING EXPENSES:
2264
2265         WHEN 15."ROYALTY/MET
2266           wa_prof_prd-oilng = wa_prof_prd-oil + wa_prof_prd-gas.
2267           wa_prd_oil-ls_met = wa_prof_prd-oil.
2268           wa_prd_gas-ls_met = wa_prof_prd-gas.
2269
2270         WHEN 16."OTHER TAXES(AS APPLICABLE)
2271           wa_prof_prd-oilng  = wa_prof_prd-oil + wa_prof_prd-gas.
2272           wa_prd_oil-ls_ottx = wa_prof_prd-oil.
2273           wa_prd_gas-ls_ottx = wa_prof_prd-gas.
2274
2275         WHEN 17."PRODUCTION EXPENSES
2276           wa_prof_prd-oilng  = wa_prof_prd-oil + wa_prof_prd-gas.
2277           wa_prd_oil-oe_prdx = wa_prof_prd-oil.
2278           wa_prd_gas-oe_prdx = wa_prof_prd-gas.
2279
2280         WHEN 18."OTHER OPERATING EXPENSE
2281           wa_prof_prd-oilng  = wa_prof_prd-oil + wa_prof_prd-gas.
2282           wa_prd_oil-oe_oexp = wa_prof_prd-oil.
2283           wa_prd_gas-oe_oexp = wa_prof_prd-gas.
2284
2285         WHEN 19."TRANSPORTATION EXPENSES
2286           wa_prof_prd-oilng  = wa_prof_prd-oil + wa_prof_prd-gas.
2287           wa_prd_oil-oechin2 = wa_prof_prd-oil.
2288           wa_prd_gas-oechin2 = wa_prof_prd-gas.
2289

2291           wa_prof_prd-oilng  = wa_prof_prd-oil + wa_prof_prd-gas.

2300                                             + wa_prd_gas-oe_prdx + wa_prd_gas-oechin2 + wa_prd_gas-oe_cgas.
2301
2302           tot_oprx_ong = wa_prof_prd-oilng = tot_oprx_oil + tot_oprx_gas.

2307

2310           wa_prd_oil-da_depl = wa_prof_prd-oil.
2311           wa_prd_gas-da_depl = wa_prof_prd-gas.

2315           wa_prd_oil-da_dpam = wa_prof_prd-oil.
2316           wa_prd_gas-da_dpam = wa_prof_prd-gas.

2321           wa_prd_gas-da_exps = wa_prof_prd-gas.

2326           wa_prd_gas-da_expd = wa_prof_prd-gas.

2330

2353         WHEN 33."ADJUSTMENT FOR COST OF SALES:
2354

2383         WHEN 40."OTHER INCOME/ EXPENSE:
2384

2388           wa_prd_gas-oth_oth = wa_prof_prd-gas.

2391           wa_prof_prd-oilng  = wa_prof_prd-oil + wa_prof_prd-gas.
2392           wa_prd_oil-oth_fin = wa_prof_prd-oil.
2393           wa_prd_gas-oth_fin = wa_prof_prd-gas.

2397           wa_prd_oil-oth_prv = wa_prof_prd-oil.
2398           wa_prd_gas-oth_prv = wa_prof_prd-gas.
2399
2400         WHEN 44."OTHER EXPENSES (INCL. EXCHANGE FLUCTUATION)
2401           wa_prof_prd-oilng  = wa_prof_prd-oil + wa_prof_prd-gas.
2402           wa_prd_oil-oth_exp = wa_prof_prd-oil.
2403           wa_prd_gas-oth_exp = wa_prof_prd-gas.
2404
2405         WHEN 45."TOTAL OTHER INCOME/ EXPENSE (X)
2406           tot_otin_oil = wa_prof_prd-oil   = wa_prd_oil-oth_oth + wa_prd_oil-oth_fin + wa_prd_oil-oth_prv + wa_prd_oil-oth_exp.
2407           tot_otin_gas = wa_prof_prd-gas   = wa_prd_gas-oth_oth + wa_prd_gas-oth_fin + wa_prd_gas-oth_prv + wa_prd_gas-oth_exp.

2414           net_btax_gas = wa_prof_prd-gas   = net_rev_gas - tot_otin_gas.

2416
2417         WHEN 48.
2418
2419         WHEN 49."INCOME TAX (XII)
2420           in_tax_oil = wa_prd_oil-inc_tax = wa_prof_prd-oil  .
2421           in_tax_gas = wa_prd_gas-inc_tax = wa_prof_prd-gas  .
2422           in_tax_ong = wa_prof_prd-oilng  = wa_prd_oil-inc_tax + wa_prd_gas-inc_tax.
2423
2424         WHEN 50.
2425
2426         WHEN 51."NET PROFIT/(LOSS) AFTER TAX XIV(XI-XII)
2427           net_atax_oil = wa_prof_prd-oil   = net_btax_oil - in_tax_oil .
2428           net_atax_gas = wa_prof_prd-gas   = net_btax_gas - in_tax_gas .
2429           net_atax_ong = wa_prof_prd-oilng = net_atax_oil + net_atax_gas.
2430
2431         WHEN 52.
2432
2433         WHEN 53."IMPAIRMENT LOSS/(REVERSAL)
2434           wa_prof_prd-oilng  = wa_prof_prd-oil + wa_prof_prd-gas.
2435           wa_prd_oil-imploss = wa_prof_prd-oil.
2436           wa_prd_gas-imploss = wa_prof_prd-gas.
2437
2438         WHEN 54.
2439
2440         WHEN 55."NET PROFIT/(LOSS) AFTER TAX-BEFORE IMPAIRMENT
2441           wa_prof_prd-oil   = net_atax_oil + wa_prd_oil-imploss .
2442           wa_prof_prd-gas   = net_atax_gas + wa_prd_gas-imploss .
2443           wa_prof_prd-oilng = wa_prof_prd-oil + wa_prof_prd-gas .
2444
2445       ENDCASE.
2446
2447       MODIFY it_prof_prd FROM wa_prof_prd TRANSPORTING oilng oil gas.
2448
2449     ENDLOOP.
2450
2451     PERFORM set_for_disp.
2452
2453   ENDFORM."CALC_0211

2488

2500
2501            in_tax_ong(10)   TYPE c,     "Income Tax (VIII) (20%)

2504

2507            net_atax_gas(10) TYPE c.

2511       CALL METHOD grid1->check_changed_data.
2512     ENDIF.
2513
2514     IF p_company IS NOT INITIAL
2515     AND p_comp IS NOT INITIAL
2516     AND p_proj IS NOT INITIAL
2517     AND p_year IS NOT INITIAL
2518     AND s_period_low IS NOT INITIAL.
2519
2520       SELECT SINGLE * FROM zpnl_prd_oil    "fetch the existing oil values
2521         INTO @DATA(chk_cre_oil)
2522         WHERE company = @p_company
2523           AND bukrs   = @p_comp
2524           AND pro_nam = @p_proj
2525           AND fisyear = @p_year
2526           AND zperiod = @s_period_low.
2527
2528       SELECT SINGLE * FROM zpnl_prd_gas   "fetch the existing gas values
2529         INTO @DATA(chk_cre_gas)
2530         WHERE company = @p_company
2531           AND bukrs   = @p_comp
2532           AND pro_nam = @p_proj
2533           AND fisyear = @p_year
2534           AND zperiod = @s_period_low.
2535
2536       IF chk_cre_gas IS NOT INITIAL AND chk_cre_oil IS NOT INITIAL.
2537         MOVE-CORRESPONDING chk_cre_oil TO wa_prd_oil.
2538         MOVE-CORRESPONDING chk_cre_gas TO wa_prd_gas.
2539       ENDIF.
2540
2541     ENDIF.
2542

2544     LOOP AT it_prof_prd INTO wa_prof_prd.   "Calculate the calculation field according to the changes
2545
2546       CASE sy-tabix.
2547         WHEN 1 ."GROSS PRODUCTION

2549           wa_prof_prd-oil   = wa_prd_oil-sh_prod .
2550           wa_prof_prd-gas   = wa_prd_gas-sh_prod .

2552
2553         WHEN 2 ."LESS: PROFIT OIL

2555           wa_prof_prd-oil = wa_prd_oil-prf_oil .
2556           wa_prof_prd-gas = wa_prd_gas-prf_oil .
2557           wa_prof_prd-oilng = wa_prof_prd-oil + wa_prof_prd-gas.
2558
2559         WHEN 3 ."OVLs ENTITLEMENT (AFTER PROFIT OIL))
2560
2561           wa_prof_prd-oil = wa_prd_oil-sh_entl .
2562           wa_prof_prd-gas = wa_prd_gas-sh_entl .
2563           wa_prof_prd-oilng  = wa_prof_prd-oil + wa_prof_prd-gas.
2564
2565         WHEN 4 ."ADD/(LESS):OVERLIFT QTY/(UNDER LIFT)
2566
2567           wa_prof_prd-oil = wa_prd_oil-ovr_und .
2568           wa_prof_prd-gas = wa_prd_gas-ovr_und .
2569           wa_prof_prd-oilng = wa_prof_prd-oil + wa_prof_prd-gas.
2570
2571         WHEN 5 ."ADD/(LESS): CHANGES IN INVENTORIES
2572
2573           wa_prof_prd-oil = wa_prd_oil-chg_inv .
2574           wa_prof_prd-gas = wa_prd_gas-chg_inv .
2575           wa_prof_prd-oilng = wa_prof_prd-oil + wa_prof_prd-gas.
2576

2730   FORM save_prof_prd.
2731
2732     IF NOT grid1 IS INITIAL.
2733       CALL METHOD grid1->check_changed_data.
2734     ENDIF.
2735
2736     PERFORM popup_save.
2737
2738     IF ( mode NE 'DIS' ) .
2739
2740       IF ans = '1'.
2741         IF wa_prd_oil IS NOT INITIAL AND wa_prd_gas IS NOT INITIAL.
2742
2743           IF mode = 'CRE' .
2744             wa_prd_oil-created_by = sy-uname.
2745             wa_prd_gas-created_by = sy-uname.
2746
2747             wa_prd_oil-created_on = sy-datum.
2748             wa_prd_gas-created_on = sy-datum.
2749
2750           ELSEIF mode = 'CHA'.
2751             wa_prd_oil-changed_by = sy-uname.
2752             wa_prd_gas-changed_by = sy-uname.
2753
2754             wa_prd_oil-changed_on = sy-datum.
2755             wa_prd_gas-changed_on = sy-datum.
2756
2757
2758             wa_prd_oil-approved_by = ' '.
2759             wa_prd_gas-approved_by = '00000000'.
2760
2761             wa_prd_oil-approved_on = ' '.
2762             wa_prd_gas-approved_on = '00000000'.
2763
2764           ENDIF.
2765
2766
2767           MODIFY zpnl_prd_oil FROM wa_prd_oil.
2768           MODIFY zpnl_prd_gas FROM wa_prd_gas.
2769
2770           MESSAGE 'Data saved successfully.' TYPE 'S'.
2771
2772           CLEAR : wa_prd_oil,
2773                   wa_prd_gas.
2774         ENDIF.
2775       ENDIF.
2776       CLEAR ans.
2777     ENDIF.
2778
2779
2780   ENDFORM."SAVE_PROF_PRD

2782   FORM app_prd_i.
2783
2784     IF mode EQ 'FRE'.
2785       IF wa_prd_oil-approved_by IS INITIAL AND wa_prd_gas-approved_by IS INITIAL.
2786         MESSAGE 'Data is not approved yet' TYPE 'E'.
2787       ENDIF.
2788     ENDIF.
2789
2790     PERFORM popup_save.
2791
2792     IF ans = '1'.
2793       IF mode = 'APP'.
2794         wa_prd_oil-approved_by = sy-uname.
2795         wa_prd_gas-approved_by = sy-uname.
2796
2797         wa_prd_oil-approved_on = sy-datum.
2798         wa_prd_gas-approved_on = sy-datum.
2799
2800         MODIFY zpnl_prd_oil FROM wa_prd_oil .
2801         MODIFY zpnl_prd_gas FROM wa_prd_gas .
2802
2803         stat = 'APPROVED'.
2804
2805         MESSAGE 'Data has been approved.' TYPE 'S'.
2806
2807         CLEAR : wa_prd_oil,
2808                 wa_prd_gas.
2809   ********      added by aparna on 03.07.2024
2810
2811       ELSEIF mode = 'APF'.
2812         wa_prd_oil-approved_by_rp = sy-uname.
2813         wa_prd_gas-approved_by_rp = sy-uname.
2814
2815         wa_prd_oil-approved_on_rp = sy-datum.
2816         wa_prd_gas-approved_on_rp = sy-datum.
2817
2818         MODIFY zpnl_prd_oil FROM wa_prd_oil .
2819         MODIFY zpnl_prd_gas FROM wa_prd_gas .
2820
2821         stat = 'APPROVED'.
2822
2823         MESSAGE 'Data has been approved.' TYPE 'S'.
2824
2825         CLEAR : wa_prd_oil,
2826                 wa_prd_gas.
2827   ********      ended by aparna on 03.07.2024
2828
2829       ELSEIF mode = 'FRE'.
2830         wa_prd_oil-freezed_by = sy-uname.
2831         wa_prd_gas-freezed_by = sy-uname.
2832
2833         wa_prd_oil-freezed_on = sy-datum.
2834         wa_prd_gas-freezed_on = sy-datum.
2835
2836         MODIFY zpnl_prd_oil FROM wa_prd_oil .
2837         MODIFY zpnl_prd_gas FROM wa_prd_gas .
2838
2839         stat = 'FREEZED'.
2840
2841         MESSAGE 'Data has been freezed. You cannot change this data now.' TYPE 'S'.
2842
2843         CLEAR : wa_prd_oil,
2844                 wa_prd_gas.
2845
2846       ENDIF.
2847     ENDIF.
2848
2849   ENDFORM."APP_PRD_I

3037   FORM calc_0311.           "calculate fetched/input data
3038
3039     IF NOT grid1 IS INITIAL.
3040       CALL METHOD grid1->check_changed_data.
3041     ENDIF.
3042
3043     wa_pnl_oth-company = p_company.
3044     wa_pnl_oth-company = p_company.
3045
3046     wa_pnl_oth-bukrs = p_comp.
3047     wa_pnl_oth-bukrs = p_comp.
3048
3049     wa_pnl_oth-pro_nam = p_proj.
3050     wa_pnl_oth-pro_nam = p_proj.
3051
3052     wa_pnl_oth-fisyear = p_year.
3053     wa_pnl_oth-fisyear = p_year.
3054
3055     wa_pnl_oth-zperiod = s_period_low.
3056     wa_pnl_oth-zperiod = s_period_low.
3057
3058     DATA : tot_inc(10)   TYPE c,
3059            tot_exp(10)   TYPE c,
3060            prof_btax(10) TYPE c,
3061            prof_atax(10) TYPE c.
3062
3063     LOOP AT it_oth_proj INTO wa_oth_proj.
3064
3065       IF wa_oth_proj-vals CA lv_spl AND sy-tabix NE 1.
3066         CLEAR sy-ucomm.
3067         MESSAGE 'Special character in amount field is not allowed' TYPE 'W' DISPLAY LIKE 'E'.
3068         EXIT.
3069       ENDIF.
3070
3071       CASE sy-tabix.
3072         WHEN 1.
3073
3074         WHEN 2.
3075           wa_pnl_oth-rev_opr = wa_oth_proj-vals.
3076         WHEN 3.
3077           wa_pnl_oth-oth_inc = wa_oth_proj-vals.
3078         WHEN 4.
3079           tot_inc = wa_oth_proj-vals = wa_pnl_oth-rev_opr + wa_pnl_oth-oth_inc.
3080         WHEN 5.
3081         WHEN 6.
3082           wa_pnl_oth-chg_inv = wa_oth_proj-vals.
3083         WHEN 7.
3084           wa_pnl_oth-ovr_und = wa_oth_proj-vals.
3085         WHEN 8.
3086           wa_pnl_oth-pts_de = wa_oth_proj-vals.
3087         WHEN 9.
3088           wa_pnl_oth-exp_cst = wa_oth_proj-vals.
3089         WHEN 10.
3090           wa_pnl_oth-fin_cst = wa_oth_proj-vals.
3091         WHEN 11.
3092           wa_pnl_oth-dda = wa_oth_proj-vals.
3093         WHEN 12.
3094           wa_pnl_oth-prov_of = wa_oth_proj-vals.
3095         WHEN 13.
3096           wa_pnl_oth-oth_exp = wa_oth_proj-vals.
3097         WHEN 14.
3098           tot_exp = wa_oth_proj-vals =  wa_pnl_oth-chg_inv + wa_pnl_oth-ovr_und
3099                                       + wa_pnl_oth-pts_de  + wa_pnl_oth-exp_cst
3100                                       + wa_pnl_oth-fin_cst + wa_pnl_oth-dda
3101                                       + wa_pnl_oth-prov_of + wa_pnl_oth-oth_exp.
3102
3103         WHEN 15.
3104         WHEN 16.
3105           prof_btax = wa_oth_proj-vals = tot_inc - tot_exp.
3106         WHEN 17.
3107         WHEN 18.
3108           wa_pnl_oth-inc_tax = wa_oth_proj-vals.
3109         WHEN 19.
3110         WHEN 20.
3111           wa_oth_proj-vals = prof_btax - wa_pnl_oth-inc_tax.
3112
3113       ENDCASE.
3114       MODIFY it_oth_proj FROM wa_oth_proj TRANSPORTING vals.
3115       CLEAR wa_oth_proj.
3116     ENDLOOP.
3117
3118
3119   ENDFORM."CALC_0311

3121   FORM change_oth_proj.    "get previously filled data
3122
3123     IF NOT grid1 IS INITIAL.
3124       CALL METHOD grid1->check_changed_data.
3125     ENDIF.
3126
3127     SELECT SINGLE * FROM zpnl_oth_proj
3128          INTO @DATA(chk_oth_proj)
3129          WHERE company = @p_company
3130            AND bukrs   = @p_comp
3131            AND pro_nam = @p_proj
3132            AND fisyear = @p_year
3133            AND zperiod = @s_period_low.
3134
3135     IF chk_oth_proj IS NOT INITIAL.
3136       MOVE-CORRESPONDING chk_oth_proj TO wa_pnl_oth.
3137     ENDIF.
3138
3139     DATA : tot_inc(10)   TYPE c,
3140            tot_exp(10)   TYPE c,
3141            prof_btax(10) TYPE c,
3142            prof_atax(10) TYPE c.
3143
3144     LOOP AT it_oth_proj INTO wa_oth_proj.
3145
3146       CASE sy-tabix.
3147         WHEN 1.
3148
3149         WHEN 2.
3150           wa_oth_proj-vals = wa_pnl_oth-rev_opr.
3151         WHEN 3.
3152           wa_oth_proj-vals = wa_pnl_oth-oth_inc.
3153         WHEN 4.
3154           tot_inc = wa_oth_proj-vals = wa_pnl_oth-rev_opr + wa_pnl_oth-oth_inc.
3155         WHEN 5.
3156         WHEN 6.
3157           wa_oth_proj-vals = wa_pnl_oth-chg_inv.
3158         WHEN 7.
3159           wa_oth_proj-vals = wa_pnl_oth-ovr_und.
3160         WHEN 8.
3161           wa_oth_proj-vals = wa_pnl_oth-pts_de .
3162         WHEN 9.
3163           wa_oth_proj-vals = wa_pnl_oth-exp_cst.
3164         WHEN 10.
3165           wa_oth_proj-vals = wa_pnl_oth-fin_cst.
3166         WHEN 11.
3167           wa_oth_proj-vals = wa_pnl_oth-dda    .
3168         WHEN 12.
3169           wa_oth_proj-vals = wa_pnl_oth-prov_of.
3170         WHEN 13.
3171           wa_oth_proj-vals = wa_pnl_oth-oth_exp.
3172         WHEN 14.
3173           tot_exp = wa_oth_proj-vals =  wa_pnl_oth-chg_inv + wa_pnl_oth-ovr_und
3174                                       + wa_pnl_oth-pts_de  + wa_pnl_oth-exp_cst
3175                                       + wa_pnl_oth-fin_cst + wa_pnl_oth-dda
3176                                       + wa_pnl_oth-prov_of + wa_pnl_oth-oth_exp.
3177
3178         WHEN 15.
3179         WHEN 16.
3180           prof_btax = wa_oth_proj-vals = tot_inc - tot_exp.
3181         WHEN 17.
3182         WHEN 18.
3183           wa_oth_proj-vals = wa_pnl_oth-inc_tax.
3184         WHEN 19.
3185         WHEN 20.
3186           wa_oth_proj-vals = prof_btax - wa_pnl_oth-inc_tax.
3187
3188       ENDCASE.
3189
3190       MODIFY it_oth_proj FROM wa_oth_proj TRANSPORTING vals.
3191       CLEAR wa_oth_proj.
3192
3193     ENDLOOP.
3194
3195   ENDFORM."CHANGE_OTH_PROJ

3197   FORM save_oth_proj.       "save data into database tables
3198
3199     IF NOT grid1 IS INITIAL.
3200       CALL METHOD grid1->check_changed_data.
3201     ENDIF.
3202
3203
3204     IF mode EQ 'FRE'.
3205       IF wa_pnl_oth-approve_by IS INITIAL AND wa_pnl_oth-approve_on IS INITIAL.
3206         MESSAGE 'Data is not approved yet' TYPE 'E'.
3207       ENDIF.
3208     ENDIF.
3209
3210     PERFORM popup_save.
3211
3212     IF ( mode NE 'DIS' ) .
3213       IF ans = '1'.
3214         IF wa_pnl_oth IS NOT INITIAL .
3215
3216           IF mode = 'CRE'.
3217             wa_pnl_oth-created_by = sy-uname.
3218             wa_pnl_oth-created_on = sy-datum.
3219
3220           ELSEIF mode = 'CHA'.
3221             wa_pnl_oth-changed_by = sy-uname.
3222             wa_pnl_oth-changed_on = sy-datum.
3223           ELSEIF mode = 'APP'.
3224             wa_pnl_oth-approve_by = sy-uname.
3225             wa_pnl_oth-approve_on = sy-datum.
3226           ELSEIF mode = 'FRE'.
3227             wa_pnl_oth-freezed_by = sy-uname.
3228             wa_pnl_oth-freezed_on = sy-datum.
3229
3230           ELSEIF mode = 'APF'.
3231             wa_pnl_oth-approved_by_rp = sy-uname.
3232             wa_pnl_oth-approved_on_rp = sy-datum.
3233           ENDIF.
3234
3235           MODIFY zpnl_oth_proj FROM wa_pnl_oth.
3236           CLEAR : wa_pnl_oth.
3237
3238           MESSAGE 'Data saved successfully.' TYPE 'S'.
3239
3240         ENDIF.
3241       ENDIF.
3242       CLEAR ans.
3243     ENDIF.
3244
3245   ENDFORM."SAVE_OTH_PROJ

3815     DATA lv_flag_ong TYPE c.
3816
3817     IF it_jvso1 IS NOT INITIAL.
3818
3819       LOOP AT it_gls1 INTO DATA(wa_gls1).
3820
3821   *      IF p_year1 LT '2023' AND wa_gls1-gl_acct = '0000200668'. "----------> Line Commented by Aparna on 22.07.2024
3822         IF p_year1 LT '2025' AND wa_gls1-gl_acct = '0000200668'.  "----------> Line Added     by Aparna on 22.07.2024
3823           wa_gls1-prd_pro = 'N/A'.
3824         ENDIF.
3825
3826
3827         LOOP AT it_jvso1 INTO DATA(wa_jvso1) WHERE racct = wa_gls1-gl_acct.
3828
3829   *Seperating revenue of Oil,Gas and Condensate accorging to their material code which are stored in single GL in database
3830
3831           IF wa_gls1-prd_pro EQ TEXT-103 OR wa_gls1-prd_pro EQ TEXT-115 OR wa_gls1-prd_pro EQ TEXT-117.    "Gl/s with description as Oil,
3832             IF wa_jvso1-matnr = lv_oil. "Crude Oil                                                        "-as Natural Gas & as Condensate
3833               lv_flag_ong = 'O'.
3834               lv_amt_o = lv_amt_o + wa_jvso1-ksl.
3835             ELSEIF wa_jvso1-matnr = lv_gas. "Natural Gas
3836               lv_flag_ong = 'G'.
3837               lv_amt_g = lv_amt_g + wa_jvso1-ksl.
3838             ELSEIF wa_gls1-gl_acct = '0000230116' AND wa_jvso1-matnr = lv_con. "Condensate
3839               lv_flag_ong = 'C'.
3840               lv_amt_c = lv_amt_c + wa_jvso1-ksl.
3841             ENDIF.
3842             lv_amt = lv_amt + wa_jvso1-ksl.
3843
3844   *Seperating Royalty of oil and gas from respt. gl to add it later in calculations
3845           ELSEIF wa_gls1-prd_pro EQ TEXT-102.     "Gl/s with description as Royalty
3846             CLEAR wa_setgls.
3847             READ TABLE it_setgls INTO wa_setgls WITH KEY valfrom = wa_gls1-gl_acct.
3848             IF wa_setgls-descript = TEXT-401. "Royalty-crude oil
3849               lv_flag_ong = 'O'.
3850               lv_amt_o = lv_amt_o + wa_jvso1-ksl."oil
3851             ELSEIF wa_setgls-descript = TEXT-402. "Royalty-natural gas
3852               lv_flag_ong = 'G'.
3853               lv_amt_g = lv_amt_g + wa_jvso1-ksl."gas
3854             ENDIF.
3855             lv_amt = lv_amt + wa_jvso1-ksl."total
3856
3857           ELSE.       "all other GLs
3858             lv_amt = lv_amt + wa_jvso1-ksl.
3859           ENDIF.
3860
3861           CLEAR wa_jvso1.
3862         ENDLOOP.
3863
3864         CONDENSE lv_amt NO-GAPS.
3865         CONDENSE lv_amt_o NO-GAPS.
3866         CONDENSE lv_amt_g NO-GAPS.
3867         CONDENSE lv_amt_c NO-GAPS.
3868
3869   *Inserting fetched values (IF the GL desc = Desc of a Row in Report).
3870   *Calculate all total and inserting the seperated oil and gas values for revenue as pre flag set in above code
3871         READ TABLE it_final_bar INTO wa_final_bar WITH KEY per = wa_gls1-prd_pro.
3872         IF wa_final_bar IS NOT INITIAL.
3873
3874           IF lv_flag_ong = 'O'.
3875             lv_amt_o = lv_amt_o / 1000000.
3876             wa_final_bar-oil1   = ( wa_final_bar-oil1 + lv_amt_o ).
3877             MODIFY it_final_bar FROM wa_final_bar TRANSPORTING oil1 WHERE per = wa_gls1-prd_pro.
3878           ELSEIF lv_flag_ong = 'G' .
3879             lv_amt_g = lv_amt_g / 1000000.
3880             wa_final_bar-gas1   = ( wa_final_bar-gas1 + lv_amt_g ).
3881             MODIFY it_final_bar FROM wa_final_bar TRANSPORTING gas1 WHERE per = wa_gls1-prd_pro.
3882           ENDIF.
3883
3884           IF wa_final_bar-gas1 IS NOT INITIAL OR wa_final_bar-oil1 IS NOT INITIAL.
3885   *          <<<<<<<<<<   added by aparna on 17/7/2024 on request of Rahul pauranik
3886             IF p_year1 GT '2022'.
3887
3888               "----------------------------------" Comment begins by Aparna on 10.08.2024 "------------------------------*
3889
3890   ****        wa_final_bar-total1 = (  wa_final_bar-total1 + wa_final_bar-oil1 + wa_final_bar-gas1 ).
3891               wa_final_bar-total1 = (  wa_final_bar-total1 + lv_amt_o + lv_amt_g ).
3892
3893               "----------------------------------" Comment ended by Aparna on 10.08.2024 "-------------------------------*
3894
3895               MODIFY it_final_bar FROM wa_final_bar TRANSPORTING total1 WHERE per = wa_gls1-prd_pro.
3896             ELSE.
3897               wa_final_bar-total1 = (   wa_final_bar-oil1 + wa_final_bar-gas1 ).
3898               MODIFY it_final_bar FROM wa_final_bar TRANSPORTING total1 WHERE per = wa_gls1-prd_pro.
3899             ENDIF.
3900           ELSE.
3901             IF lv_flag_ong = 'C' .
3902               lv_amt_c = lv_amt_c / 1000000.
3903               wa_final_bar-total1 = ( wa_final_bar-total1 + lv_amt_c ).
3904             ELSE.
3905               lv_amt = lv_amt / 1000000.
3906               wa_final_bar-total1 = ( wa_final_bar-total1 + lv_amt ).
3907             ENDIF.
3908
3909             READ TABLE it_setgls INTO wa_setgls WITH KEY valfrom = wa_gls1-gl_acct.
3910             IF wa_setgls-descript = TEXT-403 AND sy-subrc = 0.          "IMPAIRMENT LOSS/(REVERSAL)
3911               DATA(wa_final_bar_imp) = wa_final_bar.
3912               wa_final_bar_imp-total1 = lv_amt.
3913               MODIFY it_final_bar FROM wa_final_bar_imp TRANSPORTING total1 WHERE per = TEXT-403.
3914               CLEAR wa_final_bar_imp.
3915             ENDIF.
3916             CLEAR wa_setgls.
3917
3918             MODIFY it_final_bar FROM wa_final_bar TRANSPORTING total1 WHERE per = wa_gls1-prd_pro.
3919           ENDIF.
3920
3921         ENDIF.
3922
3923         CLEAR : wa_final_bar,lv_amt,wa_gls1,lv_amt_o,lv_amt_g,lv_flag_ong,lv_amt_c.
3924
3925       ENDLOOP.
3926     ENDIF.

3929     IF it_jvso2 IS NOT INITIAL.
3930       LOOP AT it_gls2 INTO DATA(wa_gls2).
3931
3932   *      IF p_year1 LT '2023' AND wa_gls1-gl_acct = '0000200668'. "----------> Line Commented by Aparna on 22.07.2024
3933         IF p_year2 LT '2025' AND wa_gls2-gl_acct = '0000200668'.  "----------> Line Added     by Aparna on 22.07.2024
3934           wa_gls2-prd_pro = 'N/A'.
3935         ENDIF.

3938
3939           IF wa_gls2-prd_pro EQ TEXT-103 OR wa_gls2-prd_pro EQ TEXT-115 OR wa_gls2-prd_pro EQ TEXT-117.
3940             IF wa_jvso2-matnr = lv_oil.
3941               lv_flag_ong = 'O'.
3942               lv_amt_o = lv_amt_o + wa_jvso2-ksl.
3943             ELSEIF  wa_jvso2-matnr = lv_gas.
3944               lv_flag_ong = 'G'.
3945               lv_amt_g = lv_amt_g + wa_jvso2-ksl.
3946             ELSEIF wa_gls2-gl_acct = '0000230116' AND wa_jvso2-matnr = lv_con. "Condensate
3947               lv_flag_ong = 'C'.
3948               lv_amt_c = lv_amt_c + wa_jvso2-ksl.
3949             ENDIF.
3950             lv_amt = lv_amt + wa_jvso2-ksl.
3951           ELSEIF wa_gls2-prd_pro EQ TEXT-102.
3952             CLEAR wa_setgls.
3953             READ TABLE it_setgls INTO wa_setgls WITH KEY valfrom = wa_gls2-gl_acct.
3954             IF wa_setgls-descript = TEXT-401. "Royalty-crude oil
3955               lv_flag_ong = 'O'.
3956               lv_amt_o = lv_amt_o + wa_jvso2-ksl.
3957             ELSEIF wa_setgls-descript = TEXT-402. "Royalty-natural gas.
3958               lv_flag_ong = 'G'.
3959               lv_amt_g = lv_amt_g + wa_jvso2-ksl.
3960             ENDIF.
3961             lv_amt = lv_amt + wa_jvso2-ksl.
3962           ELSE.
3963             lv_amt = lv_amt + wa_jvso2-ksl.
3964           ENDIF.

3966           CLEAR wa_jvso2.
3967         ENDLOOP.

4973             wa_final_bar-total4 = wa_manual_oil4-oth_fin + wa_manual_gas4-oth_fin.
4974             wa_final_bar-oil4   = wa_manual_oil4-oth_fin.
4975             wa_final_bar-gas4   = wa_manual_gas4-oth_fin.
4976
4977
4978           WHEN 35."Provisions, Write off and Other Impairment
4979             wa_final_bar-total1 = wa_manual_oil1-oth_prv + wa_manual_gas1-oth_prv.
4980             wa_final_bar-oil1   = wa_manual_oil1-oth_prv.
4981             wa_final_bar-gas1   = wa_manual_gas1-oth_prv.

4984             wa_final_bar-oil2   = wa_manual_oil2-oth_prv.
4985             wa_final_bar-gas2   = wa_manual_gas2-oth_prv.

4988             wa_final_bar-oil3   = wa_manual_oil3-oth_prv.
4989             wa_final_bar-gas3   = wa_manual_gas3-oth_prv.
4990
4991             wa_final_bar-total4 = wa_manual_oil4-oth_prv + wa_manual_gas4-oth_prv.
4992             wa_final_bar-oil4   = wa_manual_oil4-oth_prv.
4993             wa_final_bar-gas4   = wa_manual_gas4-oth_prv.
4994
4995
4996           WHEN 36."Other Expenses (incl. exchange fluctuation)
4997             wa_final_bar-total1 = wa_manual_oil1-oth_exp + wa_manual_gas1-oth_exp.
4998             wa_final_bar-oil1   = wa_manual_oil1-oth_exp.
4999             wa_final_bar-gas1   = wa_manual_gas1-oth_exp.

5001             wa_final_bar-total2 = wa_manual_oil2-oth_exp + wa_manual_gas2-oth_exp.
5002             wa_final_bar-oil2   = wa_manual_oil2-oth_exp.
5003             wa_final_bar-gas2   = wa_manual_gas2-oth_exp.
5004

5006             wa_final_bar-oil3   = wa_manual_oil3-oth_exp.
5007             wa_final_bar-gas3   = wa_manual_gas3-oth_exp.

5016             wa_final_bar-oil1   = wa_manual_oil1-inc_tax.
5017             wa_final_bar-gas1   = wa_manual_gas1-inc_tax.

5020             wa_final_bar-oil2   = wa_manual_oil2-inc_tax.
5021             wa_final_bar-gas2   = wa_manual_gas2-inc_tax.

5029             wa_final_bar-gas4   = wa_manual_gas4-inc_tax.

5033             wa_final_bar-total1 = wa_manual_oil1-imploss + wa_manual_gas1-imploss.
5034             wa_final_bar-oil1   = wa_manual_oil1-imploss.
5035             wa_final_bar-gas1   = wa_manual_gas1-imploss.
5036

5038             wa_final_bar-oil2   = wa_manual_oil2-imploss.
5039             wa_final_bar-gas2   = wa_manual_gas2-imploss.
5040

5042             wa_final_bar-oil3   = wa_manual_oil3-imploss.
5043             wa_final_bar-gas3   = wa_manual_gas3-imploss.

5046             wa_final_bar-oil4   = wa_manual_oil4-imploss.
5047             wa_final_bar-gas4   = wa_manual_gas4-imploss.

5049         ENDCASE.
5050
5051         MODIFY it_final_bar FROM wa_final_bar TRANSPORTING total1 total2 total3 total4"TOTAL1 OIL1 GAS1."TOTAL2 TOTAL3.
5052                                                            oil1   oil2   oil3   oil4
5053                                                            gas1   gas2   gas3   gas4.

5055       ENDLOOP.
5056     ENDIF.

6805               wa_final_bar-gas1 = wa_final_bar-total1.
6806               wa_final_bar-gas2 = wa_final_bar-total2.
6807               wa_final_bar-gas3 = wa_final_bar-total3.
6808               wa_final_bar-gas4 = wa_final_bar-total4.
6809
6810               MODIFY it_final_bar FROM wa_final_bar TRANSPORTING gas1 gas2 gas3 gas4.
6811
6812             ENDIF.
6813           ENDIF.
6814
6815           tot_otin_oil1 = tot_otin_oil1 + wa_final_bar-oil1 .
6816           tot_otin_oil2 = tot_otin_oil2 + wa_final_bar-oil2 .
6817           tot_otin_oil3 = tot_otin_oil3 + wa_final_bar-oil3 .
6818           tot_otin_oil4 = tot_otin_oil4 + wa_final_bar-oil4 .
6819
6820           tot_otin_gas1 = tot_otin_gas1 + wa_final_bar-gas1 .
6821           tot_otin_gas2 = tot_otin_gas2 + wa_final_bar-gas2 .
6822           tot_otin_gas3 = tot_otin_gas3 + wa_final_bar-gas3 .
6823           tot_otin_gas4 = tot_otin_gas4 + wa_final_bar-gas4 .
6824
6825         WHEN 37."Total Other Income/ Expense (X)
6826           wa_final_bar-total1 = tot_otin_ong1 .
6827           wa_final_bar-total2 = tot_otin_ong2 .
6828           wa_final_bar-total3 = tot_otin_ong3 .
6829           wa_final_bar-total4 = tot_otin_ong4 .
6830
6831           wa_final_bar-oil1 = tot_otin_oil1 .
6832           wa_final_bar-oil2 = tot_otin_oil2 .
6833           wa_final_bar-oil3 = tot_otin_oil3 .
6834           wa_final_bar-oil4 = tot_otin_oil4 .
6835
6836           wa_final_bar-gas1 = tot_otin_gas1 .
6837           wa_final_bar-gas2 = tot_otin_gas2 .
6838           wa_final_bar-gas3 = tot_otin_gas3 .
6839           wa_final_bar-gas4 = tot_otin_gas4 .
6840
6841           MODIFY it_final_bar FROM wa_final_bar TRANSPORTING total1 total2 total3 total4
6842                                                               oil1   oil2   oil3   oil4
6843                                                               gas1   gas2   gas3   gas4.
6844           CLEAR wa_final_bar.
6845
6846
6847         WHEN 39."Net Profit Before Tax X (VIII-IX)
6848           net_btax_ong1 = wa_final_bar-total1 = net_rev_ong1 - tot_otin_ong1 .
6849           net_btax_ong2 = wa_final_bar-total2 = net_rev_ong2 - tot_otin_ong2 .
6850           net_btax_ong3 = wa_final_bar-total3 = net_rev_ong3 - tot_otin_ong3 .
6851           net_btax_ong4 = wa_final_bar-total4 = net_rev_ong4 - tot_otin_ong4 .
6852
6853           net_btax_oil1 = wa_final_bar-oil1 = net_rev_oil1 - tot_otin_oil1 .
6854           net_btax_oil2 = wa_final_bar-oil2 = net_rev_oil2 - tot_otin_oil2 .
6855           net_btax_oil3 = wa_final_bar-oil3 = net_rev_oil3 - tot_otin_oil3 .
6856           net_btax_oil4 = wa_final_bar-oil4 = net_rev_oil4 - tot_otin_oil4 .
6857
6858           net_btax_gas1 = wa_final_bar-gas1 = net_rev_gas1 - tot_otin_gas1 .
6859           net_btax_gas2 = wa_final_bar-gas2 = net_rev_gas2 - tot_otin_gas2 .
6860           net_btax_gas3 = wa_final_bar-gas3 = net_rev_gas3 - tot_otin_gas3 .
6861           net_btax_gas4 = wa_final_bar-gas4 = net_rev_gas4 - tot_otin_gas4 .
6862
6863           MODIFY it_final_bar FROM wa_final_bar TRANSPORTING total1 total2 total3 total4
6864                                                               oil1   oil2   oil3   oil4
6865                                                               gas1   gas2   gas3   gas4.
6866           CLEAR wa_final_bar.
6867
6868
6869         WHEN 41."Income Tax (XI)
6870           IF manual NE 'X'.
6871             IF assoct = '3' OR assoct = '5'. "Oil Field with Associated Gas OR Gas Field with Condensate
6872               IF wa_final_bar2 IS NOT INITIAL AND wa_final_bar3 IS NOT INITIAL.
6873
6874                 IF wa_final_bar2-total1 GT wa_final_bar3-total1.
6875                   wa_final_bar-oil1 = wa_final_bar-total1 .
6876                 ELSE.
6877                   wa_final_bar-gas1 = wa_final_bar-total1 .
6878                 ENDIF.
6879
6880                 IF wa_final_bar2-total2 GT wa_final_bar3-total2.
6881                   wa_final_bar-oil2 = wa_final_bar-total2 .
6882                 ELSE.
6883                   wa_final_bar-gas2 = wa_final_bar-total2 .
6884                 ENDIF.
6885
6886                 IF wa_final_bar2-total3 GT wa_final_bar3-total3.
6887                   wa_final_bar-oil3 = wa_final_bar-total3 .
6888                 ELSE.
6889                   wa_final_bar-gas3 = wa_final_bar-total3 .
6890                 ENDIF.
6891
6892                 IF wa_final_bar2-total4 GT wa_final_bar3-total4.
6893                   wa_final_bar-oil4 = wa_final_bar-total4 .
6894                 ELSE.
6895                   wa_final_bar-gas4 = wa_final_bar-total4 .
6896                 ENDIF.
6897
6898               ENDIF.
6899
6900             ELSEIF assoct = '4'.  "Oil Field with Non-associated Gas
6901
6902               wa_final_bar-oil1   = ( wa_final_bar-total1 / ( net_btax_oil1 + net_btax_gas1 ) ) * ( net_btax_oil1 ) .
6903               wa_final_bar-oil2   = ( wa_final_bar-total2 / ( net_btax_oil2 + net_btax_gas2 ) ) * ( net_btax_oil2 ) .
6904               wa_final_bar-oil3   = ( wa_final_bar-total3 / ( net_btax_oil3 + net_btax_gas3 ) ) * ( net_btax_oil3 ) .
6905               wa_final_bar-oil4   = ( wa_final_bar-total4 / ( net_btax_oil4 + net_btax_gas4 ) ) * ( net_btax_oil4 ) .
6906
6907               wa_final_bar-oil1 = round( val = wa_final_bar-oil1 dec = '2' ) .
6908               wa_final_bar-oil2 = round( val = wa_final_bar-oil2 dec = '2' ) .
6909               wa_final_bar-oil3 = round( val = wa_final_bar-oil3 dec = '2' ) .
6910               wa_final_bar-oil4 = round( val = wa_final_bar-oil4 dec = '2' ) .
6911
6912               wa_final_bar-gas1   = wa_final_bar-total1 - wa_final_bar-oil1.
6913               wa_final_bar-gas2   = wa_final_bar-total2 - wa_final_bar-oil2.
6914               wa_final_bar-gas3   = wa_final_bar-total3 - wa_final_bar-oil3.
6915               wa_final_bar-gas4   = wa_final_bar-total4 - wa_final_bar-oil4.
6916
6917             ELSEIF assoct = '1'.
6918
6919               wa_final_bar-oil1 = wa_final_bar-total1.
6920               wa_final_bar-oil2 = wa_final_bar-total2.
6921               wa_final_bar-oil3 = wa_final_bar-total3.
6922               wa_final_bar-oil4 = wa_final_bar-total4.
6923
6924             ELSEIF assoct = '2'.
6925
6926               wa_final_bar-gas1 = wa_final_bar-total1.
6927               wa_final_bar-gas2 = wa_final_bar-total2.
6928               wa_final_bar-gas3 = wa_final_bar-total3.
6929               wa_final_bar-gas4 = wa_final_bar-total4.
6930
6931             ENDIF.
6932
6933             MODIFY it_final_bar FROM wa_final_bar TRANSPORTING total1 total2 total3 total4
6934                                                               oil1   oil2   oil3   oil4
6935                                                               gas1   gas2   gas3   gas4.
6936           ENDIF.
6937
6938           in_tax_ong1 = wa_final_bar-total1 .
6939           in_tax_ong2 = wa_final_bar-total2 .
6940           in_tax_ong3 = wa_final_bar-total3 .
6941           in_tax_ong4 = wa_final_bar-total4 .
6942
6943           in_tax_oil1 = wa_final_bar-oil1 .
6944           in_tax_oil2 = wa_final_bar-oil2 .
6945           in_tax_oil3 = wa_final_bar-oil3 .
6946           in_tax_oil4 = wa_final_bar-oil4 .
6947
6948           in_tax_gas1 = wa_final_bar-gas1 .
6949           in_tax_gas2 = wa_final_bar-gas2 .
6950           in_tax_gas3 = wa_final_bar-gas3 .
6951           in_tax_gas4 = wa_final_bar-gas4 .
6952
6953           CLEAR wa_final_bar.
6954
6955
6956         WHEN 43."Net Profit/(Loss) after tax XII (X-XI)
6957           wa_final_bar-total1 = net_atax_ong1 = net_btax_ong1 - in_tax_ong1  .
6958           wa_final_bar-total2 = net_atax_ong2 = net_btax_ong2 - in_tax_ong2  .
6959           wa_final_bar-total3 = net_atax_ong3 = net_btax_ong3 - in_tax_ong3  .
6960           wa_final_bar-total4 = net_atax_ong4 = net_btax_ong4 - in_tax_ong4  .
6961
6962           wa_final_bar-oil1 = net_atax_oil1 = net_btax_oil1 - in_tax_oil1  .
6963           wa_final_bar-oil2 = net_atax_oil2 = net_btax_oil2 - in_tax_oil2  .
6964           wa_final_bar-oil3 = net_atax_oil3 = net_btax_oil3 - in_tax_oil3  .
6965           wa_final_bar-oil4 = net_atax_oil4 = net_btax_oil4 - in_tax_oil4  .
6966
6967           wa_final_bar-gas1 = net_atax_gas1 = net_btax_gas1 - in_tax_gas1  .
6968           wa_final_bar-gas2 = net_atax_gas2 = net_btax_gas2 - in_tax_gas2  .
6969           wa_final_bar-gas3 = net_atax_gas3 = net_btax_gas3 - in_tax_gas3  .
6970           wa_final_bar-gas4 = net_atax_gas4 = net_btax_gas4 - in_tax_gas4  .
6971
6972           MODIFY it_final_bar FROM wa_final_bar TRANSPORTING total1 total2 total3 total4
6973                                                               oil1   oil2   oil3   oil4
6974                                                               gas1   gas2   gas3   gas4.
6975           CLEAR wa_final_bar.
6976
6977
6978         WHEN 45."Impairment loss/(reversal)
6979           imploss_ong1 = wa_final_bar-total1 .
6980           imploss_ong2 = wa_final_bar-total2 .
6981           imploss_ong3 = wa_final_bar-total3 .
6982           imploss_ong4 = wa_final_bar-total4 .
6983
6984           IF manual NE 'X'.
6985             IF assoct = '1'.
6986
6987               imploss_oil1 = wa_final_bar-oil1 = wa_final_bar-total1.
6988               imploss_oil2 = wa_final_bar-oil2 = wa_final_bar-total2.
6989               imploss_oil3 = wa_final_bar-oil3 = wa_final_bar-total3.
6990               imploss_oil4 = wa_final_bar-oil4 = wa_final_bar-total4.
6991
6992             ELSEIF assoct = '2'.
6993
6994               imploss_gas1 = wa_final_bar-gas1 = wa_final_bar-total1.
6995               imploss_gas2 = wa_final_bar-gas2 = wa_final_bar-total2.
6996               imploss_gas3 = wa_final_bar-gas3 = wa_final_bar-total3.
6997               imploss_gas4 = wa_final_bar-gas4 = wa_final_bar-total4.
6998
6999             ENDIF.
7000
7001             MODIFY it_final_bar FROM wa_final_bar TRANSPORTING total1 total2 total3 total4
7002                                                                 oil1   oil2   oil3   oil4
7003                                                                 gas1   gas2   gas3   gas4.
7004           ENDIF.
7005
7006
7007         WHEN 47."Net Profit/(Loss) After Tax-before Impairment
7008           wa_final_bar-total1 = net_atax_ong1 + imploss_ong1 .
7009           wa_final_bar-total2 = net_atax_ong2 + imploss_ong2 .
7010           wa_final_bar-total3 = net_atax_ong3 + imploss_ong3 .
7011           wa_final_bar-total4 = net_atax_ong4 + imploss_ong4 .
7012
7013           wa_final_bar-oil1 = net_atax_oil1 + imploss_oil1 .
7014           wa_final_bar-oil2 = net_atax_oil2 + imploss_oil2 .
7015           wa_final_bar-oil3 = net_atax_oil3 + imploss_oil3 .
7016           wa_final_bar-oil4 = net_atax_oil4 + imploss_oil4 .
7017
7018           wa_final_bar-gas1 = net_atax_gas1 + imploss_gas1 .
7019           wa_final_bar-gas2 = net_atax_gas2 + imploss_gas2 .
7020           wa_final_bar-gas3 = net_atax_gas3 + imploss_gas3 .
7021           wa_final_bar-gas4 = net_atax_gas4 + imploss_gas4 .
7022
7023           MODIFY it_final_bar FROM wa_final_bar TRANSPORTING total1 total2 total3 total4
7024                                                               oil1   oil2   oil3   oil4
7025                                                               gas1   gas2   gas3   gas4.
7026           CLEAR wa_final_bar.
7027
7028       ENDCASE.
7029
7030     ENDLOOP.
7031
7032     CLEAR : wa_final_bar2, wa_final_bar3.
7033
7034   *Rounding off every amount to 2 decimals
7035     LOOP AT it_final_bar INTO wa_final_bar.
7036
7037       wa_final_bar1-per = wa_final_bar-per.
7038
7039       wa_final_bar1-total1 = round( val = wa_final_bar-total1 dec = '2' ) .
7040       wa_final_bar1-total2 = round( val = wa_final_bar-total2 dec = '2' ) .
7041       wa_final_bar1-total3 = round( val = wa_final_bar-total3 dec = '2' ) .
7042       wa_final_bar1-total4 = round( val = wa_final_bar-total4 dec = '2' ) .
7043
7044       wa_final_bar1-oil1 = round( val = wa_final_bar-oil1 dec = '2' ) .
7045       wa_final_bar1-oil2 = round( val = wa_final_bar-oil2 dec = '2' ) .
7046       wa_final_bar1-oil3 = round( val = wa_final_bar-oil3 dec = '2' ) .
7047       wa_final_bar1-oil4 = round( val = wa_final_bar-oil4 dec = '2' ) .
7048
7049       wa_final_bar1-gas1 = round( val = wa_final_bar-gas1 dec = '2' ) .
7050       wa_final_bar1-gas2 = round( val = wa_final_bar-gas2 dec = '2' ) .
7051       wa_final_bar1-gas3 = round( val = wa_final_bar-gas3 dec = '2' ) .
7052       wa_final_bar1-gas4 = round( val = wa_final_bar-gas4 dec = '2' ) .
7053
7054   *-------------------" Changes begins by Aparna on 11.02.2023 "----------------------*
7055   *BREAK-POINT.
7056       SELECT SINGLE *
7057       FROM   zfi_pl_var_prd
7058       INTO   @DATA(wa_pl_var_prd)
7059              WHERE per    = @wa_final_bar1-per
7060              AND comp = @p_comp1
7061              AND   period = @s_period-low
7062         AND period2 = @s_period_low2
7063         AND fiscal = @p_year1
7064         AND proj = @p_proj1-low.
7065       IF sy-subrc = 0.
7066
7067         wa_final_bar1-remark = wa_pl_var_prd-remark.
7068
7069       ENDIF.
7070   *-------------------" Changes ended by Aparna on 11.02.2023 "-----------------------*
7071
7072       APPEND wa_final_bar1 TO it_final_bar1.
7073
7074       CLEAR: wa_final_bar,wa_final_bar1.
7075
7076     ENDLOOP.
7077
7078   ENDFORM."CRE_PRD_PROJ.

7099                                     'OIL2'      'Crude Oil',
7100                                     'GAS2'      'Gas'.

7110                                     'GAS3'      'Gas'.
7111     ENDIF.
7112
7113     IF  p_year4 IS NOT INITIAL.

7164   FORM top_of_page_bar.
7165
7166     REFRESH int_header[].
7167     fs_header-typ = 'H'.
7168     fs_header-info = 'Profitability Statement for Producing Project'.
7169     APPEND fs_header TO int_header.
7170     CLEAR fs_header.
7171
7172     SELECT txtcc bukrs FROM zfi_pnl_proj INTO TABLE it_comp
7173                          WHERE bukrs = p_comp1.
7174
7175     READ TABLE it_comp INTO DATA(wa_comp) WITH KEY bukrs = p_comp1.
7176     fs_header-typ = 'S'.
7177     CONCATENATE 'Company code :- ' wa_comp-bukrs ' - ' wa_comp-txtcc INTO fs_header-info SEPARATED BY space.
7178     APPEND fs_header TO int_header.
7179     CLEAR fs_header.
7180
7181     SELECT txtjv jvnam FROM zfi_pnl_proj INTO TABLE it_proj
7182                          WHERE jvnam = p_proj1-low.
7183
7184     READ TABLE it_proj INTO DATA(wa_proj) WITH KEY jvnam = p_proj1-low.
7185     fs_header-typ = 'S'.
7186     CONCATENATE 'Project :- ' wa_proj-jvnam ' - ' wa_proj-txtjv  INTO fs_header-info SEPARATED BY space.
7187     APPEND fs_header TO int_header.
7188     CLEAR fs_header.
7189
7190     fs_header-typ = 'S'.
7191     APPEND fs_header TO int_header.
7192     CLEAR fs_header.
7193
7194     fs_header-typ = 'S'.
7195     CONCATENATE '(Amount in' 'US$ Mn.)' INTO fs_header-info SEPARATED BY space.
7196     APPEND fs_header TO int_header.
7197     CLEAR fs_header.
7198
7199     CALL FUNCTION 'REUSE_ALV_COMMENTARY_WRITE'
7200       EXPORTING
7201         it_list_commentary = int_header.
7202
7203   ENDFORM."TOP_OF_PAGE_BAR

7205   FORM map_period USING VALUE(per_low).   "Get periods according to input parameters to pass in database tables
7206     REFRESH s_peri[].
7207     CLEAR : s_period_low,s_period_high,s_peri.
7208
7209     s_peri-sign   = 'I'.
7210     s_peri-option = 'BT'.
7211

7213       s_period_low  = '01'.
7214       s_period_high = '03'.
7215     ELSEIF per_low = 'Q2'.
7216       s_period_low  = '04'.
7217       s_period_high = '06'.
7218     ELSEIF per_low = 'H1'.
7219       s_period_low  = '01'.
7220       s_period_high = '06'.
7221     ELSEIF per_low = 'Q3'.
7222       s_period_low  = '07'.
7223       s_period_high = '09'.
7224     ELSEIF per_low = '9M'.
7225       s_period_low  = '01'.
7226       s_period_high = '09'.
7227     ELSEIF per_low = 'Q4'.
7228       s_period_low  = '10'.
7229       s_period_high = '16'.
7230     ELSEIF per_low = 'ANNUAL'.
7231       s_period_low  = '01'.
7232       s_period_high = '16'.
7233     ENDIF.
7234
7235     s_peri-low  = s_period_low.
7236     s_peri-high = s_period_high.
7237
7238     APPEND s_peri.

7240   ENDFORM."MAP_PERIOD

7580     IF it_jvso1 IS NOT INITIAL.

7582       REFRESH lt_gl_oth[].

7585
7586       LOOP AT lt_gl_oth INTO DATA(wa_gls1).
7587
7588         LOOP AT it_jvso1 INTO DATA(wa_jvso1) WHERE racct = wa_gls1-gl_acct.
7589           lv_amt = lv_amt + wa_jvso1-ksl.
7590           CLEAR wa_jvso1.
7591         ENDLOOP.
7592
7593         CONDENSE lv_amt NO-GAPS.
7594
7595         AT END OF oth_pro.
7596           READ TABLE it_final_non INTO wa_final_non WITH KEY per = wa_gls1-oth_pro.
7597           IF wa_final_non IS NOT INITIAL.
7598             wa_final_non-val1 = ( wa_final_non-val1 + lv_amt ) / 1000000.
7599             MODIFY it_final_non FROM wa_final_non TRANSPORTING val1 WHERE per = wa_gls1-oth_pro.
7600           ENDIF.
7601           CLEAR : wa_final_non,lv_amt.
7602         ENDAT.
7603         CLEAR wa_gls1.
7604
7605       ENDLOOP.
7606     ENDIF.

8189   FORM top_of_page_non.
8190
8191     REFRESH int_header[].
8192     IF lv_non_profitabilty EQ 'X'.
8193       fs_header-typ = 'H'.
8194       fs_header-info = 'Profitability Statement for Non-Producing Project'.
8195       APPEND fs_header TO int_header.
8196       CLEAR fs_header.
8197     ELSE.
8198       fs_header-typ = 'H'.
8199       fs_header-info = 'Variance Analysis for Non-Producing Project'.
8200       APPEND fs_header TO int_header.
8201       CLEAR fs_header.
8202
8203
8204       fs_header-typ = 'S'.
8205       fs_header-info = '"FOR SAVING THE REMARKS, SELECT ALL CELLS AND THEN CLICK ON SAVE"'.
8206       APPEND fs_header TO int_header.
8207       CLEAR fs_header.
8208     ENDIF.
8209
8210
8211     SELECT txtcc bukrs FROM zfi_pnl_proj INTO TABLE it_comp
8212                          WHERE bukrs = p_comp1.
8213
8214     READ TABLE it_comp INTO DATA(wa_comp) WITH KEY bukrs = p_comp1.
8215     fs_header-typ = 'S'.
8216     CONCATENATE 'Company code :- ' wa_comp-bukrs ' - ' wa_comp-txtcc INTO fs_header-info SEPARATED BY space.
8217     APPEND fs_header TO int_header.
8218     CLEAR fs_header.
8219
8220     SELECT txtjv jvnam FROM zfi_pnl_proj INTO TABLE it_proj
8221                          WHERE jvnam = p_proj1-low.
8222
8223     READ TABLE it_proj INTO DATA(wa_proj) WITH KEY jvnam = p_proj1-low.
8224     fs_header-typ = 'S'.
8225     CONCATENATE 'Project :- ' wa_proj-jvnam ' - ' wa_proj-txtjv  INTO fs_header-info SEPARATED BY space.
8226     APPEND fs_header TO int_header.
8227     CLEAR fs_header.
8228
8229     fs_header-typ = 'S'.
8230     APPEND fs_header TO int_header.
8231     CLEAR fs_header.
8232
8233     fs_header-typ = 'S'.
8234     CONCATENATE '(Amount in' 'US$ Mn.)'  INTO fs_header-info SEPARATED BY space.
8235     APPEND fs_header TO int_header.
8236     CLEAR fs_header.
8237
8238     CALL FUNCTION 'REUSE_ALV_COMMENTARY_WRITE'
8239       EXPORTING
8240         it_list_commentary = int_header.
8241
8242   ENDFORM. "TOP_OF_PAGE_NON.

8244   FORM chk_before_save.   " Checking (1a+8-13=9a) and convo factor not blank
8245
8246   **********************************Checking (1a+8-13=9a)
8247
8248     DATA : lv_13_o(15) TYPE c,
8249            lv_13_g(15) TYPE c,
8250            lv_9a_o(15) TYPE c,
8251            lv_9a_g(15) TYPE c.
8252
8253
8254     READ TABLE it_quant INTO DATA(wa_1a) INDEX 3.
8255     READ TABLE it_quant INTO DATA(wa_8)  INDEX 25.
8256     READ TABLE it_quant INTO DATA(wa_9a) INDEX 28.
8257     READ TABLE it_quant INTO DATA(wa_13) INDEX 35.
8258     READ TABLE it_quant INTO DATA(wa_11) INDEX 31.
8259     IF wa_1a IS NOT INITIAL AND wa_8 IS NOT INITIAL AND wa_13 IS NOT INITIAL.
8260       lv_9a_o = ( wa_1a-mmbbl + wa_8-mmbbl ) - ( wa_13-mmbbl ).
8261       lv_9a_g = ( wa_1a-mmbtu + wa_8-mmbtu ) - ( wa_13-mmbtu ).
8262     ENDIF.
8263
8264     IF wa_9a IS NOT INITIAL AND lv_9a_o IS NOT INITIAL.
8265       IF wa_9a-mmbbl NE lv_9a_o.
8266         MESSAGE 'Closing Stock is not equal to (Opening Stock+Production-Sales) for Crude Oil' TYPE 'W'.
8267       ENDIF.
8268     ELSEIF wa_9a IS NOT INITIAL AND lv_9a_g IS NOT INITIAL.
8269       IF wa_9a-mmbtu NE lv_9a_g.
8270         MESSAGE 'Closing Stock is not equal to (Opening Stock+Production-Sales) for Natural Gas' TYPE 'W'.
8271       ENDIF.
8272     ENDIF.
8273
8274     IF wa_11 IS NOT INITIAL AND wa_13 IS NOT INITIAL.
8275       IF wa_13-mmscm NE wa_11-mmscm.
8276         MESSAGE 'Sales qty. should be equal to Net qty. available for sale (for Natural Gas)' TYPE 'E'.
8277       ENDIF.
8278     ENDIF.
8279   *********************************************************
8280
8281   **********************************Conversion factor check
8282     DATA lv_convo_chk(7) TYPE p DECIMALS 6 VALUE '0.000000'.
8283     IF ( wa_final_conv-bbl_mmbbl   EQ lv_convo_chk )
8284     OR ( wa_final_conv-bbl_mmt     EQ lv_convo_chk )
8285     OR ( wa_final_conv-mmscm_bcm   EQ lv_convo_chk )
8286     OR ( wa_final_conv-mmscm_mmboe EQ lv_convo_chk )
8287     OR ( wa_final_conv-mmscm_mmbtu EQ lv_convo_chk ).
8288
8289       MESSAGE 'Conversion factors cannot be blank' TYPE 'E'.
8290
8291     ENDIF.
8292
8293     CLEAR : wa_1a,wa_8,wa_9a,wa_13,wa_11,lv_13_o,lv_13_g,lv_9a_o,lv_9a_g.
8294   *********************************************************
8295   ENDFORM. "CHK_BEFORE_SAVE

8372   FORM check_gls.  "Check all GLs are maintained in GL table maintanence
8373
8374     TYPES : BEGIN OF ty_gl_msg,
8375               saknr TYPE saknr,
8376               txt50 TYPE txt50,
8377             END OF ty_gl_msg.
8378
8379     DATA : it_gl_msg TYPE STANDARD TABLE OF ty_gl_msg,
8380            wa_gl_msg TYPE ty_gl_msg.
8381     "Code Remediation changes S4 2025 Conversion Begin of change SAP_ABAP and 12.06.2026
8382
8383   *  SELECT saknr FROM ska1 INTO TABLE @DATA(it_chk_gl1) WHERE ktopl = 'ONGC' AND xbilk = ' ' AND xloev = ' '.
8384   *BOC By Arnav on 27/09/26
8385   * In S/4HANA secondary cost elements are G/L accounts (SKA1, account group
8386   * SECC, account type S). They are not balance-sheet accounts, so the check
8387   * below listed them as unmapped P&L G/Ls. ECC had no such SKA1 rows. The
8388   * OVL trial balance excludes them, so they are excluded from this check.
8389   * ASSUMPTION: I_GLAccountInChartOfAccounts exposes SKA1-KTOKS as GLAccountGroup.
8390   *  SELECT GLAccount               AS saknr
8391   *    FROM I_GLAccountInChartOfAccounts
8392   *    INTO TABLE @DATA(it_chk_gl1)
8393   *    WHERE ChartOfAccounts          = 'ONGC'
8394   *      AND IsBalanceSheetAccount    = ' '
8395   *      AND AccountIsMarkedForDeletion = ' '.
8396     SELECT GLAccount               AS saknr
8397       FROM I_GLAccountInChartOfAccounts
8398       INTO TABLE @DATA(it_chk_gl1)
8399       WHERE ChartOfAccounts          = 'ONGC'
8400         AND IsBalanceSheetAccount    = ' '
8401         AND AccountIsMarkedForDeletion = ' '
8402         AND GLAccountGroup          <> 'SECC'.
8403   *EOC By Arnav on 27/09/26
8404
8405     IF it_chk_gl1 IS NOT INITIAL.
8406   *    SELECT saknr FROM skb1 INTO TABLE @DATA(it_chk_gl2)
8407   *                  FOR ALL ENTRIES IN @it_chk_gl1 WHERE saknr = @it_chk_gl1-saknr AND bukrs = @p_comp1 AND xloeb = ' '.
8408       SELECT GLAccount               AS saknr
8409         FROM I_GLAccountInCompanyCode
8410         INTO TABLE @DATA(it_chk_gl2)
8411         FOR ALL ENTRIES IN @it_chk_gl1
8412         WHERE GLAccount              = @it_chk_gl1-saknr
8413           AND CompanyCode            = @p_comp1
8414           AND AccountIsBlockedForPosting = ' '.
8415       "Code Remediation changes S4 2025 Conversion End of change SAP_ABAP and 12.06.2026
8416       IF it_chk_gl2 IS NOT INITIAL.
8417         SELECT * FROM zfi_pnl_gl INTO TABLE @DATA(it_gls).
8418
8419         IF it_gls IS NOT INITIAL.
8420           LOOP AT it_gls  INTO DATA(wa_gls).
8421             DELETE it_chk_gl2 WHERE saknr = wa_gls-gl_acct.
8422           ENDLOOP.
8423         ENDIF.
8424       ENDIF.
8425     ENDIF.
8426
8427     IF it_chk_gl2 IS NOT INITIAL.
8428       REFRESH :it_gl_msg.
8429       LOOP AT it_chk_gl2 INTO DATA(wa_chk_gl2).
8430         wa_gl_msg-saknr = wa_chk_gl2-saknr.
8431         wa_gl_msg-txt50 = 'GL missing from GL Maintanence' .
8432         APPEND wa_gl_msg TO it_gl_msg.
8433         CLEAR : wa_gl_msg,wa_chk_gl2.
8434       ENDLOOP.
8435     ENDIF.
8436
8437     IF it_gl_msg IS NOT INITIAL.
8438       REFRESH : it_fcat.
8439       CLEAR gl_msg_lines.
8440
8441       gl_msg_lines = lines( it_gl_msg ).
8442
8443       wa_fcat-col_pos = '1' .
8444       wa_fcat-fieldname = 'SAKNR'.
8445       wa_fcat-tabname = 'IT_GL_MSG' .
8446       wa_fcat-seltext_m = 'GL Account' .
8447       wa_fcat-outputlen = '10'.
8448       APPEND wa_fcat TO it_fcat .
8449       CLEAR:wa_fcat.
8450
8451       wa_fcat-col_pos = '2' .
8452       wa_fcat-fieldname = 'TXT50'.
8453       wa_fcat-tabname = 'IT_GL_MSG' .
8454       wa_fcat-seltext_m = 'Error Message' .
8455       wa_fcat-outputlen = '50'.
8456       APPEND wa_fcat TO it_fcat .
8457       CLEAR:wa_fcat.
8458
8459
8460       CALL FUNCTION 'REUSE_ALV_GRID_DISPLAY'
8461         EXPORTING
8462           i_callback_program     = sy-repid
8463           i_callback_top_of_page = 'TOP_OF_PAGE_GL_MSG'
8464           it_fieldcat            = it_fcat
8465           i_default              = 'X'
8466           i_save                 = 'U'
8467         TABLES
8468           t_outtab               = it_gl_msg
8469         EXCEPTIONS
8470           program_error          = 1
8471           OTHERS                 = 2.
8472
8473     ELSE.
8474
8475       gl_msg_lines = '0'.
8476
8477     ENDIF.
8478
8479
8480   ENDFORM."CHECK_GLS.

8501   FORM cre_var_prd.    "Calculations for variance analysis producing projects
8502
8503     LOOP AT it_final_bar1 INTO wa_final_bar1.
8504
8505       wa_final_bar1-total3 = wa_final_bar1-total1 - wa_final_bar1-total2.
8506       wa_final_bar1-oil3   = wa_final_bar1-oil1   - wa_final_bar1-oil2.
8507       wa_final_bar1-gas3   = wa_final_bar1-gas1   - wa_final_bar1-gas2.
8508
8509       IF ( wa_final_bar1-total1 NE '0.00' )
8510         AND ( wa_final_bar1-total2 = '0.00' ).
8511
8512         wa_final_bar1-total4  = '100.00'.
8513         wa_final_bar1-oil4    = '100.00'.
8514         wa_final_bar1-gas4    = '100.00'.
8515
8516       ELSEIF ( wa_final_bar1-total1 = '0.00' )
8517         AND ( wa_final_bar1-total2 NE '0.00' ).
8518
8519         wa_final_bar1-total4  = '100.00-'.
8520         wa_final_bar1-oil4    = '100.00-'.
8521         wa_final_bar1-gas4    = '100.00-'.
8522
8523       ELSE.
8524
8525         IF wa_final_bar1-total2 = '0.00'.
8526           wa_final_bar1-total2 = wa_final_bar1-total3.
8527         ENDIF.
8528         IF wa_final_bar1-oil2 = '0.00'.
8529           wa_final_bar1-oil2 = wa_final_bar1-total3.
8530         ENDIF.
8531         IF wa_final_bar1-gas2 = '0.00'.
8532           wa_final_bar1-gas2 = wa_final_bar1-gas3.
8533         ENDIF.
8534
8535         wa_final_bar1-total4 = ( wa_final_bar1-total3 / wa_final_bar1-total2  ) * 100.
8536         wa_final_bar1-oil4   = ( wa_final_bar1-oil3   / wa_final_bar1-oil2    ) * 100.
8537         wa_final_bar1-gas4   = ( wa_final_bar1-gas3   / wa_final_bar1-gas2    ) * 100.
8538
8539       ENDIF.
8540
8541
8542       wa_final_bar1-total3 = round( val = wa_final_bar1-total3 dec = '2' ) .
8543       wa_final_bar1-oil3   = round( val = wa_final_bar1-oil3 dec = '2' ) .
8544       wa_final_bar1-gas3   = round( val = wa_final_bar1-gas3 dec = '2' ) .
8545
8546       wa_final_bar1-total4 = round( val = wa_final_bar1-total4 dec = '2' ) .
8547       wa_final_bar1-oil4   = round( val = wa_final_bar1-oil4 dec = '2' ) .
8548       wa_final_bar1-gas4   = round( val = wa_final_bar1-gas4 dec = '2' ) .
8549
8550
8551       MODIFY it_final_bar1 FROM wa_final_bar1 TRANSPORTING total3 oil3 gas3
8552                                                            total4 oil4 gas4.
8553       CLEAR wa_final_bar1.
8554
8555     ENDLOOP.
8556
8557   ENDFORM."CRE_VAR_PRD.

8771   FORM var_prd_cell_disable .
8772   *  break abapuser02.
8773     LOOP AT it_final_bar1 INTO DATA(wa_bar1).
8774       IF wa_bar1-per = 'OPREATING EXPENSE :' OR wa_bar1-per = '' OR
8775        wa_bar1-per = 'DDA & EXPLORATION :' OR wa_bar1-per = 'ADJUSTMENT FOR COST OF SALES:' OR
8776        wa_bar1-per = 'OTHER INCOME/ EXPENSE :' OR wa_bar1-per = '(I) SALES REALISATION' OR wa_bar1-per = '(II) TOTAL OPERATING EXPENSES' OR
8777        wa_bar1-per = '(IV) OPERATING COST' OR wa_bar1-per = '(III) TOTAL DDA & EXPLORATION' OR
8778        wa_bar1-per = '(VII) COST OF GOODS SOLD (IV+V+VI)' OR wa_bar1-per = '(VIII) NET OPERATING MARGIN (I-VII)'

8904     DATA : wa_ahead LIKE thead,

8916     ENDIF.

8942   *                  WITH sy-msgv1 sy-msgv2 sy-msgv3 sy-msgv4.
8943         ENDIF.

8948   *           STREAM_LINES       =
8949               language    = sy-langu

8951             TABLES

8953               itf_text    = it_ltext.
8954         ENDIF.

8957             EXPORTING

8967               language        = 2
8968               name            = 3
8969               object          = 4
8970               OTHERS          = 5.

8972   * Implement suitable error handling here
8973           ENDIF.
8974
8975         ENDIF.

8978         MODIFY it_final_bar1 FROM wa_final_bar1 TRANSPORTING remark WHERE sel = 'X'.
8979         IF sy-subrc = 0.

8981             IMPORTING
8982               e_grid = l_grid.
8983
8984   *        l_grid->check_changed_data(

9012   FORM save_remarks .
9013     BREAK abapuser02.
9014     DATA(it_final_bar_temp) = it_final_bar1[].
9015     DELETE it_final_bar_temp WHERE per = 'OPREATING EXPENSE :'.
9016     DELETE it_final_bar_temp WHERE per = 'DDA & EXPLORATION :'.
9017     DELETE it_final_bar_temp WHERE per = 'ADJUSTMENT FOR COST OF SALES:'.
9018     DELETE it_final_bar_temp WHERE per = 'OTHER INCOME/ EXPENSE :' .
9019     "Begin of Changes     "Madhu
9020     DELETE it_final_bar_temp WHERE per = '(I) SALES REALISATION' .
9021     DELETE it_final_bar_temp WHERE per = '(II) TOTAL OPERATING EXPENSES' .
9022     DELETE it_final_bar_temp WHERE per = '(III) TOTAL DDA & EXPLORATION' .
9023     DELETE it_final_bar_temp WHERE per = '(IV) OPERATING COST' .
9024     DELETE it_final_bar_temp WHERE per = '(VII) COST OF GOODS SOLD (IV+V+VI)' .
9025     DELETE it_final_bar_temp WHERE per = '(VIII) NET OPERATING MARGIN (I-VII)' .
9026     DELETE it_final_bar_temp WHERE per = '(IX) TOTAL OTHER INCOME/ EXPENSE' .
9027     DELETE it_final_bar_temp WHERE per = '(X) NET PROFIT BEFORE TAX (VIII-IX)' .
9028     DELETE it_final_bar_temp WHERE per = '(XII) NET PROFIT/(LOSS) AFTER TAX (X-XI)' .
9029     DELETE it_final_bar_temp WHERE per = 'IMPAIRMENT LOSS/(REVERSAL)' .
9030     DELETE it_final_bar_temp WHERE per = 'NET PROFIT/(LOSS) AFTER TAX-BEFORE IMPAIRMENT' .
9031     DELETE it_final_bar_temp WHERE per = ''.
9032     "End of Changes       "Madhu
9033     CLEAR wa_final_bar1.
9034   *  READ TABLE it_final_bar1 INTO wa_final_bar1 WITH KEY sel = 'X'.
9035     LOOP AT it_final_bar_temp INTO wa_final_bar1.
9036       IF wa_final_bar1-remark IS INITIAL  .
9037   *      MESSAGE 'Please fill remarks in all columns. ' TYPE 'E'.
9038         MESSAGE 'Remarks column cannot be left blank. ' TYPE 'E'.
9039       ENDIF.
9040     ENDLOOP.
9041     LOOP AT it_final_bar_temp INTO wa_final_bar1 WHERE sel  = 'X'.
9042       IF  wa_final_bar1-per IS NOT INITIAL.
9043         IF wa_final_bar1-remark IS INITIAL  .
9044
9045   *        MESSAGE 'Please fill remarks in all columns. ' TYPE 'E'.
9046           MESSAGE 'Remarks column cannot be left blank. ' TYPE 'E'.
9047         ELSE.
9048           wa_zfi_pl_var_prd-mandt  = sy-mandt.
9049           wa_zfi_pl_var_prd-per    = wa_final_bar1-per.
9050           CONDENSE wa_final_bar1-remark.
9051           wa_zfi_pl_var_prd-remark = wa_final_bar1-remark.
9052           wa_zfi_pl_var_prd-period = s_period-low.
9053           wa_zfi_pl_var_prd-period2 =  s_period_low2.
9054           wa_zfi_pl_var_prd-fiscal = p_year1.
9055           wa_zfi_pl_var_prd-proj = p_proj1-low.
9056           wa_zfi_pl_var_prd-comp = p_comp1.
9057           APPEND wa_zfi_pl_var_prd TO  it_zfi_val  .
9058           CLEAR  wa_zfi_pl_var_prd.
9059
9060         ENDIF.
9061       ENDIF.
9062     ENDLOOP .
9063     MODIFY zfi_pl_var_prd FROM TABLE it_zfi_val.
9064     IF sy-subrc = 0.
9065       COMMIT WORK AND WAIT.
9066       MESSAGE 'Remark Saved Successfully' TYPE 'S'.
9067       CLEAR: wa_zfi_pl_var_prd, wa_final_bar1.
9068       REFRESH : it_zfi_val , it_final_bar_temp .
9069     ENDIF.
9070
9071     wa_zfi_status-mandt  = sy-mandt.
9072     wa_zfi_status-period = s_period-low.
9073     wa_zfi_status-period2 = s_period_low2.
9074     wa_zfi_status-fiscal = p_year1.
9075     wa_zfi_status-fiscal2 = p_year2.

9125   FORM cre_var_non.   "Calculations for variance analysis non-producing projects
9126
9127     CLEAR wa_final_non.
9128     LOOP AT it_final_non INTO wa_final_non.
9129
9130       wa_final_non-val3 =   wa_final_non-val1 - wa_final_non-val2.
9131
9132       IF wa_final_non-val2 = '0.00'. "value cannot be zero to avoid arthematic errors
9133         wa_final_non-val2 = '0.01'.
9134       ENDIF.
9135
9136       wa_final_non-val4 = ( wa_final_non-val3 / wa_final_non-val2  ) * 100.
9137
9138       wa_final_non-val3 = round( val = wa_final_non-val3 dec = '2' ) .
9139       wa_final_non-val4 = round( val = wa_final_non-val4 dec = '2' ) .
9140
9141
9142       MODIFY it_final_non FROM wa_final_non TRANSPORTING val3 val4.
9143
9144
9145       CLEAR wa_final_non.
9146
9147     ENDLOOP.
9148
9149   ENDFORM."CRE_VAR_NON

9326   FORM var_non_cell_disable .
9327
9328     LOOP AT it_final_non INTO DATA(wa_non).
9329       IF wa_non-per CS ':' OR wa_non-per = ' ' OR wa_non-per = 'TOTAL INCOME' OR wa_non-per = 'TOTAL EXPENSE'

9670               text_stream = i_texttable
9671               itf_text    = it_ltext.

9674           CALL FUNCTION 'SAVE_TEXT'
9675             EXPORTING

9750   FORM save_remarks1 .
9751     BREAK abapuser02.
9752     DATA(it_final_non_temp) = it_final_non[].
9753     DELETE it_final_non_temp WHERE per = ' ' .
9754     "Begin of Changes     "Madhu
9755     DELETE it_final_non_temp WHERE per = 'TOTAL INCOME' .
9756     DELETE it_final_non_temp WHERE per = 'TOTAL EXPENSE' .
9757     DELETE it_final_non_temp WHERE per = 'PROFIT BEFORE TAX' .
9758     DELETE it_final_non_temp WHERE per = 'NET PROFIT/(LOSS) AFTER TAX' .
9759     "End of Changes
9760     CLEAR wa_final_non.
9761
9762     LOOP AT it_final_non_temp INTO wa_final_non.
9763       IF wa_final_non-remark IS INITIAL  .
9764   *      MESSAGE 'Please fill remarks in all columns. ' TYPE 'E'.
9765         MESSAGE 'Remarks column cannot be left blank. ' TYPE 'E'.
9766       ENDIF.
9767     ENDLOOP.
9768     LOOP AT it_final_non_temp INTO wa_final_non WHERE sel  = 'X'.
9769       IF  wa_final_non-per IS NOT INITIAL.
9770         IF wa_final_non-remark IS INITIAL  .
9771
9772   *        MESSAGE 'Please fill remarks in all columns. ' TYPE 'E'.
9773           MESSAGE 'Remarks column cannot be left blank. ' TYPE 'E'.
9774         ELSE.
9775           wa_zfi_pl_var_non-mandt  = sy-mandt.
9776           wa_zfi_pl_var_non-per    = wa_final_non-per.
9777
9778           CONDENSE wa_final_non-remark.
9779           wa_zfi_pl_var_non-remark = wa_final_non-remark.
9780           wa_zfi_pl_var_non-period = s_period-low.
9781           wa_zfi_pl_var_non-period2 =  s_period_low2.
9782           wa_zfi_pl_var_non-fiscal = p_year1.
9783           wa_zfi_pl_var_non-proj = p_proj1-low.
9784           wa_zfi_pl_var_non-comp = p_comp1.
9785           APPEND wa_zfi_pl_var_non TO  it_zfi_non  .
9786           CLEAR  wa_zfi_pl_var_non.
9787
9788         ENDIF.
9789       ENDIF.
9790     ENDLOOP .
9791     MODIFY zfi_pl_var_non FROM TABLE it_zfi_non.
9792     IF sy-subrc = 0.
9793       COMMIT WORK AND WAIT.
9794       MESSAGE 'Remark Saved Successfully' TYPE 'S'.
9795       CLEAR: wa_zfi_pl_var_non, wa_final_non.
9796       REFRESH : it_zfi_non , it_final_non_temp .
9797     ENDIF.
9798

9800     wa_zfi_status1-period = s_period-low.
9801     wa_zfi_status1-period2 = s_period_low2.
9802     wa_zfi_status1-fiscal = p_year1.

9851   FORM conv_fact_chk.  "Check to restrict the conversion factors within the given range
9852
9853     IF ( sy-ucomm = 'CALC' ) OR ( sy-ucomm = 'SAVE' ).
9854
9855       IF ton_bbl LT '6.000000' OR ton_bbl GT '9.000000'.
9856         CLEAR sy-ucomm.
9857         MESSAGE 'Conversion factor input "Tonnes To BBL " is beyond the range . Range 6.000000 to 9.000000'  TYPE 'E'.
9858       ENDIF.
9859       IF mmscm_mmbtu LT '0.034' OR mmscm_mmbtu GT '0.039'.
9860         CLEAR sy-ucomm.
9861         MESSAGE 'Conversion factor input "MMSCM  to MMBTU " is beyond the range. Range 0.039 to 0.034' TYPE 'E'.
9862       ENDIF.
9863
9864     ENDIF.
9865
9866   ENDFORM."CONV_FACT_CHK.

10485          net_atax_oil1 = wa_final_per_bar-oilp1 = net_btax_oil1 - in_tax_oil1.
10486          net_atax_gas1 = wa_final_per_bar-gasp1 = net_btax_gas1 - in_tax_gas1.
10487          net_atax_oil2 = wa_final_per_bar-oilp2 = net_btax_oil2 - in_tax_oil2.
10488          net_atax_gas2 = wa_final_per_bar-gasp2 = net_btax_gas2 - in_tax_gas2.
10489          net_atax_oil3 = wa_final_per_bar-oilp3 = net_btax_oil3 - in_tax_oil3.
10490          net_atax_gas3 = wa_final_per_bar-gasp3 = net_btax_gas3 - in_tax_gas3.
10491          net_atax_oil4 = wa_final_per_bar-oilp4 = net_btax_oil4 - in_tax_oil4.
10492          net_atax_gas4 = wa_final_per_bar-gasp4 = net_btax_gas4 - in_tax_gas4.
10493
10494        WHEN 45."Impairment loss/(reversal)
10495
10496          imploss_oil1 = wa_final_per_bar-oilp1 = wa_final_per_bar-oil1 / wa_assoct_oil1-sh_lift.
10497          imploss_oil2 = wa_final_per_bar-gasp1 = wa_final_per_bar-gas1 / wa_assoct_gas1-sh_lift.
10498          imploss_oil3 = wa_final_per_bar-oilp2 = wa_final_per_bar-oil2 / wa_assoct_oil2-sh_lift.
10499          imploss_oil4 = wa_final_per_bar-gasp2 = wa_final_per_bar-gas2 / wa_assoct_gas2-sh_lift.
10500          imploss_gas1 = wa_final_per_bar-oilp3 = wa_final_per_bar-oil3 / wa_assoct_oil3-sh_lift.
10501          imploss_gas2 = wa_final_per_bar-gasp3 = wa_final_per_bar-gas3 / wa_assoct_gas3-sh_lift.
10502          imploss_gas3 = wa_final_per_bar-oilp4 = wa_final_per_bar-oil4 / wa_assoct_oil4-sh_lift.
10503          imploss_gas4 = wa_final_per_bar-gasp4 = wa_final_per_bar-gas4 / wa_assoct_gas4-sh_lift.
10504
10505        WHEN 47."Net Profit/(Loss) After Tax-before Impairment
10506
10507          wa_final_per_bar-oilp1 = net_atax_oil1 - imploss_oil1.
10508          wa_final_per_bar-gasp1 = net_atax_gas1 - imploss_oil2.
10509          wa_final_per_bar-oilp2 = net_atax_oil2 - imploss_oil3.
10510          wa_final_per_bar-gasp2 = net_atax_gas2 - imploss_oil4.
10511          wa_final_per_bar-oilp3 = net_atax_oil3 - imploss_gas1.
10512          wa_final_per_bar-gasp3 = net_atax_gas3 - imploss_gas2.
10513          wa_final_per_bar-oilp4 = net_atax_oil4 - imploss_gas3.
10514          wa_final_per_bar-gasp4 = net_atax_gas4 - imploss_gas4.
10515
10516      ENDCASE.
10517
10518      wa_final_per_bar-oilp1 = round( val = wa_final_per_bar-oilp1 dec = '2' ) .
10519      wa_final_per_bar-oilp2 = round( val = wa_final_per_bar-oilp2 dec = '2' ) .
10520      wa_final_per_bar-oilp3 = round( val = wa_final_per_bar-oilp3 dec = '2' ) .
10521      wa_final_per_bar-oilp4 = round( val = wa_final_per_bar-oilp4 dec = '2' ) .
10522
10523      wa_final_per_bar-gasp1 = round( val = wa_final_per_bar-gasp1 dec = '2' ) .
10524      wa_final_per_bar-gasp2 = round( val = wa_final_per_bar-gasp2 dec = '2' ) .
10525      wa_final_per_bar-gasp3 = round( val = wa_final_per_bar-gasp3 dec = '2' ) .
10526      wa_final_per_bar-gasp4 = round( val = wa_final_per_bar-gasp4 dec = '2' ) .
10527
10528      MODIFY it_final_per_bar FROM wa_final_per_bar TRANSPORTING oilp1 oilp2 oilp3 oilp4 gasp1 gasp2 gasp3 gasp4.
10529      CLEAR wa_final_per_bar.
10530
10531    ENDLOOP.
10532
10533  ENDFORM. "CRE_PER_BARL
10534
10535  FORM fcat_fill_per_bar.
10536    DATA bar_h TYPE string.
10537
10538    CLEAR bar_h.
10539    PERFORM period_text USING s_period-low p_year1.
10540
10541    CLEAR bar_h.
10542    CONCATENATE per_text 'Crude Oil' INTO bar_h SEPARATED BY space.
10543    PERFORM alv_fcat_per_bar USING : 'PER'       'Particulars',
10544                                     'OIL1'      bar_h.
10545
10546    CLEAR bar_h.
10547    CONCATENATE per_text 'Per BBL Crude Oil' INTO bar_h SEPARATED BY space.
10548    PERFORM alv_fcat_per_bar USING : 'OILP1'     bar_h.
10549
10550    CLEAR bar_h.
10551    CONCATENATE per_text 'Gas' INTO bar_h SEPARATED BY space.
10552    PERFORM alv_fcat_per_bar USING : 'GAS1'      bar_h.
10553
10554    CLEAR bar_h.
10555    CONCATENATE per_text 'Per BBL Gas' INTO bar_h SEPARATED BY space.
10556    PERFORM alv_fcat_per_bar USING : 'GASP1'     bar_h.
10557
10558    IF  p_year2 IS NOT INITIAL.
10559
10560      CLEAR bar_h.
10561      PERFORM period_text USING s_period_low2 p_year2.
10562
10563      CLEAR bar_h.
10564      CONCATENATE per_text 'Crude Oil' INTO bar_h SEPARATED BY space.
10565      PERFORM alv_fcat_per_bar USING : 'OIL2'      bar_h.
10566
10567      CLEAR bar_h.
10568      CONCATENATE per_text 'Per BBL Crude Oil' INTO bar_h SEPARATED BY space.
10569      PERFORM alv_fcat_per_bar USING : 'OILP2'     bar_h.
10570
10571      CLEAR bar_h.
10572      CONCATENATE per_text 'Gas' INTO bar_h SEPARATED BY space.
10573      PERFORM alv_fcat_per_bar USING : 'GAS2'      bar_h.
10574
10575      CLEAR bar_h.
10576      CONCATENATE per_text 'Per BBL Gas' INTO bar_h SEPARATED BY space.
10577      PERFORM alv_fcat_per_bar USING : 'GASP2'     bar_h.
10578
10579    ENDIF.
10580
10581    IF  p_year3 IS NOT INITIAL.
10582
10583      CLEAR bar_h.
10584      PERFORM period_text USING s_period_low3 p_year3.
10585
10586      CLEAR bar_h.
10587      CONCATENATE per_text 'Crude Oil' INTO bar_h SEPARATED BY space.
10588      PERFORM alv_fcat_per_bar USING : 'OIL3'      bar_h.
10589
10590      CLEAR bar_h.
10591      CONCATENATE per_text 'Per BBL Crude Oil' INTO bar_h SEPARATED BY space.
10592      PERFORM alv_fcat_per_bar USING : 'OILP3'     bar_h.
10593
10594      CLEAR bar_h.
10595      CONCATENATE per_text 'Gas' INTO bar_h SEPARATED BY space.
10596      PERFORM alv_fcat_per_bar USING : 'GAS3'      bar_h.
10597
10598      CLEAR bar_h.
10599      CONCATENATE per_text 'PER BBL Gas' INTO bar_h SEPARATED BY space.
10600      PERFORM alv_fcat_per_bar USING : 'GASP3'     bar_h.
10601
10602    ENDIF.
10603
10604    IF  p_year4 IS NOT INITIAL.
10605      CLEAR bar_h.
10606      PERFORM period_text USING s_period_low4 p_year4.
10607
10608      CLEAR bar_h.
10609      CONCATENATE per_text 'Crude Oil' INTO bar_h SEPARATED BY space.
10610      PERFORM alv_fcat_per_bar USING : 'OIL4'      bar_h.
10611
10612      CLEAR bar_h.
10613      CONCATENATE per_text 'Per BBL Crude Oil' INTO bar_h SEPARATED BY space.
10614      PERFORM alv_fcat_per_bar USING : 'OILP4'     bar_h.
10615
10616      CLEAR bar_h.
10617      CONCATENATE per_text 'Gas' INTO bar_h SEPARATED BY space.
10618      PERFORM alv_fcat_per_bar USING : 'GAS4'      bar_h.
10619
10620      CLEAR bar_h.
10621      CONCATENATE per_text 'Per BBL Gas' INTO bar_h SEPARATED BY space.
10622      PERFORM alv_fcat_per_bar USING : 'GASP4'     bar_h.
10623
10624    ENDIF.
10625
10626  ENDFORM."fcat_fill_per_bar

10904    DATA(lv_qt_rp_frz) = 'Z:FI_PL_RP_FRZ'.

10906    DATA(lv_bu_rp_apac) = 'Z:FI_PL_RP_APAC'.
10907    DATA(lv_bu_rp_mena) = 'Z:FI_PL_RP_MENA'.

10910    DATA(lv_bu_rp_lac)  = 'Z:FI_PL_RP_LAC'.
10911    DATA(lv_bu_rp_rus)  = 'Z:FI_PL_RP_RUS'.
10912    DATA(lv_bu_rp_moz)  = 'Z:FI_PL_RP_MOZ'.
10913    DATA(lv_bu_rp_corp) = 'Z:FI_PL_RP_CORP'.
10914    DATA(lv_ap_rp_corp) = 'Z:FI_PL_RP_CHG_CORP'.
10915  *  ended by Aparna on 03.07.24 on request of Rahul Pauranik
10916    IF sy-ucomm = 'CREATE' OR sy-ucomm = 'CHANGE'.
10917      SELECT SINGLE * FROM agr_users INTO @DATA(lv_cre_qt)
10918                    WHERE uname = @sy-uname
10919                    AND agr_name = @lv_qt_cre.
10920
10921      SELECT SINGLE * FROM zpnl_qi_oil
10922             INTO @DATA(chk_cre_quant)
10923             WHERE company = @p_company
10924               AND bukrs   = @p_comp
10925               AND pro_nam = @p_proj
10926               AND fisyear = @p_year
10927               AND zperiod = @s_period_low.
10928
10929      IF chk_cre_quant-approved_by IS NOT INITIAL AND sy-ucomm = 'CHANGE'.
10930        SELECT SINGLE * FROM agr_users INTO @DATA(lv_app_corp)
10931                WHERE uname = @sy-uname
10932                AND agr_name = @lv_ap_corp.
10933        IF lv_app_corp IS INITIAL.
10934          MESSAGE 'You cannot change the approved data' TYPE 'E' DISPLAY LIKE 'I'.
10935        ENDIF.
10936      ENDIF.
10937
10938      IF lv_cre_qt IS INITIAL.
10939        MESSAGE 'You are not authorized' TYPE 'E' DISPLAY LIKE 'I'.
10940      ENDIF.
10941
10942    ELSEIF sy-ucomm =  'APPROVE'.
10943      SELECT SINGLE * FROM agr_users INTO @DATA(lv_app_qt)
10944               WHERE uname = @sy-uname
10945               AND agr_name = @lv_qt_app.
10946
10947      IF lv_app_qt IS NOT INITIAL.
10948        SELECT uname,agr_name FROM agr_users INTO TABLE @DATA(lt_app_bu)
10949               WHERE uname = @sy-uname
10950               AND agr_name IN ( @lv_bu_apac,@lv_bu_mena,@lv_bu_cis,@lv_bu_col,@lv_bu_lac,@lv_bu_rus,@lv_bu_moz,@lv_bu_corp ).
10951
