11442  FORM cre_quant_figs.
11443
11444    DATA(lv_q_count) = 1.
11445
11446    IF s_period-low IS NOT INITIAL.
11447      CLEAR : lv_s_period,mode,lv_p_year.
11448      mode = 'DIS'.
11449      lv_p_year   = p_year1.
11450      lv_s_period = s_period-low.
11451      PERFORM create_quant.
11452      PERFORM fetch_quant.
11453      PERFORM cal_quant.
11454
11455      LOOP AT it_quant INTO DATA(wa_q_ana).
11456        wa_pro_ana-sr_no = wa_q_ana-sr_no.
11457        wa_pro_ana-per   = wa_q_ana-data.
11458        wa_pro_ana-oil1  = wa_q_ana-bbl.
11459        wa_pro_ana-gas1  = wa_q_ana-mmbtu.
11460
11461        APPEND wa_pro_ana TO it_pro_ana.
11462        CLEAR:wa_pro_ana,wa_q_ana.
11463      ENDLOOP.
11464    ENDIF.
11465
11466    IF s_period_low2 IS NOT INITIAL.
11467      CLEAR : lv_s_period,mode,lv_q_count,lv_p_year.
11468      mode = 'DIS'.
11469      lv_p_year   = p_year2.
11470      lv_s_period = s_period_low2.
11471      PERFORM create_quant.
11472      PERFORM fetch_quant.
11473      PERFORM cal_quant.
11474
11475      lv_q_count = 1.
11476
11477      LOOP AT it_quant INTO wa_q_ana.
11478        lv_q_count = lv_q_count + 1.
11479        READ TABLE it_pro_ana INTO wa_pro_ana INDEX lv_q_count.
11480        wa_pro_ana-oil2 = wa_q_ana-bbl.
11481        wa_pro_ana-gas2 = wa_q_ana-mmbtu.
11482
11483        MODIFY it_pro_ana FROM wa_pro_ana INDEX lv_q_count TRANSPORTING oil2 gas2.
11484        CLEAR:wa_pro_ana,wa_q_ana.
11485      ENDLOOP.
11486      CLEAR lv_q_count.
11487    ENDIF.
11488
11489    IF s_period_low3 IS NOT INITIAL.
11490      CLEAR : lv_s_period,mode,lv_q_count,lv_p_year.
11491      mode = 'DIS'.
11492      lv_p_year   = p_year3.
11493      lv_s_period = s_period_low3.
11494      PERFORM create_quant.
11495      PERFORM fetch_quant.
11496      PERFORM cal_quant.
11497
11498      lv_q_count = 1.
11499
11500      LOOP AT it_quant INTO wa_q_ana.
11501        lv_q_count = lv_q_count + 1.
11502        READ TABLE it_pro_ana INTO wa_pro_ana INDEX lv_q_count.
11503        wa_pro_ana-oil3 = wa_q_ana-bbl.
11504        wa_pro_ana-gas3 = wa_q_ana-mmbtu.
11505
11506        MODIFY it_pro_ana FROM wa_pro_ana INDEX lv_q_count TRANSPORTING oil3 gas3 .
11507        CLEAR:wa_pro_ana,wa_q_ana.
11508      ENDLOOP.
11509      CLEAR lv_q_count.
11510    ENDIF.
11511
11512    IF s_period_low4 IS NOT INITIAL.
11513      CLEAR : lv_s_period,mode,lv_q_count,lv_p_year.
11514      mode = 'DIS'.
11515      lv_p_year   = p_year4.
11516      lv_s_period = s_period_low4.
11517      PERFORM create_quant.
11518      PERFORM fetch_quant.
11519      PERFORM cal_quant.
11520
11521      lv_q_count = 1.
11522
11523      LOOP AT it_quant INTO wa_q_ana.
11524        lv_q_count = lv_q_count + 1.
11525        READ TABLE it_pro_ana INTO wa_pro_ana INDEX lv_q_count.
11526        wa_pro_ana-oil4 = wa_q_ana-bbl.
11527        wa_pro_ana-gas4 = wa_q_ana-mmbtu.
11528
11529        MODIFY it_pro_ana FROM wa_pro_ana INDEX lv_q_count  TRANSPORTING oil4 gas4.
11530        CLEAR:wa_pro_ana,wa_q_ana.
11531      ENDLOOP.
11532      CLEAR lv_q_count.
11533    ENDIF.
11534
11535  ENDFORM. "CRE_QUANT_FIGS

11699  FORM get_pend_rep .
11700    REFRESH lt_pend_fin[].
11701
11702    IF r_pqi = 'X'.
11703
11704      SELECT * FROM zfi_pnl_proj INTO TABLE @DATA(lt_proj1).
11705
11706      REFRESH lt_pend[].
11707      SELECT company,
11708             pro_nam,
11709             fisyear,
11710             zperiod,
11711             bukrs,
11712             created_by,
11713             created_on,
11714             approved_by,
11715             approved_on,
11716             approved_by_rp,
11717             approved_on_rp
11718        FROM zpnl_qi_oil INTO CORRESPONDING FIELDS OF TABLE @lt_pend
11719        WHERE fisyear = @p_year1
11720          AND zperiod IN @s_period.
11721
11722      IF lt_pend[] IS INITIAL.
11723        SELECT company,
11724               pro_nam,
11725               fisyear,
11726               zperiod,
11727               bukrs,
11728               created_by,
11729               created_on,
11730               approved_by,
11731               approved_on,
11732              approved_by_rp,
11733             approved_on_rp
11734          FROM zpnl_qi_gas INTO CORRESPONDING FIELDS OF TABLE @lt_pend
11735          WHERE fisyear =  @p_year1
11736            AND zperiod IN @s_period.
11737      ENDIF.
11738
11739    ELSEIF r_ppp = 'X'.
11740
11741      REFRESH lt_proj1[].
11742      SELECT *
11743      FROM   zfi_pnl_proj
11744      INTO   TABLE lt_proj1
11745             WHERE manual = 'YES'
11746             AND   prod   = 'YES'.
11747
11748      REFRESH lt_pend[].
11749      SELECT company,
11750             pro_nam,
11751             fisyear,
11752             zperiod,
11753             bukrs,
11754             created_by,
11755             created_on,
11756             approved_by,
11757             approved_on,
11758             approved_by_rp,
11759             approved_on_rp
11760        FROM zpnl_prd_oil
11761        INTO CORRESPONDING FIELDS OF TABLE @lt_pend
11762             WHERE fisyear =  @p_year1
11763             AND   zperiod IN @s_period.
11764
11765      IF lt_pend[] IS INITIAL.
11766        SELECT company,
11767               pro_nam,
11768               fisyear,
11769               zperiod,
11770               bukrs,
11771               created_by,
11772               created_on,
11773               approved_by,
11774               approved_on,
11775               approved_by_rp,
11776               approved_on_rp
11777          FROM zpnl_prd_gas
11778          INTO CORRESPONDING FIELDS OF TABLE @lt_pend
11779               WHERE fisyear =  @p_year1
11780               AND   zperiod IN @s_period.
11781      ENDIF.
11782
11783    ELSEIF r_pop = 'X'.
11784
11785      REFRESH lt_proj1[].
11786      SELECT *
11787      FROM   zfi_pnl_proj
11788      INTO   TABLE lt_proj1
11789             WHERE manual = 'YES'
11790             AND   prod   = 'NO'.
11791
11792      REFRESH lt_pend[].
11793      SELECT  company,
11794              pro_nam,
11795              fisyear,
11796              zperiod,
11797              bukrs,
11798              created_by,
11799              created_on,
11800              approve_by,
11801              approve_on,
11802              approved_by_rp,
11803              approved_on_rp
11804        FROM  zpnl_oth_proj
11805        INTO  TABLE @DATA(lt_other)
11806              WHERE fisyear =  @p_year1
11807              AND   zperiod IN @s_period.
11808
11809    ELSEIF r_var = 'X'.
11810
11811      REFRESH lt_proj1[].
11812
11813      SELECT *
11814      FROM   zfi_pnl_proj
11815      INTO   TABLE lt_proj1.
11816  *           WHERE   prod   = 'YES'.
11817

11823             WHERE fiscal =  @p_year1
11824             AND   period IN @s_period.
11825
11826    ELSEIF r_nvar = 'X'.
11827

11829

11832      INTO   TABLE lt_proj1.
11833  *           WHERE  prod   = 'NO'.
11834

11836

11839      INTO   TABLE @DATA(lt_nvar)
11840             WHERE fiscal =  @p_year1
11841             AND   period IN @s_period.
11842
11843    ENDIF.
11844
11845    IF p_compy1 IS NOT INITIAL.
11846      DELETE lt_pend[] WHERE company NE p_compy1.
11847      DELETE lt_proj1[] WHERE company NE p_compy1.
11848    ENDIF.

11850      DELETE lt_pend[] WHERE pro_nam NE p_proj1-low.
11851      DELETE lt_proj1[] WHERE jvnam NE p_proj1-low.
11852    ENDIF.
11853
11854    IF r_pop = 'X'.
11855

11857        READ TABLE lt_other INTO DATA(ls_other) WITH KEY pro_nam = ls_proj1-jvnam zperiod = s_period-low.
11858        IF ls_other-approve_by IS NOT INITIAL.
11859          ls_pend-status = 'Approved'.
11860        ELSE.
11861          ls_pend-status  = 'Pending'.
11862        ENDIF.
11863        ls_pend-company = ls_proj1-company.
11864        ls_pend-bukrs   = ls_proj1-bukrs.
11865        ls_pend-pro_nam = ls_proj1-jvnam.
11866        ls_pend-fisyear = p_year1.
11867        ls_pend-zperiod = s_period-low.
11868        ls_pend-approved_by  = ls_other-approve_by.
11869        ls_pend-approved_on  = ls_other-approve_on.
11870        ls_pend-approved_by_rp  = ls_other-approved_by_rp.
11871        ls_pend-approved_on_rp  = ls_other-approved_on_rp.
11872        ls_pend-created_by  = ls_other-created_by.
11873        ls_pend-created_on  = ls_other-created_on.
11874
11875
11876        APPEND ls_pend TO lt_pend_fin.
11877        CLEAR ls_pend.
11878      ENDLOOP.
11879    ELSEIF r_var = 'X'.
11880      LOOP AT lt_proj1 INTO ls_proj1.
11881        LOOP AT lt_var INTO DATA(ls_var) WHERE  proj = ls_proj1-jvnam  AND period = s_period-low.
11882          IF ls_var-approved_by IS NOT INITIAL.
11883            ls_pend-status = 'Approved'.
11884          ELSE.
11885            ls_pend-status  = 'Pending'.
11886          ENDIF.
11887          ls_pend-company = ls_proj1-company.
11888          ls_pend-bukrs   = ls_proj1-bukrs.
11889          ls_pend-pro_nam = ls_proj1-jvnam.
11890          ls_pend-fisyear = p_year1.
11891          ls_pend-fisyear1 = ls_var-fiscal2.
11892          ls_pend-zperiod = s_period-low.
11893          ls_pend-zperiod1 = ls_var-period2.
11894          ls_pend-approved_by  = ls_var-approved_by.
11895          ls_pend-approved_on  = ls_var-approved_on.
11896          ls_pend-approved_by_rp  = ls_var-approved_by_rp.
11897          ls_pend-approved_on_rp  = ls_var-approved_on_rp.
11898          ls_pend-created_by  = ls_var-created_by.
11899          ls_pend-created_on  = ls_var-created_on.
11900          APPEND ls_pend TO lt_pend_fin.
11901          CLEAR ls_pend.
11902        ENDLOOP.
11903      ENDLOOP.
11904    ELSEIF r_nvar = 'X'.
11905      LOOP AT lt_proj1 INTO ls_proj1.
11906        LOOP AT lt_nvar INTO DATA(ls_nvar) WHERE  proj = ls_proj1-jvnam AND period = s_period-low.
11907          IF ls_nvar-approved_by IS NOT INITIAL.
11908            ls_pend-status = 'Approved'.
11909          ELSE.
11910            ls_pend-status  = 'Pending'.
11911          ENDIF.
11912          ls_pend-company = ls_proj1-company.
11913          ls_pend-bukrs   = ls_proj1-bukrs.
11914          ls_pend-pro_nam = ls_proj1-jvnam.
11915          ls_pend-fisyear = p_year1.
11916          ls_pend-zperiod = s_period-low.
11917          ls_pend-approved_by  = ls_nvar-approved_by.
11918          ls_pend-approved_on  = ls_nvar-approved_on.
11919          ls_pend-approved_by_rp  = ls_nvar-approved_by_rp.
11920          ls_pend-approved_on_rp  = ls_nvar-approved_on_rp.
11921          ls_pend-created_by  = ls_nvar-created_by.
11922          ls_pend-created_on  = ls_nvar-created_on.
11923          APPEND ls_pend TO lt_pend_fin.
11924          CLEAR ls_pend.
11925        ENDLOOP.
11926      ENDLOOP.
11927    ELSE.
11928      LOOP AT lt_proj1 INTO ls_proj1.
11929        READ TABLE lt_pend INTO DATA(ls_pend) WITH KEY pro_nam = ls_proj1-jvnam zperiod = s_period-low.
11930        IF ls_pend-approved_by IS NOT INITIAL.
11931          ls_pend-status = 'Approved'.
11932        ELSE.
11933          ls_pend-company = ls_proj1-company.
11934          ls_pend-bukrs   = ls_proj1-bukrs.
11935          ls_pend-pro_nam = ls_proj1-jvnam.
11936          ls_pend-fisyear = p_year1.
11937          ls_pend-zperiod = s_period-low.
11938          ls_pend-status  = 'Pending'.
11939        ENDIF.
11940
11941        APPEND ls_pend TO lt_pend_fin.
11942        CLEAR ls_pend.
11943      ENDLOOP.

12494  *&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
12495  *&      Form  CRE_PRD_QTY
12496  *&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
12497  *       text
12498  *----------------------------------------------------------------------*
12499  *  -->  p1        text
12500  *  <--  p2        text
12501  *----------------------------------------------------------------------*
12502  FORM cre_prd_qty .
12503    BREAK abapuser02.
12504  *  PERFORM fetch_quant.
12505    SELECT txtjv
12506           jvnam
12507    FROM   zfi_pnl_proj
12508    INTO   TABLE it_proj
12509           WHERE
12510          prod  = 'YES'.
12511
12512    IF it_proj IS NOT INITIAL.
12513      SELECT *
12514      FROM   zpnl_qi_oil
12515      INTO   TABLE @DATA(it_q1)
12516             FOR ALL ENTRIES IN @it_proj
12517             WHERE pro_nam = @it_proj-jvnam
12518             AND   fisyear = @p_year1
12519             AND   zperiod = @s_period-low .
12520  *           AND   bukrs = @p_comp.
12521
12522      SELECT *
12523      FROM   zpnl_qi_oil
12524      INTO   TABLE @DATA(it_q2)
12525             FOR ALL ENTRIES IN @it_proj
12526             WHERE pro_nam = @it_proj-jvnam
12527             AND   fisyear = @p_year2
12528             AND zperiod = @s_period_low2.
12529  *           bukrs = @p_comp.
12530
12531      SELECT *
12532     FROM   zpnl_qi_oil
12533     INTO   TABLE @DATA(it_h1)
12534            FOR ALL ENTRIES IN @it_proj
12535            WHERE pro_nam = @it_proj-jvnam
12536            AND   fisyear = @p_year2
12537            AND zperiod = 'H1'.
12538
12539      SELECT *
12540       FROM   zpnl_qi_oil
12541       INTO   TABLE @DATA(it_9m)
12542              FOR ALL ENTRIES IN @it_proj
12543              WHERE pro_nam = @it_proj-jvnam
12544              AND   fisyear = @p_year2
12545              AND zperiod = '9M'.
12546      SELECT *
12547  FROM   zpnl_qi_oil
12548  INTO   TABLE @DATA(it_q11)
12549        FOR ALL ENTRIES IN @it_proj
12550        WHERE pro_nam = @it_proj-jvnam
12551        AND   fisyear = @p_year2
12552        AND zperiod = 'Q1'.
12553
12554      SELECT *
12555   FROM   zpnl_qi_gas
12556   INTO   TABLE @DATA(it_q3)
12557          FOR ALL ENTRIES IN @it_proj
12558          WHERE pro_nam = @it_proj-jvnam
12559          AND   fisyear = @p_year1
12560          AND   zperiod = @s_period-low .
12561  *           AND   bukrs = @p_comp.
12562
12563      SELECT *
12564      FROM   zpnl_qi_gas
12565      INTO   TABLE @DATA(it_q4)
12566             FOR ALL ENTRIES IN @it_proj
12567             WHERE pro_nam = @it_proj-jvnam
12568             AND   fisyear = @p_year2
12569             AND zperiod = @s_period_low2.
12570  *           bukrs = @p_comp.
12571    ENDIF.
12572    REFRESH : it_oil.
12573    LOOP AT it_proj INTO DATA(wa_proj).
12574      wa_oil-jv = wa_proj-txtjv.
12575      wa_oil-jv_name = wa_proj-jvnam.
12576
12577      TRY.
12578          DATA(wa_q1) = it_q1[ pro_nam = wa_proj-jvnam ].
12579          CONDENSE: wa_q1-sh_prod,wa_q1-tot_sal.
12580          wa_oil-sh_prd_q1  = wa_q1-sh_prod * '0.000001'.
12581          wa_oil-tot_sal_q1 = wa_q1-tot_sal * '0.000001'.
12582        CATCH cx_sy_itab_line_not_found.
12583      ENDTRY.
12584      IF s_period_low2 = 'Q2'.
12585
12586        TRY.
12587            DATA(wa_h1) = it_h1[ pro_nam = wa_proj-jvnam ].
12588  *          CONDENSE: wa_q2-sh_prod,wa_q2-tot_sal.
12589  *          wa_oil-sh_prd_q2  = wa_q2-sh_prod * '0.000001'.
12590  *          wa_oil-tot_sal_q2 = wa_q2-tot_sal * '0.000001'.
12591
12592          CATCH cx_sy_itab_line_not_found.
12593        ENDTRY.
12594
12595        TRY.
12596            DATA(wa_q11) = it_q11[ pro_nam = wa_proj-jvnam ].
12597  *          CONDENSE: wa_q2-sh_prod,wa_q2-tot_sal.
12598  *          wa_oil-sh_prd_q2  = wa_q2-sh_prod * '0.000001'.
12599  *          wa_oil-tot_sal_q2 = wa_q2-tot_sal * '0.000001'.
12600
12601          CATCH cx_sy_itab_line_not_found.
12602            wa_oil-sh_prd_q2  = ( wa_h1-sh_prod - wa_q11-sh_prod ) * '0.000001'.
12603            wa_oil-tot_sal_q2 = ( wa_h1-tot_sal - wa_q11-tot_sal )  * '0.000001'.
12604        ENDTRY.
12605
12606
12607  *      CONDENSE:  wa_q2-sh_prod ,wa_q2-tot_sal.
12608        wa_oil-sh_prd_q2  = ( wa_h1-sh_prod - wa_q11-sh_prod ) * '0.000001'.
12609        wa_oil-tot_sal_q2 = ( wa_h1-tot_sal - wa_q11-tot_sal )  * '0.000001'.
12610
12611      ELSE.
12612        TRY.
12613            DATA(wa_q2) = it_q2[ pro_nam = wa_proj-jvnam ].
12614            CONDENSE: wa_q2-sh_prod,wa_q2-tot_sal.
12615            wa_oil-sh_prd_q2  = wa_q2-sh_prod * '0.000001'.
12616            wa_oil-tot_sal_q2 = wa_q2-tot_sal * '0.000001'.
12617
12618          CATCH cx_sy_itab_line_not_found.
12619        ENDTRY.
12620      ENDIF.
12621      TRY.
12622          DATA(wa_q3) = it_q3[ pro_nam = wa_proj-jvnam ].
12623          CONDENSE: wa_q3-sh_prod,wa_q3-tot_sal.
12624          wa_oil-gas1 = wa_q3-sh_prod * '0.006290'.
12625          wa_oil-gas2 = wa_q3-tot_sal * '0.006290'.
12626          wa_oil-gas3 = wa_q3-sh_prod.
12627          wa_oil-gas4 = wa_q3-tot_sal.
12628          wa_oil-gas5 = wa_q3-sh_prod * wa_final_conv-mmscm_mmbtu.
12629          wa_oil-gas6 =  wa_q3-tot_sal * wa_final_conv-mmscm_mmbtu.
12630        CATCH cx_sy_itab_line_not_found.
12631      ENDTRY.
12632
12633      TRY.
12634          DATA(wa_q4) = it_q4[ pro_nam = wa_proj-jvnam ].
12635          CONDENSE: wa_q4-sh_prod,wa_q4-tot_sal.
12636          wa_oil-gas7 = wa_q3-sh_prod * '0.006290'.
12637          wa_oil-gas8 = wa_q3-tot_sal * '0.006290'.
12638          wa_oil-gas9 = wa_q3-sh_prod.
12639          wa_oil-gas10 = wa_q3-tot_sal.
12640          wa_oil-gas11 = wa_q3-sh_prod * wa_final_conv-mmscm_mmbtu.
12641          wa_oil-gas12 =  wa_q3-tot_sal * wa_final_conv-mmscm_mmbtu.
12642        CATCH cx_sy_itab_line_not_found.
12643      ENDTRY.
12644
12645      APPEND wa_oil TO it_oil.
12646      CLEAR : wa_oil , wa_h1 , wa_q11.
12647    ENDLOOP.
12648  ENDFORM.

12885  FORM cre_rev .
12886    BREAK abapuser02.
12887
12888    SELECT txtjv
12889           jvnam
12890    FROM   zfi_pnl_proj
12891    INTO   TABLE it_proj
12892           WHERE prod  = 'YES'.
12893
12894    IF it_proj IS NOT INITIAL.
12895
12896  ***************** FOR FIRST PERIOD <<<<<<<<<<<<<<<<<<<<<<<<<<<<<<<<<<
12897      SELECT *
12898      FROM   zfi_agenda
12899      INTO   TABLE @DATA(it_agenda1)
12900             FOR ALL ENTRIES IN @it_proj
12901      WHERE proj    = @it_proj-jvnam
12902      AND   fiscal1 = @p_year1
12903      AND   period1 = @s_period-low
12904      AND   per_key = 'A2'.
12905
12906      IF it_agenda1 IS INITIAL.
12907
12908        SELECT *
12909        FROM   zfi_agenda
12910        INTO   TABLE @DATA(it_agenda2)
12911               FOR ALL ENTRIES IN @it_proj
12912        WHERE proj    = @it_proj-jvnam
12913        AND   fiscal2 = @p_year1
12914        AND   period2 = @s_period-low
12915        AND   per_key = 'A2'.
12916      ENDIF.
12917
12918      IF it_agenda2 IS INITIAL.
12919
12920        SELECT *
12921        FROM   zfi_agenda
12922        INTO   TABLE @DATA(it_agenda3)
12923               FOR ALL ENTRIES IN @it_proj
12924        WHERE proj    = @it_proj-jvnam
12925        AND   fiscal3 = @p_year1
12926        AND   period3 = @s_period-low
12927        AND   per_key = 'A2'.
12928
12929      ENDIF.
12930      IF it_agenda3 IS INITIAL.
12931
12932        SELECT *
12933        FROM   zfi_agenda
12934        INTO   TABLE @DATA(it_agenda4)
12935               FOR ALL ENTRIES IN @it_proj
12936        WHERE proj    = @it_proj-jvnam
12937        AND   fiscal4 = @p_year1
12938        AND   period4 = @s_period-low
12939        AND   per_key = 'A2'.
12940      ENDIF.
12941
12942  ************************************* FOR SECOND PERIOD<<<<<<<<<<<<<<<<<<<<<<<<<<<<<<<<<<<<<<<<<<<
12943      SELECT *
12944      FROM   zfi_agenda
12945      INTO   TABLE @DATA(it_agenda5)
12946             FOR ALL ENTRIES IN @it_proj
12947      WHERE proj    = @it_proj-jvnam
12948      AND   fiscal1 = @p_year2
12949      AND period1  = @s_period_low2
12950      AND   per_key = 'A2'.
12951
12952      IF it_agenda5 IS INITIAL.
12953        SELECT *
12954          FROM   zfi_agenda
12955          INTO   TABLE @DATA(it_agenda6)
12956                 FOR ALL ENTRIES IN @it_proj
12957          WHERE proj    = @it_proj-jvnam
12958          AND   fiscal2 = @p_year2
12959          AND period2  = @s_period_low2
12960          AND   per_key = 'A2'.
12961
12962      ENDIF.
12963
12964      IF it_agenda6 IS INITIAL.
12965        SELECT *
12966         FROM   zfi_agenda
12967         INTO   TABLE @DATA(it_agenda7)
12968                FOR ALL ENTRIES IN @it_proj
12969         WHERE proj    = @it_proj-jvnam
12970         AND   fiscal3 = @p_year2
12971         AND period3  = @s_period_low2
12972         AND   per_key = 'A2'.
12973
12974      ENDIF.
12975
12976      IF it_agenda7 IS INITIAL.
12977        SELECT *
12978         FROM   zfi_agenda
12979         INTO   TABLE @DATA(it_agenda8)
12980                FOR ALL ENTRIES IN @it_proj
12981         WHERE proj    = @it_proj-jvnam
12982         AND   fiscal4 = @p_year2
12983         AND period4  = @s_period_low2
12984         AND   per_key = 'A2'.
12985
12986      ENDIF.
12987
12988    ENDIF.
12989    REFRESH it_oil1.
12990    LOOP AT it_proj INTO DATA(wa_proj).
12991      wa_oil1-jv       = wa_proj-jvnam.
12992      wa_oil1-jv_name  = wa_proj-txtjv.
12993  *
12994  *    TRY.
12995  *        wa_oil1-sh_prd_q1 = it_agenda[ proj = wa_proj-jvnam per = 'REVENUE FROM CRUDE OIL' ]-crude_oil1.
12996  *      CATCH cx_sy_itab_line_not_found.
12997  *    ENDTRY.
12998  *
12999  *    TRY.
13000  *        wa_oil1-tot_sal_q1 = it_agenda[ proj = wa_proj-jvnam per = 'REVENUE FROM NATURAL GAS' ]-gas1.
13001  *      CATCH cx_sy_itab_line_not_found.
13002  *    ENDTRY.
13003  *
13004  *    TRY.
13005  *        wa_oil1-sh_prd_q2 = it_agenda2[ proj = wa_proj-jvnam per = 'REVENUE FROM CRUDE OIL' ]-crude_oil2.
13006  *
13007  *      CATCH cx_sy_itab_line_not_found.
13008  *    ENDTRY.
13009  *    TRY.
13010  *        wa_oil1-tot_sal_q2 = it_agenda2[ proj = wa_proj-jvnam per = 'REVENUE FROM NATURAL GAS' ]-gas2.
13011  *      CATCH cx_sy_itab_line_not_found.
13012  *    ENDTRY.
13013
13014      LOOP AT it_agenda1 INTO DATA(wa_agenda) WHERE proj = wa_proj-jvnam.
13015        IF wa_agenda-per =  'REVENUE FROM CRUDE OIL'.
13016          wa_oil1-sh_prd_q1 = wa_agenda-crude_oil1.
13017        ENDIF.
13018
13019        IF wa_agenda-per =  'REVENUE FROM NATURAL GAS'.
13020          wa_oil1-tot_sal_q1 = wa_agenda-gas1.
13021        ENDIF.
13022        IF wa_agenda-per = 'PRODUCTION EXPENSES' OR wa_agenda-per = 'OTHER OPERATING EXPENSE'.
13023          wa_oil1-gas1 = wa_oil1-gas1 + wa_agenda-crude_oil1.
13024          wa_oil1-gas2 = wa_oil1-gas2 + wa_agenda-gas1      .
13025        ENDIF.
13026        IF wa_agenda-per = 'ROYALTY' OR wa_agenda-per = 'OTHER TAXES (AS APPLICABLE)'.
13027          wa_oil1-gas3 = wa_oil1-gas3 + wa_agenda-crude_oil1.
13028          wa_oil1-gas4 = wa_oil1-gas4 + wa_agenda-gas1      .
13029        ENDIF.
13030        IF wa_agenda-per = 'TRANSPORTATION EXPENSES'.
13031          wa_oil1-gas5 = wa_oil1-gas5 + wa_agenda-crude_oil1.
13032          wa_oil1-gas6 = wa_oil1-gas6 + wa_agenda-gas1      .
13033        ENDIF.
13034        IF wa_agenda-per = 'DEPLETION' OR wa_agenda-per = 'DEPRECIATION AND AMORTISATION'.
13035          wa_oil1-gas7 = wa_oil1-gas7 + wa_agenda-crude_oil1.
13036          wa_oil1-gas8 = wa_oil1-gas8 + wa_agenda-gas1      .
13037        ENDIF.
13038        IF wa_agenda-per = 'EXPLORATION COSTS(SURVEY)' OR wa_agenda-per = 'EXPLORATION COSTS(DRY WELL)'.
13039          wa_oil1-gas9  = wa_oil1-gas9  + wa_agenda-crude_oil1.
13040          wa_oil1-gas10 = wa_oil1-gas10 + wa_agenda-gas1      .
13041        ENDIF.
13042        IF wa_agenda-per = '(IV) OPERATING COST' .
13043          wa_oil1-gas11  = wa_oil1-gas11  + wa_agenda-crude_oil1.
13044          wa_oil1-gas12 = wa_oil1-gas12 + wa_agenda-gas1      .
13045        ENDIF.
13046        IF wa_agenda-per = '(VII) COST OF GOODS SOLD (IV+V+VI)' .
13047          wa_oil1-gas25  = wa_oil1-gas25  + wa_agenda-crude_oil1.
13048          wa_oil1-gas26 = wa_oil1-gas26 + wa_agenda-gas1      .
13049        ENDIF.
13050        IF wa_agenda-per = '(VIII) NET OPERATING MARGIN (I-VII)' .
13051          wa_oil1-gas27  = wa_oil1-gas27  + wa_agenda-crude_oil1.
13052          wa_oil1-gas28 = wa_oil1-gas28 + wa_agenda-gas1      .
13053        ENDIF.
13054
13055  *      CLEAR wa_agenda.
13056      ENDLOOP.
13057
13058  *****************    FOR Q2
13059      LOOP AT it_agenda2 INTO wa_agenda WHERE proj = wa_proj-jvnam.
13060        IF wa_agenda-per =  'REVENUE FROM CRUDE OIL'.
13061          wa_oil1-sh_prd_q1 = wa_agenda-crude_oil2.
13062        ENDIF.
13063
13064        IF wa_agenda-per =  'REVENUE FROM NATURAL GAS'.
13065          wa_oil1-tot_sal_q1 = wa_agenda-gas2.
13066        ENDIF.
13067        IF wa_agenda-per = 'PRODUCTION EXPENSES' OR wa_agenda-per = 'OTHER OPERATING EXPENSE'.
13068          wa_oil1-gas1 = wa_oil1-gas1 + wa_agenda-crude_oil2.
13069          wa_oil1-gas2 = wa_oil1-gas2 + wa_agenda-gas2      .
13070        ENDIF.
13071        IF wa_agenda-per = 'ROYALTY' OR wa_agenda-per = 'OTHER TAXES (AS APPLICABLE)'.
13072          wa_oil1-gas3 = wa_oil1-gas3 + wa_agenda-crude_oil2.
13073          wa_oil1-gas4 = wa_oil1-gas4 + wa_agenda-gas2      .
13074        ENDIF.
13075        IF wa_agenda-per = 'TRANSPORTATION EXPENSES'.
13076          wa_oil1-gas5 = wa_oil1-gas5 + wa_agenda-crude_oil2.
13077          wa_oil1-gas6 = wa_oil1-gas6 + wa_agenda-gas2      .
13078        ENDIF.
13079        IF wa_agenda-per = 'DEPLETION' OR wa_agenda-per = 'DEPRECIATION AND AMORTISATION'.
13080          wa_oil1-gas7 = wa_oil1-gas7 + wa_agenda-crude_oil2.
13081          wa_oil1-gas8 = wa_oil1-gas8 + wa_agenda-gas2      .
13082        ENDIF.
13083        IF wa_agenda-per = 'EXPLORATION COSTS(SURVEY)' OR wa_agenda-per = 'EXPLORATION COSTS(DRY WELL)'.
13084          wa_oil1-gas9  = wa_oil1-gas9  + wa_agenda-crude_oil2.
13085          wa_oil1-gas10 = wa_oil1-gas10 + wa_agenda-gas2      .
13086        ENDIF.
13087        IF wa_agenda-per = '(IV) OPERATING COST' .
13088          wa_oil1-gas11  = wa_oil1-gas11  + wa_agenda-crude_oil2.
13089          wa_oil1-gas12 = wa_oil1-gas12 + wa_agenda-gas2      .
13090        ENDIF.
13091        IF wa_agenda-per = '(VII) COST OF GOODS SOLD (IV+V+VI)' .
13092          wa_oil1-gas25  = wa_oil1-gas25  + wa_agenda-crude_oil2.
13093          wa_oil1-gas26 = wa_oil1-gas26 + wa_agenda-gas2      .
13094        ENDIF.
13095        IF wa_agenda-per = '(VIII) NET OPERATING MARGIN (I-VII)' .
13096          wa_oil1-gas27  = wa_oil1-gas27  + wa_agenda-crude_oil2.
13097          wa_oil1-gas28 = wa_oil1-gas28 + wa_agenda-gas2      .
13098        ENDIF.
13099
13100  *      CLEAR wa_agenda.
13101      ENDLOOP.
13102
13103  ************************FOR Q3
13104

13739      EXPORTING
13740        i_callback_program = sy-cprog
13741        is_layout          = wa_layout
13742        it_fieldcat        = it_fcat
13743        i_default          = 'X'

13746        t_outtab           = it_oil1
13747      EXCEPTIONS
13748        program_error      = 1
13749        OTHERS             = 2.

13755  *&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
13756  *&      Form  CRE_BAR
13757  *&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
13758  *       text
13759  *----------------------------------------------------------------------*
13760  *  -->  p1        text
13761  *  <--  p2        text
13762  *----------------------------------------------------------------------*
13763  FORM cre_bar .
13764    BREAK abapuser02.
13765    BREAK abapuser02.
13766    SELECT txtjv
13767           jvnam
13768    FROM   zfi_pnl_proj
13769    INTO   TABLE it_proj
13770           WHERE prod  = 'YES'.
13771
13772    IF it_proj IS NOT INITIAL.
13773
13774  ***************** FOR FIRST PERIOD <<<<<<<<<<<<<<<<<<<<<<<<<<<<<<<<<<
13775      SELECT *
13776      FROM   zfi_agenda
13777      INTO   TABLE @DATA(it_agenda1)
13778             FOR ALL ENTRIES IN @it_proj
13779      WHERE proj    = @it_proj-jvnam
13780      AND   fiscal1 = @p_year1
13781      AND   period1 = @s_period-low
13782      AND   per_key = 'A3'.
13783
13784      IF it_agenda1 IS INITIAL.
13785
13786        SELECT *
13787        FROM   zfi_agenda
13788        INTO   TABLE @DATA(it_agenda2)
13789               FOR ALL ENTRIES IN @it_proj
13790        WHERE proj    = @it_proj-jvnam
13791        AND   fiscal2 = @p_year1
13792        AND   period2 = @s_period-low
13793        AND   per_key = 'A3'.
13794      ENDIF.
13795
13796      IF it_agenda2 IS INITIAL.
13797
13798        SELECT *
13799        FROM   zfi_agenda
13800        INTO   TABLE @DATA(it_agenda3)
13801               FOR ALL ENTRIES IN @it_proj
13802        WHERE proj    = @it_proj-jvnam
13803        AND   fiscal3 = @p_year1
13804        AND   period3 = @s_period-low
13805        AND   per_key = 'A3'.
13806
13807      ENDIF.
13808      IF it_agenda3 IS INITIAL.
13809
13810        SELECT *
13811        FROM   zfi_agenda
13812        INTO   TABLE @DATA(it_agenda4)
13813               FOR ALL ENTRIES IN @it_proj
13814        WHERE proj    = @it_proj-jvnam
13815        AND   fiscal4 = @p_year1
13816        AND   period4 = @s_period-low
13817        AND   per_key = 'A3'.
13818      ENDIF.
13819
13820  ************************************* FOR SECOND PERIOD<<<<<<<<<<<<<<<<<<<<<<<<<<<<<<<<<<<<<<<<<<<
13821      SELECT *
13822      FROM   zfi_agenda
13823      INTO   TABLE @DATA(it_agenda5)
13824             FOR ALL ENTRIES IN @it_proj
13825      WHERE proj    = @it_proj-jvnam
13826      AND   fiscal1 = @p_year2
13827      AND period1  = @s_period_low2
13828      AND   per_key = 'A3'.
13829
13830      IF it_agenda5 IS INITIAL.
13831        SELECT *
13832          FROM   zfi_agenda
13833          INTO   TABLE @DATA(it_agenda6)
13834                 FOR ALL ENTRIES IN @it_proj
13835          WHERE proj    = @it_proj-jvnam
13836          AND   fiscal2 = @p_year2
13837          AND period2  = @s_period_low2
13838          AND   per_key = 'A3'.
13839
13840      ENDIF.
13841
13842      IF it_agenda6 IS INITIAL.
13843        SELECT *
13844         FROM   zfi_agenda
13845         INTO   TABLE @DATA(it_agenda7)
13846                FOR ALL ENTRIES IN @it_proj
13847         WHERE proj    = @it_proj-jvnam
13848         AND   fiscal3 = @p_year2
13849         AND period3  = @s_period_low2
13850         AND   per_key = 'A3'.
13851
13852      ENDIF.
13853
13854      IF it_agenda7 IS INITIAL.
13855        SELECT *
13856         FROM   zfi_agenda
13857         INTO   TABLE @DATA(it_agenda8)
13858                FOR ALL ENTRIES IN @it_proj
13859         WHERE proj    = @it_proj-jvnam
13860         AND   fiscal4 = @p_year2
13861         AND period4  = @s_period_low2
13862         AND   per_key = 'A3'.
13863
13864      ENDIF.
13865
13866    ENDIF.
13867    REFRESH it_oil1.
13868    LOOP AT it_proj INTO DATA(wa_proj).
13869      wa_oil1-jv       = wa_proj-jvnam.
13870      wa_oil1-jv_name  = wa_proj-txtjv.
13871  *
13872  *    TRY.
13873  *        wa_oil1-sh_prd_q1 = it_agenda[ proj = wa_proj-jvnam per = 'REVENUE FROM CRUDE OIL' ]-crude_oil1.
13874  *      CATCH cx_sy_itab_line_not_found.
13875  *    ENDTRY.
13876  *
13877  *    TRY.
13878  *        wa_oil1-tot_sal_q1 = it_agenda[ proj = wa_proj-jvnam per = 'REVENUE FROM NATURAL GAS' ]-gas1.
13879  *      CATCH cx_sy_itab_line_not_found.
13880  *    ENDTRY.
13881  *
13882  *    TRY.
13883  *        wa_oil1-sh_prd_q2 = it_agenda2[ proj = wa_proj-jvnam per = 'REVENUE FROM CRUDE OIL' ]-crude_oil2.
13884  *
13885  *      CATCH cx_sy_itab_line_not_found.
13886  *    ENDTRY.
13887  *    TRY.
13888  *        wa_oil1-tot_sal_q2 = it_agenda2[ proj = wa_proj-jvnam per = 'REVENUE FROM NATURAL GAS' ]-gas2.
13889  *      CATCH cx_sy_itab_line_not_found.
13890  *    ENDTRY.
13891
13892      LOOP AT it_agenda1 INTO DATA(wa_agenda) WHERE proj = wa_proj-jvnam.
13893        IF wa_agenda-per =  'REVENUE FROM CRUDE OIL'.
13894          wa_oil1-sh_prd_q1 = wa_agenda-crude_oil1.
13895        ENDIF.
13896
13897        IF wa_agenda-per =  'REVENUE FROM NATURAL GAS'.
13898          wa_oil1-tot_sal_q1 = wa_agenda-gas1.
13899        ENDIF.
13900        IF wa_agenda-per = 'PRODUCTION EXPENSES' OR wa_agenda-per = 'OTHER OPERATING EXPENSE'.
13901          wa_oil1-gas1 = wa_oil1-gas1 + wa_agenda-crude_oil1.
13902          wa_oil1-gas2 = wa_oil1-gas2 + wa_agenda-gas1      .
13903        ENDIF.
13904        IF wa_agenda-per = 'ROYALTY' OR wa_agenda-per = 'OTHER TAXES (AS APPLICABLE)'.
13905          wa_oil1-gas3 = wa_oil1-gas3 + wa_agenda-crude_oil1.
13906          wa_oil1-gas4 = wa_oil1-gas4 + wa_agenda-gas1      .
13907        ENDIF.
13908        IF wa_agenda-per = 'TRANSPORTATION EXPENSES'.
13909          wa_oil1-gas5 = wa_oil1-gas5 + wa_agenda-crude_oil1.
13910          wa_oil1-gas6 = wa_oil1-gas6 + wa_agenda-gas1      .
13911        ENDIF.
13912        IF wa_agenda-per = 'DEPLETION' OR wa_agenda-per = 'DEPRECIATION AND AMORTISATION'.
13913          wa_oil1-gas7 = wa_oil1-gas7 + wa_agenda-crude_oil1.
13914          wa_oil1-gas8 = wa_oil1-gas8 + wa_agenda-gas1      .
13915        ENDIF.
13916        IF wa_agenda-per = 'EXPLORATION COSTS(SURVEY)' OR wa_agenda-per = 'EXPLORATION COSTS(DRY WELL)'.
13917          wa_oil1-gas9  = wa_oil1-gas9  + wa_agenda-crude_oil1.
13918          wa_oil1-gas10 = wa_oil1-gas10 + wa_agenda-gas1      .
13919        ENDIF.
13920        IF wa_agenda-per = '(IV) OPERATING COST' .
13921          wa_oil1-gas11  = wa_oil1-gas11  + wa_agenda-crude_oil1.
13922          wa_oil1-gas12 = wa_oil1-gas12 + wa_agenda-gas1      .
13923        ENDIF.
13924        IF wa_agenda-per = '(VII) COST OF GOODS SOLD (IV+V+VI)' .
13925          wa_oil1-gas25  = wa_oil1-gas25  + wa_agenda-crude_oil1.
13926          wa_oil1-gas26 = wa_oil1-gas26 + wa_agenda-gas1      .
13927        ENDIF.
13928        IF wa_agenda-per = '(VIII) NET OPERATING MARGIN (I-VII)' .
13929          wa_oil1-gas27  = wa_oil1-gas27  + wa_agenda-crude_oil1.
13930          wa_oil1-gas28 = wa_oil1-gas28 + wa_agenda-gas1      .
13931        ENDIF.
13932
13933  *      CLEAR wa_agenda.
13934      ENDLOOP.
13935
13936  *****************    FOR Q2
13937      LOOP AT it_agenda2 INTO wa_agenda WHERE proj = wa_proj-jvnam.
13938        IF wa_agenda-per =  'REVENUE FROM CRUDE OIL'.
13939          wa_oil1-sh_prd_q1 = wa_agenda-crude_oil2.
13940        ENDIF.
13941
13942        IF wa_agenda-per =  'REVENUE FROM NATURAL GAS'.
13943          wa_oil1-tot_sal_q1 = wa_agenda-gas2.
13944        ENDIF.
13945        IF wa_agenda-per = 'PRODUCTION EXPENSES' OR wa_agenda-per = 'OTHER OPERATING EXPENSE'.
13946          wa_oil1-gas1 = wa_oil1-gas1 + wa_agenda-crude_oil2.
13947          wa_oil1-gas2 = wa_oil1-gas2 + wa_agenda-gas2      .
13948        ENDIF.
13949        IF wa_agenda-per = 'ROYALTY' OR wa_agenda-per = 'OTHER TAXES (AS APPLICABLE)'.
13950          wa_oil1-gas3 = wa_oil1-gas3 + wa_agenda-crude_oil2.
13951          wa_oil1-gas4 = wa_oil1-gas4 + wa_agenda-gas2      .
13952        ENDIF.
13953        IF wa_agenda-per = 'TRANSPORTATION EXPENSES'.
13954          wa_oil1-gas5 = wa_oil1-gas5 + wa_agenda-crude_oil2.
13955          wa_oil1-gas6 = wa_oil1-gas6 + wa_agenda-gas2      .
13956        ENDIF.
13957        IF wa_agenda-per = 'DEPLETION' OR wa_agenda-per = 'DEPRECIATION AND AMORTISATION'.
13958          wa_oil1-gas7 = wa_oil1-gas7 + wa_agenda-crude_oil2.
13959          wa_oil1-gas8 = wa_oil1-gas8 + wa_agenda-gas2      .
13960        ENDIF.
13961        IF wa_agenda-per = 'EXPLORATION COSTS(SURVEY)' OR wa_agenda-per = 'EXPLORATION COSTS(DRY WELL)'.
13962          wa_oil1-gas9  = wa_oil1-gas9  + wa_agenda-crude_oil2.
13963          wa_oil1-gas10 = wa_oil1-gas10 + wa_agenda-gas2      .
13964        ENDIF.
13965        IF wa_agenda-per = '(IV) OPERATING COST' .
13966          wa_oil1-gas11  = wa_oil1-gas11  + wa_agenda-crude_oil2.
13967          wa_oil1-gas12 = wa_oil1-gas12 + wa_agenda-gas2      .
13968        ENDIF.
13969        IF wa_agenda-per = '(VII) COST OF GOODS SOLD (IV+V+VI)' .
13970          wa_oil1-gas25  = wa_oil1-gas25  + wa_agenda-crude_oil2.
13971          wa_oil1-gas26 = wa_oil1-gas26 + wa_agenda-gas2      .
13972        ENDIF.
13973        IF wa_agenda-per = '(VIII) NET OPERATING MARGIN (I-VII)' .
13974          wa_oil1-gas27  = wa_oil1-gas27  + wa_agenda-crude_oil2.

14334  *        wa_oil1-gas10 = wa_oil1-gas10 + wa_agenda-gas1      .
14335  *      ENDIF.
14336  *      IF wa_agenda-per = '(IV) OPERATING COST' .
14337  *        wa_oil1-gas11  = wa_oil1-gas9  + wa_agenda-crude_oil1.
14338  *        wa_oil1-gas12 = wa_oil1-gas10 + wa_agenda-gas1      .
14339  *      ENDIF.
14340  *
14341  *      IF wa_agenda-per = '(VII) COST OF GOODS SOLD (IV+V+VI)' .
14342  *        wa_oil1-gas25  = wa_oil1-gas25  + wa_agenda-crude_oil1.
14343  *        wa_oil1-gas26 = wa_oil1-gas26 + wa_agenda-gas1      .
14344  *      ENDIF.
14345  *      IF wa_agenda-per = '(XII) NET PROFIT/(LOSS) AFTER TAX (X-XI)' .
14346  *        wa_oil1-gas27  = wa_oil1-gas27  + wa_agenda-crude_oil1.
14347  *        wa_oil1-gas28 = wa_oil1-gas28 + wa_agenda-gas1      .
14348  *      ENDIF.
14349  *    ENDLOOP.
14350  *
14351  *    CLEAR: wa_agenda.
14352  *    LOOP AT it_agenda2 INTO wa_agenda WHERE  proj = wa_proj-jvnam..
14353  *      IF wa_agenda-per = 'PRODUCTION EXPENSES' OR wa_agenda-per = 'OTHER OPERATING EXPENSE'.
14354  *        wa_oil1-gas13  = wa_oil1-gas13 + wa_agenda-crude_oil1.
14355  *        wa_oil1-gas14  = wa_oil1-gas14 + wa_agenda-gas1      .
14356  *      ENDIF.
14357  *      IF wa_agenda-per = 'ROYALTY' OR wa_agenda-per = 'OTHER TAXES (AS APPLICABLE)'.
14358  *        wa_oil1-gas15  = wa_oil1-gas15 + wa_agenda-crude_oil1.
14359  *        wa_oil1-gas16  = wa_oil1-gas16 + wa_agenda-gas1      .
14360  *      ENDIF.
14361  *      IF wa_agenda-per = 'TRANSPORTATION EXPENSES'.
14362  *        wa_oil1-gas17  = wa_oil1-gas17 + wa_agenda-crude_oil1.
14363  *        wa_oil1-gas18  = wa_oil1-gas18 + wa_agenda-gas1      .
14364  *      ENDIF.
14365  *      IF wa_agenda-per = 'DEPLETION' OR wa_agenda-per = 'DEPRECIATION AND AMORTISATION'.
14366  *        wa_oil1-gas19  = wa_oil1-gas19 + wa_agenda-crude_oil1.
14367  *        wa_oil1-gas20  = wa_oil1-gas20 + wa_agenda-gas1      .
14368  *      ENDIF.
14369  *      IF wa_agenda-per = 'EXPLORATION COSTS(SURVEY)' OR wa_agenda-per = 'EXPLORATION COSTS(DRY WELL)'.
14370  *        wa_oil1-gas21  = wa_oil1-gas21  + wa_agenda-crude_oil1.
14371  *        wa_oil1-gas22  = wa_oil1-gas22  + wa_agenda-gas1      .
14372  *      ENDIF.
14373  *      IF wa_agenda-per = '(IV) OPERATING COST' .
14374  *        wa_oil1-gas23  = wa_oil1-gas23  + wa_agenda-crude_oil1.
14375  *        wa_oil1-gas24  = wa_oil1-gas24  + wa_agenda-gas1      .
14376  *      ENDIF.
14377  *
14378  *      IF wa_agenda-per = '(VII) COST OF GOODS SOLD (IV+V+VI)' .
14379  *        wa_oil1-gas29  = wa_oil1-gas25  + wa_agenda-crude_oil1.
14380  *        wa_oil1-gas30 = wa_oil1-gas26 + wa_agenda-gas1      .
14381  *      ENDIF.
14382  *      IF wa_agenda-per = '(XII) NET PROFIT/(LOSS) AFTER TAX (X-XI)' .
14383  *        wa_oil1-gas31  = wa_oil1-gas27  + wa_agenda-crude_oil1.
14384  *        wa_oil1-gas32 = wa_oil1-gas28 + wa_agenda-gas1      .
14385  *      ENDIF.
14386  *    ENDLOOP.
14387  *
14388  *    APPEND wa_oil1 TO it_oil1.
14389  *    CLEAR: wa_oil1, wa_proj, wa_agenda.
14390  *  ENDLOOP.
14391  ENDFORM.

15294  FORM approvefi .
15295
15296    SELECT SINGLE * FROM zfi_pl_status_n INTO @DATA(wa_status1) WHERE period = @s_period-low
15297                                                 AND period2  = @s_period_low2
15298                                                 AND fiscal   = @p_year1
15299                                                 AND fiscal2   = @p_year2
15300                                                 AND proj     = @p_proj1-low
